    assert row["corrected_by"] == "ops" and row["republished"] is True
    assert row["downstream"]["revision"] == 2 and row["downstream"]["review_state"] == "reviewed"


def test_a_stale_page_is_told_to_refresh_instead_of_overwriting(client):
    http, db = client
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    http.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{result.id}/level",
             json={"level": "L1", "reason": "先手", "idempotency_key": "k1", "expected_level": "L3"})
    late = http.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{result.id}/level",
                    json={"level": "L4", "reason": "后手", "idempotency_key": "k2", "expected_level": "L3"})
    assert late.status_code == 409
    assert late.json()["detail"]["code"] == "incremental_correction_level_conflict"


def test_a_retried_submission_returns_the_same_answer(client):
    http, db = client
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    body = {"level": "L1", "reason": "重试", "idempotency_key": "same", "expected_level": "L3"}
    first = http.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{result.id}/level", json=body)
    second = http.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{result.id}/level", json=body)
    assert first.status_code == second.status_code == 200
    assert second.json()["level"] == "L1"
    assert second.json()["downstream"]["revision"] == 2, "重试不能再发一版"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("payload,status", [
    ({"level": "L1", "reason": "", "idempotency_key": "k"}, 422),
    ({"level": "L1", "reason": "   ", "idempotency_key": "k"}, 422),
    ({"level": "L1", "reason": "x", "idempotency_key": ""}, 422),
    ({"reason": "x", "idempotency_key": "k"}, 422),
    ({"level": "L5", "reason": "x", "idempotency_key": "k"}, 422),
])
def test_an_incomplete_correction_is_refused(client, payload, status):
    http, db = client
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    assert http.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{result.id}/level", json=payload).status_code == status


def test_a_result_that_is_not_incremental_is_refused(client):
    http, db = client
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3", run_mode="stock_regression")
    response = http.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{result.id}/level",
                        json={"level": "L1", "reason": "x", "idempotency_key": "k"})
    assert response.status_code == 409
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "incremental_correction_not_incremental"


def test_a_missing_result_is_a_404_not_a_crash(client):
    http, _ = client
    response = http.put("/api/incremental-corrections/999999/level",
                        json={"level": "L1", "reason": "x", "idempotency_key": "k"})
    assert response.status_code == 404


def test_the_new_filters_and_the_id_search_are_reachable_over_http(client):
    http, db = client
    result, run = _material(db, 1)
    _material(db, 2)
    run.upstream_class_id = 2124
    run.upstream_task_id = 2588
    db.commit()
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-corrections", params={"upstream_class_id": 2124}).json()["total"] == 1
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-corrections", params={"search": "2588"}).json()["items"][0]["evaluation_id"] == result.id
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-corrections", params={"corrected_by": "nobody"}).json()["total"] == 0
    bad = http.get("/api/incremental-corrections", params={"published_from": "2026/09/17"})
    assert bad.status_code == 422 and bad.json()["detail"]["code"] == "incremental_correction_date_invalid"
    options = http.get("/api/incremental-corrections/filters").json()
    assert options["upstream_classes"] == [{"class_id": 2124, "label": "SU · 家具模型", "total": 1, "corrected": 0}]
    assert options["correctors"] == []


def test_a_page_can_be_corrected_in_one_batch_over_http(client):
    http, db = client
    first, _ = _material(db, 1)
    second, _ = _material(db, 2)
    response = http.put("/api/incremental-corrections/batch/level", json={
        "items": [{"evaluation_id": first.id, "expected_level": "L3"},
                  {"evaluation_id": second.id, "expected_level": "L1"}],
        "level": "L2", "reason": "先全部回捞到 L2", "idempotency_key": "batch-http-1",
    })
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert [row["evaluation_id"] for row in body["corrected"]] == [first.id]
    assert body["skipped"] == [{"evaluation_id": second.id, "code": "incremental_correction_level_conflict",
                               "message": body["skipped"][0]["message"]}]
    assert http.put("/api/incremental-corrections/batch/level", json={
        "items": [], "level": "L2", "reason": "x", "idempotency_key": "k"}).status_code == 422
    blank = http.put("/api/incremental-corrections/batch/level", json={
        "items": [{"evaluation_id": first.id}], "level": "L2", "reason": "   ", "idempotency_key": "k"})
    assert blank.status_code == 422, "批量也必须写理由"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_correction_continuity.py =====
"""Correction publication integration checks using only local fixture adapters."""

import json

from sqlalchemy import select

from app.connector_delivery import FixtureDownstreamAdapter, deliver_outbox_batch
from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, current_policy, set_admission
from app.models import ConnectorContractVersion, ProductionRun, RuntimeOutboxEvent, WorkflowVersion
from app.runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
from app.workflow_correction import _republish_incremental_projection
from app.workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _close, _fixture, _headers, _payload


def test_correction_republish_preserves_route_and_other_entity_delivers(monkeypatch):
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
        policy = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="split", ratio_percent=100,
            ownership_contract="local-correction-continuity", reason="测试", actor="test")
        set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=policy["revision"], paused=False, reason="开始", actor="test")
        db.commit()

        def admit(identity):
            payload = _payload(event_id=f"continuity-{identity}", asset_id=asset.id)
            payload["payload"]["content_id"] = identity
            response = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload)
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == response.json()["production_run_key"]))
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            node = next(n for n in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] if n["kind"] == "downstream_projection")
            return run, snapshot, node

        corrected_run, snapshot, node = admit("corrected")
        target = db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction == "downstream"))
        adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint)
        first = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
            db, corrected_run, step_key=node["node_instance_id"], output_manifest={"score": 40}))
        delivered = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="continuity", adapter=adapter)
        assert delivered.delivered_event_ids == (first.id,)
        db.commit()

        corrected_ids = _republish_incremental_projection(db, run=corrected_run, snapshot=snapshot,
            nodes={node["node_instance_id"]: node},
            outputs={node["node_instance_id"]: {"status": "succeeded", "output": {"score": 90}}},
            actor="operator", reason="人工纠偏", correction_event_id="correction-continuity")
        assert len(corrected_ids) == 1
        reviewed = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, corrected_ids[0])
        payload = load_snapshot_blob(reviewed.payload_blob)
        assert payload["incremental_route"] == snapshot["runtime_context"]["incremental_route"]
        assert payload["incremental_route"]["destination"] == "formal"
        assert payload["review_state"] == "reviewed"
        # 重发的是整条节点结果，和自动路径同层——投递器就是从里面取 output 的。
        assert payload["result"] == {"status": "succeeded", "output": {"score": 90}}
        assert reviewed.revision == first.revision + 1
        db.commit()

        other_run, _, other_node = admit("unrelated")
        automatic = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
            db, other_run, step_key=other_node["node_instance_id"], output_manifest={"score": 70}))
        assert automatic.business_idempotency_key != reviewed.business_idempotency_key
        assert automatic.review_state == "auto"
        delivered = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="continuity", adapter=adapter)
        assert set(delivered.delivered_event_ids) == {reviewed.id, automatic.id}
        assert delivered.blocked_event_ids == ()
        assert current_policy(db, **scope).admission_paused is False
        assert {row.key.business_idempotency_key: row.key.review_state for row in adapter.rows} == {
            reviewed.business_idempotency_key: "reviewed", automatic.business_idempotency_key: "auto"}
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_correction_queue.py =====
"""增量纠偏：100% 进队、不阻断、人工改了才覆盖下游。

「今日人审」每天只抽 100 条，没抽到的记成「未抽查」，严重风险超容量还会暂停链路。
这里要的是相反的性质：每条增量素材都在列表里，运营挑有问题的改，没改的保持 AI 结果，
任何时候都不拦住自动链路。所以这些测试盯的是三件事——

1. 队列是全量的，筛选缩小的是同一组条件下的 total，不是页;
2. 改等级会记一条可审计的人工事实，并按 revision+1/reviewed 重发下游覆盖同一行;
3. 多人同时处理时，后手不能悄悄把前手刚改完的结果盖回去。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.runner import _migration_118_production_run_mode
from app.workflow_result_contract import hash_manifest
from app.incremental_correction import (
    batch_correct_incremental_level,
    IncrementalCorrectionError,
    correct_incremental_field,
    correct_incremental_level,
    correction_dashboard,
    correction_filter_options,
    incremental_correction_detail,
    list_incremental_corrections,
    selectable_levels,
)
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    ContentIngressEvent,
    ContentRecord,
    DownstreamPublishLedger,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    ProductionRun,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

CONTRACT_KEY = "factory.prod.downstream"
LEVELS = [
    {"level": "L1", "min_score": 80, "enabled": True},
    {"level": "L2", "min_score": 61, "enabled": True},
    {"level": "L3", "min_score": 41, "enabled": True},
    {"level": "L4", "min_score": 0, "enabled": True},
    {"level": "L5", "enabled": False},
]


def _manifest(category_key: str) -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "nodes": [
            {"node_instance_id": "prompt_a", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"asset_key": "a"}},
            {
                "node_instance_id": "score_level_matcher_7",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {
                    "module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
                    "component_snapshot": {"content_json": json.dumps({"levels": LEVELS})},
                },
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "downstream",
                "kind": "downstream_projection",
                "config": {
                    "connector_key": CONTRACT_KEY,
                    "connector_snapshot": {
                        "connector_contract_id": 1,
                        "contract_key": CONTRACT_KEY,
                        "direction": "downstream",
                    },
                    "mapping": {"schema_version": "factory-result-v1"},
                },
            },
        ],
        "edges": [
            {"from": "prompt_a", "to": "score_level_matcher_7"},
            {"from": "score_level_matcher_7", "to": "downstream"},
        ],
    }


def _snapshot(category_key: str, *, run_mode: str, frozen: dict) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        {
            "runtime_context": {
                "run_mode": run_mode,
                "model_execution_mode": "real",
                "input_manifest": frozen,
            },
            "workflow": {"manifest": _manifest(category_key)},
        }
    )


def _ingress(db, index: int, *, category: str, asset_id: int) -> dict:
    """真实的接入事实。下游写入要靠它重建源版本证明，冻结快照里的数字对不上就不许写。"""

    content_id = f"c{index}"
    record = ContentRecord(
        source_system="factory", source_content_id=content_id, category_key=category,
        source_version="v1", source_occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        content_key=f"factory:{content_id}", status="ready", identity_status="verified")
    db.add(record)
    db.flush()
    event = ContentIngressEvent(
        event_id=f"evt-{index}", schema_version="content-ingress-v1", event_type="content.created",
        source_system="factory", occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), payload_hash=f"{index:064d}",
        payload_json=json.dumps({"content_id": content_id, "content_version": "v1"}),
        identity_snapshot_json=json.dumps({"content_key": f"factory:{content_id}"}),
        content_record_id=record.id, status="applied")
    db.add(event)
    db.flush()
    return {
        "asset_id": asset_id,
        "source_version": "v1",
        "content_record_id": record.id,
        "content_ingress_event_db_id": event.id,
        "source_system": "factory",
        "source_content_id": content_id,
        "content_key": f"factory:{content_id}",
    }, event.id


def _node_outputs(level: str) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        {
            "prompt_a": {"status": "succeeded", "output": {"classification": {}}},
            "score_level_matcher_7": {"status": "succeeded", "output": {"level": "", "score": 0.0}},
            "downstream": {
                "status": "succeeded",
                "output": {
                    "schema_version": "factory-result-v1",
                    "final_level": level,
                    "final_score": 78,
                    "normalized_result": {"level": level, "score": 78, "steps": []},
                },
            },
        }
    )


def _seed_reference_data(session):
    """工作流版本和下游合同——两张表的内容都会被冻结进运行快照，必须真的存在。"""

    definition = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key="wf", name="wf", owner="t", created_by="t")
    session.add(definition)
    session.flush()
    for index in (1, 2):
        session.add(WorkflowVersion(
            id=index, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, version=f"v{index}", status="active",
            step_manifest_json="[]", edge_manifest_json="[]", input_schema_json="{}",
            output_schema_json="{}", canonical_hash=f"{index:064d}", created_by="t"))
    session.add(ConnectorContractVersion(
        id=1, contract_key=CONTRACT_KEY, direction="downstream",
        category_key="model_3d_su", version=1, status="active", logical_locator="logical.x",
        adapter_key="factory-submit-v1", schema_fingerprint="f" * 64,
        contract_hash="c" * 64,
        field_mapping_json="{}", cursor_contract_json="{}", idempotency_contract_json="{}",
        batch_size=1, timeout_seconds=30, owner="t", created_by="t"))
    session.flush()


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    _seed_reference_data(session)
    session.commit()
    yield session
    session.close()
    engine.dispose()


def _material(db, index: int, *, level: str | None = "L3", category: str = "model_3d_su",
              version_id: int = 1, run_mode: str = "incremental", review_revision: int = 0,
              published: bool = True):
    asset = Asset(original_name=f"{index}.jpg", stored_name=f"{index}.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",
                  size_bytes=10, width=8, height=8, sha256=f"{index:064d}", category_key=category)
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, category_key=category, status="completed",
                        stage="done", progress=100)
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()
    frozen, event_id = _ingress(db, index, category=category, asset_id=asset.id)
    snapshot = _snapshot(category, run_mode=run_mode, frozen=frozen)
    # 真算哈希，不塞假值：纠偏重放第一件事就是校验快照没被动过，
    # 假哈希会让整条详情纠偏路径永远走不到被测的逻辑。
    snapshot_hash = hash_manifest(json.loads(snapshot))
    run = ProductionRun(
        run_key=f"run-{index:04d}", idempotency_key=f"idem-{index}", source_type="content_ingress_event",
        source_id=event_id, workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=version_id,
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
        snapshot_json=snapshot,
        category_key=category, queue_class="production_batch", status="succeeded", blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by="t", owner="t", reason="", environment="dry_run", total_steps=3,
        completed_steps=3, failed_steps=0,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=index),
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id, job_id=job.id, workflow_version_id=version_id, production_run_id=run.id,
        workflow_snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash, workflow_node_outputs_json=_node_outputs(level or "L3"),
        normalized_result_json=json.dumps({"level": level, "score": 78}),
        precheck_json="{}", scoring_json=json.dumps({"level": level, "score": 78, "v3_context": None}),
        raw_response_a="{}", level=level, score=78, confidence=0.9, needs_review=False,
        review_revision=review_revision, model_id="m", prompt_a_version="a", rubric_version="r",
        engine_version="e", created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=index),
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.flush()
    if published and run_mode == "incremental":
        # 走真正的首发路径，而不是手造一行台账：纠偏要发的是「下一版」，
        # 版本号从已有事件推出来，假数据会让 revision 永远停在 1。
        _publish_auto(db, run, result)
    db.commit()
    return result, run


def _publish_auto(db, run, result):
    from app.downstream_publish_ledger import record_downstream_publish
    from app.runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox
    from app.workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request

    # 自动路径发的是整条节点结果（投递器再从里面取 output），不是里面的 output。
    node_result = json.loads(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)["downstream"]
    request = build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
        db, run, step_key="downstream", output_manifest=node_result, review_state="auto",
        change={"trigger_kind": "auto_run", "actor": "system", "reason": ""},
    )
    assert request is not None
    event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, request)
    ledger = record_downstream_publish(db, event=event, payload=request.payload)
    ledger.delivery_status = "delivered"
    db.flush()


def _reviewed_events(db):
    return db.scalars(
        select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.review_state == "reviewed")
    ).all()


def test_the_queue_holds_every_incremental_material_not_a_daily_sample(db):
    for index in range(1, 13):
        _material(db, index)
    page = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=5)
    assert page["total"] == 12, "队列是 100% 的，不是抽样容量"
    assert len(page["items"]) == 5 and page["has_more"] is True
    assert page["items"][0]["correction_state"] == "uncorrected"


def test_a_stock_regression_run_never_enters_the_incremental_queue(db):
    _material(db, 1)
    _material(db, 2, run_mode="baseline")
    page = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)
    assert page["total"] == 1, "存量回归不写下游，不属于增量纠偏"


def test_rows_from_before_migration_118_still_fall_back_to_the_snapshot(db):
    # 迁移 118 之前建的行 run_mode 列是 NULL；队列必须照旧从冻结快照里判断，
    # 否则回填前的一瞬间，整页历史增量会从纠偏队列里消失。
    _, run = _material(db, 1)
    assert run.run_mode is None
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)["total"] == 1


def test_once_the_column_is_filled_it_decides_without_parsing_the_snapshot(db):
    _, run = _material(db, 1)  # 快照说 incremental
    run.run_mode = "baseline"
    db.commit()
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)["total"] == 0, "列已填时以列为准，不再解析快照"
    run.run_mode = "incremental"
    db.commit()
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)["total"] == 1


def test_migration_118_backfills_the_column_from_the_snapshot_and_can_rerun(db):
    _, incremental = _material(db, 1)
    _, stock = _material(db, 2, run_mode="baseline")
    assert incremental.run_mode is None and stock.run_mode is None
    _migration_118_production_run_mode(db.connection())
    db.commit()
    db.expire_all()
    assert incremental.run_mode == "incremental" and stock.run_mode == "baseline"
    _migration_118_production_run_mode(db.connection())  # 列已存在、行已回填：再跑一次是空操作
    db.commit()
    page = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)
    assert page["total"] == 1 and page["items"][0]["production_run_id"] == incremental.id


def test_every_row_carries_what_the_list_has_to_show(db):
    result, run = _material(db, 1)
    row = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=1)["items"][0]
    assert row["evaluation_id"] == result.id and row["production_run_id"] == run.id
    assert row["asset_id"] == result.asset_id and row["asset_name"] == "1.jpg"
    assert row["level"] == "L3" and row["category_key"] == "model_3d_su"
    # 列表要能直接改等级，所以每行都得说清楚这条能改成哪些。
    assert row["selectable_levels"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"], "停用的 L5 不能出现"
    # 先发后审：AI 那一版已经在下游了。
    published_at = row["downstream"].pop("published_at")
    assert row["downstream"] == {
        "published": True, "revision": 1, "review_state": "auto", "delivery_status": "delivered"}
    # 「发布时间」是第一次发到下游的时间，按东八区给运营看，用来按天筛。
    assert published_at == row["published_at"] and published_at.endswith("+08:00")
    assert row["upstream"] == {"res_type": None, "class_id": None, "class_label": "", "task_id": None, "img_id": None, "ll_id": None}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("filters,expected", [
    ({"state": "uncorrected"}, 2),
    ({"state": "corrected"}, 1),
    ({"category_key": "model_3d_su"}, 2),
    ({"category_key": "inspiration_image"}, 1),
    ({"workflow_version_id": 2}, 1),
    ({"level": "L1"}, 1),
    ({"category_key": "model_3d_su", "state": "corrected"}, 1),
])
def test_filters_narrow_the_total_so_several_operators_can_split_the_work(db, filters, expected):
    _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    _material(db, 2, level="L1", review_revision=1)
    _material(db, 3, level="L2", category="inspiration_image", version_id=2)
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50, **filters)["total"] == expected


def _tag_upstream(db, run, *, class_id=2124, task_id=2588, img_id=46272219, ll_id=34015031):
    run.upstream_class_id = class_id
    run.upstream_task_id = task_id
    run.upstream_img_id = img_id
    run.upstream_ll_id = ll_id
    db.commit()


def test_the_queue_can_be_narrowed_to_one_upstream_class(db, monkeypatch):
    _, panorama = _material(db, 1)
    _, model = _material(db, 2)
    _tag_upstream(db, panorama, class_id=2124)
    _tag_upstream(db, model, class_id=4138, task_id=2590, img_id=46272228, ll_id=34011106)
    monkeypatch.setenv("INCREMENTAL_UPSTREAM_CLASS_LABELS_JSON", json.dumps({"2124": "全景图"}))
    page = list_incremental_corrections(db, upstream_class_id=2124, limit=50)
    assert page["total"] == 1 and page["items"][0]["production_run_id"] == panorama.id
    assert page["items"][0]["upstream"] == {
        "res_type": None, "class_id": 2124, "class_label": "SU · 家具模型", "task_id": 2588, "img_id": 46272219, "ll_id": 34015031}
    options = correction_filter_options(db)["upstream_classes"]
    assert options == [
        {"class_id": 2124, "label": "SU · 家具模型", "total": 1, "corrected": 0},
        {"class_id": 4138, "label": "3D · 展会设计", "total": 1, "corrected": 0},
    ], "上游正式映射优先于历史环境变量中的错误配置"


def test_an_id_search_finds_the_material_by_any_of_its_ids(db):
    result, run = _material(db, 1)
    _material(db, 2)
    _tag_upstream(db, run)
    for term in (str(result.id), str(run.id), str(result.asset_id), "2588", "46272219", "34015031", run.run_key):
        page = list_incremental_corrections(db, search=term, limit=50)
        assert page["total"] == 1 and page["items"][0]["evaluation_id"] == result.id, term
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, search="999999", limit=50)["total"] == 0
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, search="   ", limit=50)["total"] == 2, "空白搜索等于不筛"


def test_the_queue_can_be_narrowed_by_publish_day_in_the_platform_zone(db):
    _, early = _material(db, 1)
    _, late = _material(db, 2)
    # 素材 1 于北京时间 9-16 23:30 发布（UTC 15:30），素材 2 于北京时间 9-17 00:10 发布（UTC 9-16 16:10）。
    for run, stamp in ((early, datetime(2026, 9, 16, 15, 30, tzinfo=timezone.utc)),
                       (late, datetime(2026, 9, 16, 16, 10, tzinfo=timezone.utc))):
        ledger = db.scalar(select(DownstreamPublishLedger).where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key == run.run_key))
        ledger.created_at = stamp
    db.commit()
    # 两端都是当天 00:00（东八区，老大 2026-09-20 定的口径）：选 9/16~9/17 就是
    # 9/16 00:00 到 9/17 00:00，9/17 00:10 发的那条落在区间外。
    assert [r["production_run_id"] for r in list_incremental_corrections(db, published_from="2026-09-17", limit=50)["items"]] == [late.id]
    assert [r["production_run_id"] for r in list_incremental_corrections(db, published_to="2026-09-17", limit=50)["items"]] == [early.id]
    assert [r["production_run_id"] for r in list_incremental_corrections(
        db, published_from="2026-09-16", published_to="2026-09-17", limit=50)["items"]] == [early.id]
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, published_from="2026-09-16", published_to="2026-09-18", limit=50)["total"] == 2
    # 结束日期就是上界，所以它当天 00:00 之后的都不算：选同一天等于空区间。
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, published_from="2026-09-16", published_to="2026-09-16", limit=50)["total"] == 0
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
        list_incremental_corrections(db, published_from="16/09/2026")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_date_invalid" and info.value.status_code == 422
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError):
        list_incremental_corrections(db, published_from="2026-09-17", published_to="2026-09-16")


def test_the_correction_moment_reaches_the_operator_in_the_platform_zone(db):
    """同一行里 created_at/published_at 都是东八区，纠偏时间不能独自留在 UTC。

    2026-09-20 老大指出：页面上的纠偏时间比实际早 8 小时。留痕是历年多个写入方留下的，
    有 UTC 也有东八区，所以换算放在出口做，存储不动。
    """
    from app.incremental_correction import platform_moment

    result, _ = _material(db, 1)
    result.correction_history_json = json.dumps([{
        "corrector": "ops", "reason": "存量留痕写的是 UTC",
        "corrected_at": "2026-09-20T09:15:18.601125+00:00",
    }])
    db.commit()
    row = next(r for r in list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)["items"]
               if r["evaluation_id"] == result.id)
    assert row["corrected_at"] == "2026-09-20T17:15:18.601125+08:00"
    # 已经是东八区的留痕原样保持，不会再被挪一次。
    assert platform_moment("2026-09-20T17:15:18.601125+08:00") == "2026-09-20T17:15:18.601125+08:00"
    # 没有时区的历史值按 UTC 解释（写入方都在跑 UTC 的服务器上）。
    assert platform_moment("2026-09-20T09:15:18").startswith("2026-09-20T17:15:18")
    # 认不出来的原样返回，不编时间。
    assert platform_moment("上次那回") == "上次那回" and platform_moment(None) == ""


def test_the_queue_can_be_narrowed_to_one_operator(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1)
    _material(db, 2)
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L2", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="太宽松", actor="xiaotie", idempotency_key="op-1")
    db.commit()
    page = list_incremental_corrections(db, corrected_by="xiaotie", limit=50)
    assert page["total"] == 1 and page["items"][0]["evaluation_id"] == result.id
    assert list_incremental_corrections(db, corrected_by="nobody", limit=50)["total"] == 0
    assert correction_filter_options(db)["correctors"] == [{"corrector": "xiaotie", "total": 1}]


def test_a_batch_changes_every_selected_material_and_reports_the_ones_it_could_not(db):
    first, _ = _material(db, 1)
    second, _ = _material(db, 2)
    already, _ = _material(db, 3, level="L2")
    stale, _ = _material(db, 4)
    unpublished, _ = _material(db, 5, published=False)
    outputs = json.loads(unpublished.workflow_node_outputs_json)
    outputs["downstream"]["status"] = "failed"   # 从没发到过下游，没有可覆盖的那一版
    unpublished.workflow_node_outputs_json = json.dumps(outputs)
    db.commit()
    outcome = batch_correct_incremental_level(
        db,
        items=[
            {"evaluation_id": first.id, "expected_level": "L3"},
            {"evaluation_id": second.id, "expected_level": "L3"},
            {"evaluation_id": already.id, "expected_level": "L2"},
            {"evaluation_id": stale.id, "expected_level": "L1"},   # 勾选后被别人改过
            {"evaluation_id": unpublished.id, "expected_level": "L3"},
            {"evaluation_id": first.id, "expected_level": "L3"},   # 重复勾选只算一次
        ],
        level="L2", reason="L1/L3 抓得太松，先全部回捞到 L2", actor="ops", idempotency_key="batch-1",
    )
    assert outcome["requested"] == 5
    assert [row["evaluation_id"] for row in outcome["corrected"]] == [first.id, second.id]
    assert all(row["level"] == "L2" and row["downstream"]["revision"] == 2 for row in outcome["corrected"])
    assert outcome["unchanged"] == [already.id]
    assert {item["evaluation_id"]: item["code"] for item in outcome["skipped"]} == {
        stale.id: "incremental_correction_level_conflict",
        unpublished.id: "incremental_correction_no_published_output",
    }
    # 成功的已各自提交；失败的没有留下半条记录。
    db.expire_all()
    assert db.get(EvaluationResult, first.id).level == "L2"
    assert db.get(EvaluationResult, stale.id).level == "L3"
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
        batch_correct_incremental_level(db, items=[], level="L2", reason="x", actor="ops", idempotency_key="b")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_batch_size_invalid"


def test_an_unknown_correction_state_is_refused_rather_than_silently_ignored(db):
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
        list_incremental_corrections(db, state="maybe")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_state_invalid"


def test_the_filter_options_carry_their_own_counts(db):
    _material(db, 1)
    _material(db, 2, review_revision=1)
    _material(db, 3, category="inspiration_image", version_id=2)
    options = correction_filter_options(db)
    categories = {item["category_key"]: item for item in options["categories"]}
    assert categories["model_3d_su"] == {"category_key": "model_3d_su", "total": 2, "corrected": 1}
    assert categories["inspiration_image"]["total"] == 1
    assert {item["workflow_version_id"] for item in options["workflows"]} == {1, 2}
    assert [item["key"] for item in options["states"]] == ["uncorrected", "corrected"]


def test_correcting_from_the_list_overwrites_the_downstream_row(db):
    result, run = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    correct_incremental_level(
        db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L1", expected_level="L3",
        reason="这张实际是好图", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k1")
    db.commit()

    assert result.level == "L1", "人工等级就是新的事实"
    # 重发一版覆盖同一行，而不是新开一行。
    event = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).order_by(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id.desc()))
    assert event.revision == 2 and event.review_state == "reviewed"
    ledger = db.scalar(
        select(DownstreamPublishLedger)
        .where(DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id == event.id))
    assert ledger.trigger_kind == "human_correction"
    assert ledger.actor == "ops" and ledger.reason == "这张实际是好图"
    assert ledger.revision == 2 and ledger.previous_revision == 1

    published = json.loads(ledger.payload_json)["result"]
    # 人工这一版和自动那一版必须是同一层结构，否则下游投递器按自动那一层去取
    # output 会取空，直接判成契约错误死信——人工纠偏永远覆盖不到下游。
    auto = json.loads(db.scalar(
        select(DownstreamPublishLedger)
        .where(DownstreamPublishLedger.revision == 1)).payload_json)["result"]
    assert set(published) == set(auto)
    output = published["output"]
    assert output["final_level"] == "L1", "投出去的就是人工等级"
    # 模型自己算的那一版必须仍然看得见，否则事后分不清谁改的。
    assert output["normalized_result"]["model_level"] == "L3"
    assert output["human_override"]["previous_level"] == "L3"
    assert output["human_override"]["actor"] == "ops"


def test_the_list_shows_the_correction_after_it_happened(db):
    result, run = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L2", expected_level="L3",
                             reason="偏高", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k1")
    db.commit()
    row = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=1)["items"][0]
    assert row["level"] == "L2" and row["correction_state"] == "corrected"
    assert row["corrected_by"] == "ops" and row["correction_reason"] == "偏高"
    assert row["downstream"]["revision"] == 2 and row["downstream"]["review_state"] == "reviewed"


def test_leaving_a_row_alone_is_not_recorded_as_human_approval(db):
    _material(db, 1)
    row = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=1)["items"][0]
    assert row["correction_state"] == "uncorrected" and row["corrected_by"] == ""
    # 没人动过就只有自动那一版——「没纠偏」不能被读成「人工通过」。
    assert row["downstream"]["review_state"] == "auto"
    assert _reviewed_events(db) == []


def test_two_operators_on_the_same_row_do_not_silently_overwrite_each_other(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L2", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="第一个人改的", actor="ops-a", idempotency_key="k1")
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
        # 第二个人还看着 L3 的旧页面。
        correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L4", expected_level="L3",
                                  reason="第二个人改的", actor="ops-b", idempotency_key="k2")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_level_conflict"


def test_repeating_the_same_submission_does_not_publish_twice(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    for _ in range(2):
        correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L1", expected_level=None,
                                  reason="重复提交", actor="ops", idempotency_key="same")
        db.commit()
    assert len(_reviewed_events(db)) == 1, "重试不能再发一版"


def test_the_same_key_cannot_be_reused_for_a_different_level(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L1", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="x", actor="ops", idempotency_key="same")
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
        correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L2", expected_level=None,
                                  reason="x", actor="ops", idempotency_key="same")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_idempotency_conflict"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("level,code", [
    ("L5", "incremental_correction_level_invalid"),   # 该工作流已停用
    ("L9", "incremental_correction_level_invalid"),
    ("好", "incremental_correction_level_invalid"),
    ("", "incremental_correction_level_invalid"),
])
def test_a_level_the_workflow_does_not_publish_is_refused(db, level, code):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
        correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level=level, expected_level="L3",
                                  reason="x", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k")
    assert info.value.code == code


def test_a_correction_without_a_reason_is_refused(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    for reason in ("", "   "):
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
            correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L1", expected_level="L3",
                                      reason=reason, actor="ops", idempotency_key="k")
        assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_reason_required"


def test_setting_the_level_it_already_has_is_not_a_correction(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
        correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L3", expected_level="L3",
                                  reason="x", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_level_unchanged"
    assert _reviewed_events(db) == []


def test_a_material_that_never_reached_downstream_has_nothing_to_overwrite(db):
    result, run = _material(db, 1, level="L3", published=False)
    outputs = json.loads(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)
    outputs["downstream"]["status"] = "failed"
    result.workflow_node_outputs_json = json.dumps(outputs)
    db.commit()

        correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L1", expected_level="L3",
                                  reason="x", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_no_published_output"

def test_the_detail_view_says_which_steps_can_be_corrected_and_why_not(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1)
    detail = incremental_correction_detail(db, evaluation_id=result.id)
    nodes = {item["node_instance_id"]: item for item in detail["nodes"]}
    assert set(nodes) == {"prompt_a", "score_level_matcher_7", "downstream"}
    assert nodes["prompt_a"]["correctable"] is True
    # 写下游是接口节点：只留证据，不能改。
    assert nodes["downstream"]["correctable"] is False
    assert "接口" in nodes["downstream"]["read_only_reason"]
    assert detail["level"] == "L3" and detail["selectable_levels"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"]
    assert detail["model_execution_mode"] in {"simulated", "real"}


def test_the_detail_view_flattens_the_output_into_editable_fields(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1)
    detail = incremental_correction_detail(db, evaluation_id=result.id)
    matcher = next(item for item in detail["nodes"]
                   if item["node_instance_id"] == "score_level_matcher_7")
    paths = {field["field_path"]: field for field in matcher["fields"]}
    assert set(paths) == {"level", "score"}
    assert paths["score"]["value_type"] == "number"


def test_a_step_that_did_not_run_is_shown_but_not_editable(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1)
    outputs = json.loads(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)
    outputs["prompt_a"]["status"] = "skipped"
    result.workflow_node_outputs_json = json.dumps(outputs)
    db.commit()
    detail = incremental_correction_detail(db, evaluation_id=result.id)
    node = next(item for item in detail["nodes"] if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a")
    assert node["correctable"] is False and node["fields"] == []
    assert node["read_only_reason"] == "本次没有执行这一步"

def test_a_field_correction_syncs_the_list_columns_back_from_the_replay(db, monkeypatch):
    """重放引擎本身在 test_workflow_runtime_recovery 一线已有覆盖；这里测的是外面这层。

    重放会重算最终等级和分数。如果不同步回 ``evaluation_results``，列表还显示旧等级，
    详情显示新等级，同一条素材在两个页面上会各说各话。
    """

    from app import incremental_correction as module

    result, run = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    captured = {}

    def _fake_replay(_db, **kwargs):
        captured.update(kwargs)
        return SimpleNamespace(
            normalized_result={"level": "L1", "score": 91},
            affected_nodes=("score_level_matcher_7", "downstream"),
            downstream_publish_status="queued",
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "app.workflow_correction.append_node_correction_and_replay", _fake_replay)
    payload = module.correct_incremental_field(
        db, evaluation_id=result.id, node_instance_id="score_level_matcher_7",
        field_path="score", value=91, reason="分数算低了", actor="ops",
        idempotency_key="k1")
    db.commit()

    assert result.level == "L1" and result.score == 91
    assert result.review_revision == 1
    assert payload["level"] == "L1" and payload["correction_state"] == "corrected"
    assert payload["affected_nodes"] == ["score_level_matcher_7", "downstream"]
    # 理由和操作者要原样交给重放引擎，它才能写进下游台账。
    assert captured["reason"] == "分数算低了" and captured["actor"] == "ops"
    assert captured["run_id"] == run.id and captured["field_path"] == "score"
    assert captured["real_execution_confirmed"] is False


def test_a_paid_rerun_confirmation_is_passed_through_not_assumed(db, monkeypatch):
    from app import incremental_correction as module

    result, run = _material(db, 1)
    captured = {}
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "app.workflow_correction.append_node_correction_and_replay",
        lambda _db, **kwargs: (captured.update(kwargs), SimpleNamespace(
            normalized_result={}, affected_nodes=(), downstream_publish_status="queued"))[1])
    module.correct_incremental_field(
        db, evaluation_id=result.id, node_instance_id="prompt_a",
        field_path="classification.doc_primary", value="建筑", reason="分类写错",
        actor="ops", idempotency_key="k1", real_execution_confirmed=True)
    assert captured["real_execution_confirmed"] is True


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 看板：最近 N 条里人工把 AI 的等级改成了什么。
#
# 这里没有基准集真值，只有「改过的」和「没动的」。没动不等于人工确认正确——
# 下面的断言就是用来钉住这个口径的。
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def test_the_dashboard_counts_corrections_over_the_whole_window(db):
    for index in range(1, 11):
        _material(db, index, level="L3")
    board = correction_dashboard(db, window=1000)
    assert board["window_actual"] == 10 and board["corrected"] == 0
    assert board["uncorrected"] == 10 and board["correction_rate"] == 0.0
    # 一条都没改，矩阵必须是空的，而不是十条堆在对角线上冒充「人工确认正确」。
    assert board["level_changed"] == 0
    assert all(v == 0 for row in board["confusion_matrix"].values() for v in row.values())


def test_the_matrix_records_where_a_level_was_moved_to(db):
    first, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    second, _ = _material(db, 2, level="L3")
    _material(db, 3, level="L1")
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=first.id, level="L1", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="其实是好图", actor="ops-a", idempotency_key="k1")
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=second.id, level="L4", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="其实很差", actor="ops-b", idempotency_key="k2")
    db.commit()

    board = correction_dashboard(db, window=1000)
    assert board["corrected"] == 2 and board["uncorrected"] == 1
    assert board["correction_rate"] == 2 / 3
    assert board["confusion_matrix"]["L3"]["L1"] == 1
    assert board["confusion_matrix"]["L3"]["L4"] == 1
    by_level = {row["level"]: row for row in board["by_level"]}
    # AI 判了三条 L3？不——两条 L3 被改走了，原判仍按 AI 的算。
    assert by_level["L3"]["ai_count"] == 2 and by_level["L3"]["corrected_away"] == 2
    assert by_level["L3"]["changed_rate"] == 1.0
    assert by_level["L1"]["corrected_into"] == 1 and by_level["L1"]["net"] == 1
    assert by_level["L4"]["net"] == 1
    # 没出现过的等级是 None，不是 0——「从没判过」和「判了没被改」不是一回事。
    assert by_level["L2"]["ai_count"] == 0 and by_level["L2"]["changed_rate"] is None
    assert {item["actor"] for item in board["correctors"]} == {"ops-a", "ops-b"}


def test_the_ai_level_survives_being_corrected_more_than_once(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L2", expected_level="L3",
                              reason="一次", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k1")
    db.commit()
    correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level="L1", expected_level="L2",
                              reason="再来一次", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k2")
    db.commit()
    board = correction_dashboard(db, window=1000)
    # 模型判的始终是最早那次改动的旧值 L3，不是中间态 L2。
    assert board["confusion_matrix"]["L3"]["L1"] == 1
    assert sum(v for row in board["confusion_matrix"].values() for v in row.values()) == 1


def test_the_window_takes_the_most_recent_materials(db):
    for index in range(1, 8):
        _material(db, index, level="L3")
    assert correction_dashboard(db, window=3)["window_actual"] == 3
    assert correction_dashboard(db, window=1000)["window_actual"] == 7


def test_a_category_without_levels_is_counted_but_not_charted(db):
    result, _ = _material(db, 1, level=None)
    _material(db, 2, level="L3")
    board = correction_dashboard(db, window=1000)
    assert board["window_actual"] == 2 and board["unlevelled"] == 1
    assert sum(v for row in board["confusion_matrix"].values() for v in row.values()) == 0


def test_the_dashboard_honours_the_same_filters_as_the_queue(db):
    _material(db, 1, level="L3")
    _material(db, 2, level="L3", category="inspiration_image", version_id=2)
    assert correction_dashboard(db, category_key="model_3d_su")["window_actual"] == 1
    assert correction_dashboard(db, workflow_version_id=2)["window_actual"] == 1


def test_an_empty_window_reports_no_rate_rather_than_zero(db):
    board = correction_dashboard(db, window=1000)
    assert board["window_actual"] == 0
    # 一条素材都没有时「纠偏率 0%」是假信息，正确答案是「算不出来」。
    assert board["correction_rate"] is None and board["level_changed_rate"] is None


def test_indexed_and_legacy_rows_keep_one_queue_and_dashboard_window(db):
    # Both branches are disjoint; a populated mode overrides the frozen legacy
    # value. Equal timestamps exercise the stable id tiebreaker across pages.
    values = [_material(db, index, review_revision=int(index == 2)) for index in range(1, 5)]
    values[0][1].run_mode = 'incremental'
    values[2][1].run_mode = 'stock_regression'  # snapshot still says incremental
    values[3][1].run_mode = 'incremental'
    stamp = values[0][0].created_at
    for result, _ in values:
        result.created_at = stamp
    db.commit()
    expected = [values[i][0].id for i in (3, 1, 0)]
    pages = [list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=1, offset=i) for i in range(4)]
    assert [p['items'][0]['evaluation_id'] for p in pages[:3]] == expected
    assert all(p['total'] == 3 for p in pages)
    assert pages[-1]['items'] == [] and pages[-1]['has_more'] is False
    dashboard = correction_dashboard(db, window=2)
    assert dashboard['window_actual'] == 2
    assert dashboard['corrected'] == 1
    options = correction_filter_options(db)
    assert sum(row['total'] for row in options['categories']) == 3


def test_queue_queries_do_not_load_unused_model_payloads_or_retain_dashboard_snapshots(db):
    from sqlalchemy import event, inspect
    for i in range(1, 5):
        _, run = _material(db, i)
        run.run_mode = 'incremental'
    db.commit()
    db.expunge_all()
    statements = []
    def capture(_conn, _cursor, statement, _params, _context, _many):
        statements.append(statement)
    event.listen(db.bind, 'before_cursor_execute', capture)
    try:
        page = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=2)
        assert len(page['items']) == 2
        dashboard = correction_dashboard(db, window=3)
        assert dashboard['window_actual'] == 3
        correction_filter_options(db)
    finally:
        event.remove(db.bind, 'before_cursor_execute', capture)
    # No N+1 deferred reads; large A/B and per-node payloads have no purpose in
    # these endpoints, even when statistics span a thousand frozen runs.
    sql = '\n'.join(statements)
    assert 'evaluation_results.raw_response_a' not in sql
    assert 'evaluation_results.workflow_node_outputs_json' not in sql
    assert 'UNION ALL' in sql
    count = next(s for s in statements if 'count(*)' in s)
    assert 'evaluation_results.correction_history_json' not in count
    assert not any(isinstance(obj, ProductionRun) for obj in db.identity_map.values())


def test_inspiration_card_shows_the_factory_task_id_from_the_frozen_manifest(db):
    """灵感图的工厂任务号冻结在 input_manifest.inspiration_task 里，卡片要能显示出来和工厂对账。"""
    _, run = _material(db, 1, category="inspiration_image")
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]["inspiration_task"] = {
        "task_id": 13, "cloud_id": 2102, "project_id": "YQWJ-1155", "image_url": "https://img.example/13.jpg", "model_source": 2}
    run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    db.commit()
    row = list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)["items"][0]
    assert row["upstream"]["task_id"] == 13
    assert row["upstream"]["img_id"] is None and row["upstream"]["ll_id"] is None


def test_inspiration_level_corrections_across_l5_are_locked_until_the_factory_supports_it(db, monkeypatch):
    """灵感图 L5 是过滤：纠到 L5 要下游下架、从 L5 纠回要补发布，工厂侧上线前都锁住，页面据此置灰。"""
    from app.incremental_correction import INSPIRATION_L5_CORRECTION_ENV, level_lock

    monkeypatch.delenv(INSPIRATION_L5_CORRECTION_ENV, raising=False)
    # 真实灵感图机制启用 L5（过滤）
    monkeypatch.setitem(globals(), "LEVELS", [*LEVELS[:4], {"level": "L5", "min_score": 0, "enabled": True}])
    passed, _ = _material(db, 1, level="L3", category="inspiration_image", published=False)
    filtered, _ = _material(db, 2, level="L5", category="inspiration_image", published=False)

    rows = {row["evaluation_id"]: row for row in list_incremental_corrections(db, limit=50)["items"]}
    assert rows[passed.id]["locked_levels"] == ["L5"]
    assert rows[filtered.id]["locked_levels"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]
    assert "下架" in rows[passed.id]["level_lock_reason"] and "补发布" in rows[filtered.id]["level_lock_reason"]
    for result, target, current in ((passed, "L5", "L3"), (filtered, "L3", "L5")):
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalCorrectionError) as info:
            correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level=target, expected_level=current,
                                      reason="过滤", actor="ops", idempotency_key=f"lock-{result.id}")
        assert info.value.code == "incremental_correction_level_locked"
        db.rollback()
    outcome = batch_correct_incremental_level(
        db, items=[{"evaluation_id": passed.id, "expected_level": "L3"}], level="L5",
        reason="过滤", actor="ops", idempotency_key="lock-batch")
    assert outcome["skipped"][0]["code"] == "incremental_correction_level_locked"

    # 适配完成后置开关即放开；3DSU 从不受影响
    monkeypatch.setenv(INSPIRATION_L5_CORRECTION_ENV, "1")
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, passed.production_run_id)
    assert level_lock(run, "L3") == (frozenset(), None)
    monkeypatch.delenv(INSPIRATION_L5_CORRECTION_ENV)
    three_d, _ = _material(db, 3, level="L3")
    assert level_lock(db.get(ProductionRun, three_d.production_run_id), "L3") == (frozenset(), None)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_dashboard.py =====
from datetime import date,datetime,timezone
from app.models import WorkflowDefinition
from app.incremental_dashboard import aggregate_days,comparison_day,workflow_dashboard
from test_incremental_correction_queue import db,_material
import pytest

def test_completion_day_is_not_intake_day_and_history_is_not_zero():
    facts=[{'admitted':datetime(2026,9,19,15,tzinfo=timezone.utc),'completed':datetime(2026,9,19,17,tzinfo=timezone.utc)}]
    r=aggregate_days(facts,date(2026,9,19),date(2026,9,20),'year',today=date(2026,9,21))
    assert [p['platform_success'] for p in r['series']]==[0,1]
    assert [p['platform_admitted'] for p in r['series']]==[1,0]
    assert r['summary']['comparison_success'] is None
    assert r['summary']['upstream_increment'] is None and r['summary']['coverage'] is None
    assert r['summary']['cumulative_success']==1

def test_calendar_counterparts_do_not_duplicate_nonexistent_days():
    assert comparison_day(date(2024,2,29),'year') is None
    assert comparison_day(date(2026,3,31),'month') is None
    assert comparison_day(date(2026,1,3),'month')==date(2025,12,3)
    assert comparison_day(date(2026,9,21),'week')==date(2026,9,14)

def test_dates_are_bounded_and_empty_history_stays_unknown():
    assert aggregate_days([],date(2026,9,21),date(2026,9,21),'none',today=date(2026,9,21))['summary']['platform_success'] is None
    with pytest.raises(ValueError): aggregate_days([],date(2026,9,22),date(2026,9,21),'none',today=date(2026,9,21))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError): aggregate_days([],date(2025,1,1),date(2026,9,21),'none',today=date(2026,9,21))

def test_real_incremental_db_scope(db):
    _,run=_material(db,301,published=False)
    db.get(WorkflowDefinition,run.workflow_definition_id).allowed_categories_json='["model_3d_su"]'
    run.model_execution_mode='real'; run.run_mode='incremental'
    run.status='succeeded';run.created_at=datetime(2026,9,19,15);run.finished_at=datetime(2026,9,19,17)
    db.flush()
    result=workflow_dashboard(db,category_key=run.category_key,workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id,start=date(2026,9,19),end=date(2026,9,20),comparison='day',now=datetime(2026,9,21,tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert result['summary']['platform_success']==1
    assert result['series'][1]['platform_success']==1
    with pytest.raises(LookupError): workflow_dashboard(db,category_key='unrelated',workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id,start=date(2026,9,19),end=date(2026,9,20),comparison='none')


def test_material_dedup_manual_and_simulation_exclusion(db):
    from app.models import ContentIngressEvent
    from app.failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution
    runs=[]
    for index in (311,312,313,314):
        _,run=_material(db,index,published=False)
        run.run_mode='incremental';run.model_execution_mode='real'
        run.created_at=datetime(2026,9,19,15);run.finished_at=datetime(2026,9,19,17)
        runs.append(run)
    db.get(WorkflowDefinition,1).allowed_categories_json='["model_3d_su"]'
    # A second event/run for the same business material must not inflate counts.
    db.get(ContentIngressEvent,runs[1].source_id).content_record_id=db.get(ContentIngressEvent,runs[0].source_id).content_record_id
    runs[2].model_execution_mode='simulated'
    runs[3].status='failed';runs[3].finished_at=None
    db.add(ManualFailureResolution(idempotency_key='manual-dashboard',production_run_id=runs[3].id,
        level='L3',score=50,status='published',created_at=datetime(2026,9,20,2)))
    db.flush()
    result=workflow_dashboard(db,category_key='model_3d_su',workflow_definition_id=1,
        start=date(2026,9,19),end=date(2026,9,20),comparison='none',now=datetime(2026,9,21,tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert result['summary']['platform_admitted']==2
    assert result['summary']['platform_success']==2
    assert result['series'][1]['platform_success']==2


def test_business_dates_do_not_accept_unzoned_timestamps():
    from app.incremental_dashboard import business_date_isoformat
    assert business_date_isoformat(date(2026, 9, 21)) == '2026-09-21'
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        business_date_isoformat(datetime(2026, 9, 21))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_delivery_isolation.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import RuntimeAuditEvent
from app.runtime_outbox import RuntimeOutboxRequest, enqueue_runtime_outbox, claim_outbox_event
from app.connector_delivery import deliver_outbox_batch, FixtureDownstreamAdapter
from app.connector_governance import set_connector_pause_state
from test_connector_delivery import _mechanism, _target, _enqueue


@pytest.mark.parametrize("poison_state", ["queued", "delayed", "leased", "exhausted", "paused"])
def test_invalid_route_does_not_block_healthy_other_workflow(poison_state):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            poison = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, RuntimeOutboxRequest(
                event_key="invalid-managed", target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
                target_contract_version_id=target.id, mechanism_version_id=version.id,
                business_idempotency_key="poison", payload={"incremental_route": {"assignment_id": 999}}))
            # Distinct frozen workflow version, same physical target.
            from app.models import WorkflowVersion
            healthy_version = WorkflowVersion(workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
                version="native-v2", status="active", workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
                canonical_hash="b" * 64, created_by="test", step_manifest_json=version.step_manifest_json,
                edge_manifest_json=version.edge_manifest_json, input_schema_json=version.input_schema_json,
                output_schema_json=version.output_schema_json, resource_policy_json=version.resource_policy_json,
                validation_report_json=version.validation_report_json)
            db.add(healthy_version)
            db.flush()
            healthy = _enqueue(db, healthy_version, target, "healthy")
            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            if poison_state == "delayed":
                poison.available_at = now + timedelta(minutes=10)
            elif poison_state == "leased":
                claim_outbox_event(db, "other-worker", now=now, lease_seconds=600)
            elif poison_state == "exhausted":
                poison.attempt_count = poison.max_attempts
            elif poison_state == "paused":
                set_connector_pause_state(db, category_key=target.category_key,
                    mechanism_key=f"workflow-version:{version.id}", contract_key=target.contract_key,
                    state="paused", reason="test", expected_revision=None, actor="test")
            db.commit()
            adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint)
            result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="test", adapter=adapter, now=now)
            db.commit()
            db.expire_all()
            assert result.delivered_event_ids == (healthy.id,)
            assert adapter.attempted_batches == [("knowledge_graph:healthy",)]
            if poison_state == "queued":
                assert result.blocked_event_ids == (poison.id,)
                assert poison.status == "dead_lettered"
                assert poison.last_error_code == "incremental_route_mismatch"
                assert db.scalar(select(RuntimeAuditEvent).where(RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_key == str(poison.id))) is not None
            else:
                assert poison.status == ("leased" if poison_state == "leased" else "queued")
                assert result.blocked_event_ids == ()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_fence_quarantine_survives_unsafe_target_without_claiming_healthy_row():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            poison = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, RuntimeOutboxRequest(event_key="invalid", target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
                target_contract_version_id=target.id, mechanism_version_id=version.id, business_idempotency_key="invalid",
                payload={"incremental_route": {"assignment_id": 999}}))
            healthy = _enqueue(db, version, target, "healthy")
            db.commit()
            adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint, least_privileged=False)
            result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="test", adapter=adapter)
            db.commit()
            db.expire_all()
            assert result.blocked_event_ids == (poison.id,)
            assert poison.status == "dead_lettered"
            assert healthy.status == "queued"
            assert healthy.attempt_count == 0
            assert adapter.attempted_batches == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_route_is_rechecked_after_verification_before_physical_write():
    from app.workflow_snapshot_store import get_or_create_snapshot_blob
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target, "changed")
            db.commit()

            class ChangesDuringVerification(FixtureDownstreamAdapter):
                def verify_target(self):
                    event.payload_blob = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, {"incremental_route": {"assignment_id": 999}})
                    db.flush()
                    return super().verify_target()

                def read_back(self, keys):
                    pytest.fail("Rejected row must not trigger readback")

            adapter = ChangesDuringVerification(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint)
            result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="test", adapter=adapter)
            db.commit()
            assert result.blocked_event_ids == (event.id,)
            assert event.status == "dead_lettered"
            assert adapter.attempted_batches == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_empty_preflight_still_terminalizes_exhausted_expired_lease_without_io():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target, "exhausted")
            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            claim_outbox_event(db, "old-worker", now=now, lease_seconds=1)
            event.attempt_count = event.max_attempts
            db.commit()

            class NoIO(FixtureDownstreamAdapter):
                def verify_target(self):
                    pytest.fail("No eligible rows")

            deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="test", now=now + timedelta(seconds=2),
                adapter=NoIO(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint))
            db.commit()
            assert event.status == "dead_lettered"
            assert event.last_error_code == "outbox_delivery_lease_exhausted"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def _index_shapes(connection, table_name: str) -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    return {
        str(item["name"]): tuple(item["column_names"])
        for item in inspect(connection).get_indexes(table_name)
    }


def _insert_legacy_package(connection, *, dialect: str) -> None:
    if dialect == "mysql":
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO material_packages "
            "(status, package_key, name, source, category_key, "
            "mechanism_snapshot_json, created_by, created_at) "
            "VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)",
            (
                "active",
                "legacy-v91-package",
                "legacy",
                "production_import",
                "space_image",
                "{}",
                "migration-test",
            ),
        )
        return
    connection.execute(
        text(
            "INSERT INTO material_packages "
            "(status, package_key, name, source, category_key, "
            "mechanism_snapshot_json, created_by, created_at) "
            "VALUES ('active', 'legacy-v91-package', 'legacy', "
            "'production_import', 'space_image', '{}', "
            "'migration-test', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
        )
    )


def test_sqlite_v91_to_latest_is_idempotent_and_freezes_lane_identity(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'incremental-v91.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations ("
                "version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, "
                "applied_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
            )
            for migration in MIGRATIONS[:91]:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) VALUES (?, ?)",
                    (migration.version, migration.name),
                )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE content_ingress_events (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE material_packages ("
                "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active', "
                "package_key VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL, name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, "
                "source VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, category_key VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL, "
                "workflow_version_id INTEGER, mechanism_snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL, "
                "created_by VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL, created_at DATETIME NOT NULL)"
            )
            _insert_legacy_package(connection, dialect="sqlite")

            run_migrations(connection)
            run_migrations(connection)

            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == MIGRATIONS[-1].version
            assert LANE_COLUMNS | RETRY_COLUMNS <= {
                item["name"]
                for item in inspect(connection).get_columns("material_packages")
            }
            assert (LANE_INDEXES | RETRY_INDEXES).items() <= _index_shapes(
                connection, "material_packages"
            ).items()
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status FROM material_packages "
                "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
            ).scalar_one() == "incremental_legacy"
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE material_packages SET status='incremental_sent', "
                "incremental_failure_count=1, "
                "incremental_retry_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="immutable"):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE material_packages SET incremental_lane_key='drift' "
                    "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_v91_to_latest_adds_lane_contract_and_conditional_trigger(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        _insert_legacy_package(connection, dialect="mysql")
        for foreign_key in inspect(connection).get_foreign_keys("material_packages"):
            if tuple(foreign_key["constrained_columns"]) == ("incremental_event_id",):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "ALTER TABLE `material_packages` DROP FOREIGN KEY "
                    f"`{foreign_key['name']}`"
                )
        for index_name, columns in _index_shapes(
            connection, "material_packages"
        ).items():
            if set(columns) & (LANE_COLUMNS | RETRY_COLUMNS):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE `material_packages` DROP INDEX `{index_name}`"
                )
        for column_name in LANE_COLUMNS | RETRY_COLUMNS:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"ALTER TABLE `material_packages` DROP COLUMN `{column_name}`"
            )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= 92"
        )

        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=93)

        assert LANE_COLUMNS | RETRY_COLUMNS <= {
            item["name"]
            for item in inspect(connection).get_columns("material_packages")
        }
        assert (LANE_INDEXES | RETRY_INDEXES).items() <= _index_shapes(
            connection, "material_packages"
        ).items()
        assert any(
            tuple(item["constrained_columns"]) == ("incremental_event_id",)
            and tuple(item["referred_columns"]) == ("id",)
            for item in inspect(connection).get_foreign_keys("material_packages")
        )
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT status FROM material_packages "
            "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
        ).scalar_one() == "incremental_legacy"
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
            "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND "
            "TRIGGER_NAME='trg_material_packages_no_metadata_update'"
        ).scalar_one() == 1
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "UPDATE material_packages SET status='incremental_sent', "
            "incremental_failure_count=1, "
            "incremental_retry_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) "
            "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
        )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="immutable"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE material_packages SET incremental_lane_key='drift' "
                "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
            )


def test_postgres_v91_to_latest_adds_lane_contract_and_conditional_trigger(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        _insert_legacy_package(connection, dialect="postgresql")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            'DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS "trg_material_packages_no_metadata_update" '
            'ON "material_packages"'
        )
        for index_name in LANE_INDEXES | RETRY_INDEXES:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f'DROP INDEX IF EXISTS "{index_name}"')
        for column_name in LANE_COLUMNS | RETRY_COLUMNS:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f'ALTER TABLE material_packages DROP COLUMN "{column_name}" CASCADE'
            )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= 92"
        )

        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)

        assert LANE_COLUMNS | RETRY_COLUMNS <= {
            item["name"]
            for item in inspect(connection).get_columns("material_packages")
        }
        assert (LANE_INDEXES | RETRY_INDEXES).items() <= _index_shapes(
            connection, "material_packages"
        ).items()
        assert any(
            tuple(item["constrained_columns"]) == ("incremental_event_id",)
            and tuple(item["referred_columns"]) == ("id",)
            for item in inspect(connection).get_foreign_keys("material_packages")
        )
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT status FROM material_packages "
            "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
        ).scalar_one() == "incremental_legacy"
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pg_trigger WHERE NOT tgisinternal "
            "AND tgname='trg_material_packages_no_metadata_update'"
        ).scalar_one() == 1
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "UPDATE material_packages SET status='incremental_sent', "
            "incremental_failure_count=1, "
            "incremental_retry_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
            "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
        )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="immutable"):
        with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE material_packages SET incremental_lane_key='drift' "
                "WHERE package_key='legacy-v91-package'"
            )


def test_postgres_current_baseline_keeps_active_incremental_package_runnable(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        event_id = connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO content_ingress_events "
            "(event_id, schema_version, event_type, source_system, occurred_at, "
            "payload_hash, payload_json, status, received_by, received_at) "
            "VALUES ('current-v93-event', 'content-event-v1', "
            "'content.created', 'migration-test', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, "
            "'aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa', "
            "'{}', 'applied', 'migration-test', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) "
            "RETURNING id"
        ).scalar_one()
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO material_packages "
            "(status, package_key, name, source, category_key, "
            "mechanism_snapshot_json, incremental_event_id, "
            "incremental_snapshot_hash, incremental_lane_key, "
            "incremental_batch_size, incremental_due_at, created_by, created_at) "
            "VALUES ('active', 'current-v93-package', 'current', "
            "'production_import', 'space_image', '{}', %(event_id)s, "
            "'bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb', "
            "'space_image:current', 100, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, "
            "'migration-test', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)",
            {"event_id": event_id},
        )

        create_postgres_baseline(connection)

        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT status FROM material_packages "
            "WHERE package_key='current-v93-package'"
        ).scalar_one() == "active"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_evidence.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import importlib.util
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.database import Base
from app.models import WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion, IncrementalEntityAssignment, IncrementalShadowResult, ProductionRun
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def test_evidence_module_exists():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec('app.incremental_evidence') is not None, 'persistent comparison and sampling are missing'


@pytest.fixture
def evidence_db():
    from app import incremental_evidence_models
    from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://', connect_args={'check_same_thread':False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        versions = seed_incremental_versions(db)
        db.commit()
        yield db, versions[0]
    engine.dispose()


def reference(version, **changes):
    item = dict(category_key='space_image', workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
        workflow_version_id=version.id, source_system='old', entity_id='asset1', source_version='v1',
        step_key='score', result={'grade':'A', 'status':'ok', 'score':2}, evidence_kind='actual', reference_source='operator-export')
    return {**item, **changes}


def shadow(db, version, *, source_version='v1', result=None):
    assignment = IncrementalEntityAssignment(identity_hash='a'*64, category_key='space_image', source_system='old',
        entity_id='asset1', workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id, policy_id=1, destination='shadow', bucket=1)
    db.add(assignment); db.flush()
    row=IncrementalShadowResult(assignment_id=assignment.id, run_key='run1', step_key='score',
        workflow_version_id=version.id, snapshot_hash='b'*64, event_id='e1', source_version=source_version,
        policy_revision=1, result_json=json.dumps(result or {'grade':'A','status':'ok','score':2}), result_hash='c'*64)
    db.add(row);db.commit()
    return row


def test_reference_is_immutable_and_idempotent(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import import_reference_results, EvidenceError
    from app.incremental_evidence_models import IncrementalReferenceResult
    db,v=evidence_db
    first=import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v)],reason='compare',actor='admin')
    second=import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v)],reason='retry',actor='admin')
    assert first['items'][0]['id']==second['items'][0]['id']
    assert second['reused']==1
    with pytest.raises(EvidenceError, match='不可覆盖'):
        import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v,result={'grade':'B'})],reason='change',actor='admin')
    assert json.loads(db.scalar(select(IncrementalReferenceResult)).result_json)['grade']=='A'


def test_pairing_requires_exact_source_version_and_reports_differences(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import import_reference_results, list_comparisons
    db,v=evidence_db
    shadow(db,v)
    args=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id)
    import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v,source_version='v0')],reason='old',actor='admin')
    assert list_comparisons(db,**args)['items'][0]['comparison_status']=='missing_reference'
    import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v,result={'grade':'B','status':'ok','score':1})],reason='match',actor='admin')
    pair=list_comparisons(db,**args)['items'][0]
    assert pair['comparison_status']=='different'
    assert [x['path'] for x in pair['differences']]==['/grade','/score']
    assert pair['differences'][1]['numeric_delta']==1


def test_equal_and_incompatible_are_distinct(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import import_reference_results, list_comparisons
    db,v=evidence_db
    s=shadow(db,v)
    import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v)],reason='match',actor='admin')
    args=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id)
    assert list_comparisons(db,**args)['items'][0]['comparison_status']=='equal'
    s.workflow_version_id=v.id+999; db.flush()
    assert list_comparisons(db,**args)['items'][0]['comparison_status']=='incompatible'


def test_sampling_is_opt_in_persisted_and_time_bounded(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import configure_capacity_sampling, collect_due_capacity_samples, acceptance_report
    from app.incremental_evidence_models import IncrementalCapacitySample
    db,v=evidence_db
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    assert collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now)['sampled']==0
    args=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id)
    policy=configure_capacity_sampling(db,**args,enabled=True,expected_revision=0,interval_seconds=60,reason='observe',actor='admin',now=now)
    assert policy['revision']==1
    assert collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now)['sampled']==1
    db.commit()
    assert collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now+timedelta(seconds=30))['sampled']==0
    assert collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now+timedelta(seconds=60))['sampled']==1
    db.commit()
    assert len(list(db.scalars(select(IncrementalCapacitySample))))==2
    result=acceptance_report(db,**args,since=now-timedelta(seconds=1),until=now+timedelta(seconds=61))
    assert result['capacity']['backlog_slope_per_second']==0
    assert result['capacity']['observed_drain_seconds'] is None
    assert result['quality']['human_accuracy'] is None
    assert result['acceptance_status']=='insufficient_evidence'
    assert result['cost']['invoice_reconciled'] is False


def test_report_requires_timezone_and_valid_window(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import acceptance_report, EvidenceError
    db,v=evidence_db
    with pytest.raises(EvidenceError):
        acceptance_report(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
