def test_report_uses_real_run_cost_and_observed_backlog_recovery(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import configure_capacity_sampling, collect_due_capacity_samples, acceptance_report
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    db,v=evidence_db
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    run=ProductionRun(run_key='evidence-run',idempotency_key='evidence-key',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
        workflow_version_id=v.id,snapshot_json=json.dumps({'runtime_context':{'incremental_route':{'destination':'shadow'}}}),
        snapshot_hash='a'*64,category_key='space_image',source_type='content_ingress_event',source_id=1,
        requested_by='test',owner='test',model_execution_mode='real',status='queued',created_at=now-timedelta(seconds=10))
    db.add(run);db.flush()
    args=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id)
    configure_capacity_sampling(db,**args,enabled=True,expected_revision=0,interval_seconds=60,reason='observe',actor='admin',now=now)
    collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now)
    db.commit()
    run.status='succeeded';run.finished_at=now+timedelta(seconds=30)
    collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now+timedelta(seconds=60))
    report=acceptance_report(db,**args,since=now-timedelta(minutes=1),until=now+timedelta(minutes=2))
    assert report['runs']==[{'destination':'shadow','evidence_kind':'actual','status':'succeeded','count':1}]
    assert report['capacity']['observed_drain_seconds']==60
    assert report['capacity']['backlog_slope_per_second']==pytest.approx(-1/60)
    assert report['capacity']['samples'][0]['oldest_age_seconds']==10


def test_failed_import_batch_is_atomic_and_source_mismatch_is_missing(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import import_reference_results, EvidenceError
    from app.incremental_evidence_models import IncrementalReferenceResult
    db,v=evidence_db
    import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v)],reason='initial',actor='admin')
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(EvidenceError):
        import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v,entity_id='second'),reference(v,result={'bad':1})],reason='batch',actor='admin')
    db.commit()
    assert len(list(db.scalars(select(IncrementalReferenceResult))))==1


def test_api_requires_admin_for_mutation_and_bounded_requests(evidence_db):
    from fastapi import FastAPI
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.incremental_evidence_api import build_incremental_evidence_router
    db,v=evidence_db
    current=SimpleNamespace(username='operator',role='reviewer',is_active=True,is_admin=False)
    app=FastAPI();app.include_router(build_incremental_evidence_router(lambda: current))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db]=lambda: (yield db)
    client=TestClient(app)
    path=f'/api/incremental-evidence/sampling/space_image/{v.workflow_definition_id}'
    payload={'enabled':True,'expected_revision':0,'interval_seconds':60,'reason':'observe'}
    assert client.put(path,json=payload).status_code==403
    current.is_admin=True
    assert client.put(path,json={**payload,'reason':'   '}).status_code==422
    assert client.put(path,json=payload).status_code==200
    assert client.put(path,json=payload).status_code==409
    assert client.get('/api/incremental-evidence/comparisons',params={'category_key':'space_image',
        'workflow_definition_id':v.workflow_definition_id,'limit':201}).status_code==422


def test_baseline_frozen_truth_metrics_are_available_for_operations_review(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import acceptance_report
    from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem
    db,v=evidence_db
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    run=BaselineRegressionRun(baseline_set_id=1,sequence_no=1,workflow_version_id=v.id,
        execution_engine='native_workflow',category_key='space_image',strategy_snapshot_json='{}',
        execution_snapshot_json=json.dumps({'model_execution_mode':'real'}),baseline_set_fingerprint='f'*64,
        status='completed',total=1,completed=1,valid_predictions=1,created_at=now)
    db.add(run);db.flush()
    db.add(BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=run.id,baseline_set_item_id=1,asset_id=1,status='completed',
        expected_result_json=json.dumps({'room':'living','unknown_field':''}),truth_contract_key='kg-v1',
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps({'normalized_result':{'room':'living'}})))
    db.flush()
    report=acceptance_report(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
        since=now-timedelta(minutes=1),until=now+timedelta(minutes=1))
    baseline=report['quality']['baseline_runs'][0]
    assert baseline['workflow_version_id']==v.id
    assert baseline['metrics']['field_aggregate']['accuracy']==1
    assert baseline['metrics']['pending_truth_field_count']==1
    assert baseline['truth_contract_keys']==['kg-v1']
    assert report['acceptance_status']=='evidence_available_for_review'
    assert report['quality']['baseline_truth_accuracy']==1
    assert 'actual_capacity_samples' in report['missing_evidence']


def test_capacity_trends_keep_synthetic_and_actual_separate(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import configure_capacity_sampling, collect_due_capacity_samples, acceptance_report
    db,v=evidence_db
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for mode in ('real','simulated'):
        db.add(ProductionRun(run_key=mode,idempotency_key=mode,workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
            workflow_version_id=v.id,snapshot_json=json.dumps({'runtime_context':{'incremental_route':{'destination':'shadow'}}}),
            snapshot_hash='a'*64,category_key='space_image',source_type='content_ingress_event',source_id=1,
            requested_by='test',owner='test',model_execution_mode=mode,status='queued',created_at=now))
    db.flush()
    args=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id)
    configure_capacity_sampling(db,**args,enabled=True,expected_revision=0,interval_seconds=60,reason='observe',actor='admin',now=now)
    collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now);db.commit()
    simulated=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key=='simulated'))
    simulated.status='succeeded';db.flush()
    collect_due_capacity_samples(db,now=now+timedelta(seconds=60))
    report=acceptance_report(db,**args,since=now-timedelta(seconds=1),until=now+timedelta(minutes=2))
    groups=report['capacity']['groups']
    actual=next(g for g in groups if g['evidence_kind']=='actual' and g['destination']=='shadow')
    synthetic=next(g for g in groups if g['evidence_kind']=='synthetic' and g['destination']=='shadow')
    assert actual['backlog_slope_per_second']==0
    assert actual['observed_drain_seconds'] is None
    assert synthetic['backlog_slope_per_second']==pytest.approx(-1/60)
    assert report['capacity']['aggregate_acceptance_eligible'] is False


def test_level_truth_accuracy_and_missing_predictions_are_not_conflated(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import acceptance_report
    from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem
    db,v=evidence_db
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        execution_engine='native_workflow',category_key='space_image',strategy_snapshot_json='{}',
        execution_snapshot_json='{"model_execution_mode":"real"}',baseline_set_fingerprint='f'*64,
        status='completed',total=1,completed=1,valid_predictions=1,created_at=now)
    db.add(run);db.flush()
    item=BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=run.id,baseline_set_item_id=1,asset_id=1,status='completed',
        expected_level='L3',result_snapshot_json='{"normalized_result":{"level":"L3"}}')
    db.add(item);db.flush()
    args=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
        since=now-timedelta(minutes=1),until=now+timedelta(minutes=1))
    assert acceptance_report(db,**args)['quality']['baseline_truth_accuracy']==1
    item.result_snapshot_json='{}';db.flush()
    assert acceptance_report(db,**args)['quality']['baseline_truth_accuracy'] is None


def test_cost_summary_preserves_unknown_outcome_and_excludes_settled_estimates(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import acceptance_report
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    db,v=evidence_db
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    run=ProductionRun(run_key='cost-run',idempotency_key='cost-key',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
        workflow_version_id=v.id,snapshot_json='{"runtime_context":{"incremental_route":{"destination":"formal"}}}',
        snapshot_hash='a'*64,category_key='space_image',source_type='content_ingress_event',source_id=1,
        requested_by='test',owner='test',model_execution_mode='real',status='running',created_at=now)
    db.add(run);db.flush()
    for key,status,error,actual,estimate in [('known','succeeded','',20,30),('unknown','reserved','model_invocation_outcome_unknown',0,50)]:
        db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id,step_attempt_id=1,invocation_key=key,node_instance_id='score',
            provider='test',model_id='test',status=status,error_code=error,actual_cost_micros=actual,
            estimated_cost_micros=estimate,created_at=now))
    db.flush()
    report=acceptance_report(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
        since=now-timedelta(minutes=1),until=now+timedelta(minutes=1))
    known=next(g for g in report['cost']['groups'] if g['status']=='succeeded')
    unknown=next(g for g in report['cost']['groups'] if g['status']=='reserved')
    assert known['recorded_actual_micros']==20 and known['reserved_estimate_micros']==0
    assert unknown['unresolved_calls']==1 and unknown['reserved_estimate_micros']==50
    assert report['cost']['invoice_reconciled'] is False


def test_comparison_marks_historical_source_and_preserves_null_vs_missing(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import import_reference_results, list_comparisons
    from app.models import ContentRecord
    db,v=evidence_db

    assert item['differences'][0]['shadow_present'] is True


def test_comparison_keyset_pagination_does_not_repeat_rows(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import list_comparisons
    db,v=evidence_db
    first=shadow(db,v)
    db.add(IncrementalShadowResult(assignment_id=first.assignment_id,run_key='run2',step_key='score',
        workflow_version_id=v.id,snapshot_hash='b'*64,event_id='e2',source_version='v2',policy_revision=1,
        result_json='{"grade":"A"}',result_hash='d'*64));db.flush()
    args=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,limit=1)
    page1=list_comparisons(db,**args)
    page2=list_comparisons(db,**args,before_id=page1['next_before_id'])
    assert page1['items'][0]['shadow_id']!=page2['items'][0]['shadow_id']
    assert page2['next_before_id'] is None


def test_reference_can_target_successive_workflow_versions_without_overwriting(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import import_reference_results, list_comparisons
    db,v=evidence_db
    other=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id==v.workflow_definition_id,
        WorkflowVersion.id!=v.id).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id))
    assert other is not None
    first=shadow(db,v)
    db.add(IncrementalShadowResult(assignment_id=first.assignment_id,run_key='next-version',step_key=first.step_key,
        workflow_version_id=other.id,snapshot_hash='f'*64,event_id=first.event_id,source_version=first.source_version,
        policy_revision=1,result_json='{"grade":"B","status":"ok","score":3}',result_hash='e'*64))
    old=import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(v)],reason='old mechanism',actor='admin')
    new=import_reference_results(db,items=[reference(other,result={'grade':'B','status':'ok','score':3})],reason='new mechanism',actor='admin')
    assert old['items'][0]['id']!=new['items'][0]['id']
    comparisons=list_comparisons(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id)['items']
    assert len(comparisons)==2
    assert all(item['comparison_status']=='equal' for item in comparisons)
    assert comparisons[0]['reference_id']==new['items'][0]['id']
    assert comparisons[1]['reference_id']==old['items'][0]['id']


def test_baseline_without_comparable_truth_keeps_rates_unknown(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import acceptance_report
    from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem
    db,v=evidence_db
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    run=BaselineRegressionRun(baseline_set_id=1,sequence_no=1,workflow_version_id=v.id,
        execution_engine='native_workflow',category_key='space_image',strategy_snapshot_json='{}',
        execution_snapshot_json='{"model_execution_mode":"real"}',baseline_set_fingerprint='f'*64,
        status='running',total=2,completed=0,valid_predictions=0,created_at=now)
    db.add(run);db.flush()
    db.add(BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=run.id,baseline_set_item_id=1,asset_id=1,status='queued',
        expected_result_json='{"room":""}',result_snapshot_json='{}'))
    db.flush()
    report=acceptance_report(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=v.workflow_definition_id,
        since=now-timedelta(minutes=1),until=now+timedelta(minutes=1))

    assert baseline['metrics']['field_aggregate']['accuracy'] is None
    assert baseline['metrics']['field_aggregate']['precision'] is None
    assert baseline['metrics']['field_aggregate']['recall'] is None
    assert baseline['sample_evidence_complete'] is False
    assert baseline['accuracy'] is None


def test_sampling_stale_session_cannot_overwrite_newer_disabled_revision(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import configure_capacity_sampling, EvidenceError
    from app.incremental_evidence_models import IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy
    db, version = evidence_db
    args = dict(category_key='space_image', workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
        enabled=True, expected_revision=0, interval_seconds=60, reason='observe', actor='test')
    configure_capacity_sampling(db, **args); db.commit()
    cached = db.scalar(select(IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy)); db.commit()
    assert cached.revision == 1
    with Session(db.bind) as other:
        configure_capacity_sampling(other, **{**args, 'enabled':False, 'expected_revision':1})
        other.commit()
    with pytest.raises(EvidenceError) as error:
        configure_capacity_sampling(db, **{**args, 'expected_revision':1})
    assert error.value.code == 'sampling_revision_conflict'
    db.rollback(); db.refresh(cached)
    assert cached.revision == 2 and cached.enabled is False


def test_running_baseline_with_all_item_rows_is_not_complete_accuracy(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_evidence import acceptance_report
    from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem
    db, version = evidence_db
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    run = BaselineRegressionRun(baseline_set_id=1, sequence_no=1, workflow_version_id=version.id,
        execution_engine='native_workflow', category_key='space_image', strategy_snapshot_json='{}',
        execution_snapshot_json='{"model_execution_mode":"real"}', baseline_set_fingerprint='f'*64,
        status='running', total=2, completed=1, valid_predictions=1, created_at=now)
    db.add(run); db.flush()
    db.add(BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=run.id, baseline_set_item_id=1, asset_id=1, status='completed',
        expected_result_json='{"room":"living"}', result_snapshot_json='{"normalized_result":{"room":"living"}}'))
    pending = BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=run.id, baseline_set_item_id=2, asset_id=2, status='queued',
        expected_result_json='{"room":"kitchen"}', result_snapshot_json='{}')
    db.add(pending); db.flush()
    args = dict(category_key='space_image', workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
        since=now-timedelta(minutes=1), until=now+timedelta(minutes=1))
    report = acceptance_report(db, **args)
    baseline = report['quality']['baseline_runs'][0]
    assert baseline['metrics']['field_aggregate']['accuracy'] == 1
    assert baseline['sample_evidence_complete'] is False
    assert baseline['accuracy'] is None
    assert report['quality']['baseline_truth_accuracy'] is None
    assert 'actual_baseline_truth_metrics' in report['missing_evidence']
    # Once the remaining item finishes, the complete cohort's accuracy is usable.
    pending.status = 'completed'; pending.result_snapshot_json = '{"normalized_result":{"room":"bedroom"}}'
    run.status = 'completed'; run.completed = 2; run.valid_predictions = 2
    db.flush()
    finished = acceptance_report(db, **args)
    assert finished['quality']['baseline_runs'][0]['sample_evidence_complete'] is True
    assert finished['quality']['baseline_truth_accuracy'] == .5

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_execution_mode.py =====
"""Binding execution authority supplements frozen manifests, never rewrites them."""
import copy
import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.incremental_input import IncrementalInputError, incremental_autorun_policy
from app.incremental_mechanisms import (
    IncrementalMechanismError, list_incremental_mechanism_events,
    resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue, rollback_incremental_mechanism,
    set_incremental_mechanism,
)
from app.models import CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, ProductionRun
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def snapshot(mode='real'):
    return {'workflow': {'manifest': {'resource_policy': {}}}, 'runtime_context': {
        'run_mode': 'incremental', 'incremental_binding_id': 1,
        'incremental_binding_lock_version': 2,
        'incremental_execution_policy': {'authority': 'incremental_binding',
            'binding_id': 1, 'binding_lock_version': 2, 'model_execution_mode': mode}}}


def test_binding_policy_supplies_missing_mode_without_mutating_manifest():
    frozen = snapshot()
    before = copy.deepcopy(frozen)
    assert incremental_autorun_policy(frozen)['model_execution_mode'] == 'real'
    assert frozen == before
    frozen['workflow']['manifest']['resource_policy']['incremental_autorun'] = {'model_execution_mode': 'simulated'}
    assert incremental_autorun_policy(frozen)['model_execution_mode'] == 'simulated'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', [dict(binding_id=9), dict(binding_lock_version=3),
                                   dict(authority='event_payload'), dict(model_execution_mode='invalid')])
def test_mismatched_binding_authority_is_rejected(change):
    frozen = snapshot()
    frozen['runtime_context']['incremental_execution_policy'].update(change)
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):
        incremental_autorun_policy(frozen)


def test_mode_switch_cas_snapshot_and_rollback_preserve_manifests(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "mode.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        version = seed_incremental_versions(db, count=1)[0]
        original = (version.canonical_hash, canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)))
        binding = set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key='space_image', workflow_version_id=version.id,
            expected_lock_version=None, reason='模拟启用', actor='admin', model_execution_mode='simulated')
        first = resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, 'space_image')
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key='space_image', workflow_version_id=version.id,
            expected_lock_version=binding.lock_version, reason='明确真实调用', actor='admin', model_execution_mode='real')
        second = resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, 'space_image')
        assert incremental_autorun_policy(second.workflow_snapshot)['model_execution_mode'] == 'real'
        assert incremental_autorun_policy(first.workflow_snapshot)['model_execution_mode'] == 'simulated'
        assert first.snapshot_hash != second.snapshot_hash
        assert original == (version.canonical_hash, canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)))
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError, match='刷新'):
            set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key='space_image', workflow_version_id=version.id,
                expected_lock_version=1, reason='stale', actor='admin', model_execution_mode='simulated')
        events = list_incremental_mechanism_events(db, 'space_image')
        assert events[-1].previous_model_execution_mode == 'simulated'
        assert events[-1].next_model_execution_mode == 'real'
        rolled = rollback_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key='space_image', event_id=events[-1].id,
            expected_lock_version=2, reason='恢复模拟', actor='admin')
        assert rolled.model_execution_mode == 'simulated'
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('binding_mode', ['real', 'simulated', None])
def test_native_dispatch_uses_binding_authority_not_event_payload(monkeypatch, binding_mode):
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _headers, _payload, _close
    from test_incremental_autorun import _enable_real_incremental_policy
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        version, _ = _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch)
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        del policy['incremental_autorun']['model_execution_mode']
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        version.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)).encode()).hexdigest()
        original = version.canonical_hash
        binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding))
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key='space_image', workflow_version_id=version.id,
            expected_lock_version=binding.lock_version, reason='test explicit mode', actor='admin',
            model_execution_mode=binding_mode)
        db.commit()
        payload = _payload(event_id='binding-mode-event', asset_id=asset.id)
        payload['payload']['model_execution_mode'] = 'real'
        payload['payload']['runtime_context'] = snapshot()['runtime_context']
        response = client.post('/api/content-ingress/events', headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        db.expire_all()
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run is not None, response.text
        assert run.model_execution_mode == (binding_mode or 'simulated')
        assert db.get(type(version), version.id).canonical_hash == original
        if binding_mode is not None:
            runtime = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)['runtime_context']
            assert runtime['incremental_execution_policy']['model_execution_mode'] == binding_mode
            assert runtime['real_execution_confirmed'] is (binding_mode == 'real')
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

    from app.incremental_mechanisms_api import IncrementalMechanismSetRequest

        IncrementalMechanismSetRequest(workflow_version_id=1, reason='bad', model_execution_mode='automatic')


def test_frozen_explicit_simulated_manifest_cannot_be_enabled_as_real(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "simulation-only.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = seed_incremental_versions(db, count=1)[0]
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy['incremental_autorun'] = {'model_execution_mode': 'simulated'}
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        original = version.canonical_hash
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError) as error:

                expected_lock_version=None, reason='refused', actor='admin', model_execution_mode='real')
        assert error.value.code == 'incremental_execution_mode_forbidden'
        assert db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)) is None
        assert version.canonical_hash == original
    engine.dispose()

@pytest.mark.parametrize('mode', ['real', None])
def test_dashboard_uses_bound_mode_and_remains_read_only(mode):
    from sqlalchemy import event
    from app.incremental_automation import automation_cards
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding))
        binding.model_execution_mode = mode
        db.commit()
        def refuse_writes(_connection, _cursor, statement, *_args):
            assert statement.lstrip().upper().startswith(('SELECT', 'PRAGMA')), statement
        event.listen(engine, 'before_cursor_execute', refuse_writes)
        try:
            card = next(row for row in automation_cards(db)['items'] if row['bound'])
        finally:
            event.remove(engine, 'before_cursor_execute', refuse_writes)
        assert card['execution_mode'] == (mode or 'simulated')
        if mode == 'real':
            assert card['state'] == 'blocked'
            assert card['enable_blocker']
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

def test_batch_route_is_selected_once_and_frozen_with_matching_budget(monkeypatch):
    from app import doubao_endpoints
    from app.doubao_endpoint_models import DoubaoInferenceEndpoint
    from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelRegistryEntry, WorkflowDefinition
    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_plan_for_manifest
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _headers, _payload, _close
    from test_incremental_autorun import _enable_real_incremental_policy
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        model = db.scalar(select(ModelConfig))
        registry = db.scalar(select(ModelRegistryEntry))
        model.provider = registry.provider = 'doubao'
        model.model_id = registry.model_id = 'doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428'
        db.flush()
        version, _ = _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch)
        definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, version.workflow_definition_id)
        endpoint = DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(endpoint_id='ep-bi-synthetic-only', display_name='synthetic',
            category_key='space_image', foundation_model=model.model_id)
        db.add(endpoint)
        db.flush()
        definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id = endpoint.id
        definition.doubao_inference_mode = 'batch'
        db.commit()
        original_hash = version.canonical_hash
        selected = []
        original_select = doubao_endpoints.select_inference_route
        def capture_route(*args, **kwargs):
            route = original_select(*args, **kwargs)
            selected.append(route)
            return route
        monkeypatch.setattr(doubao_endpoints, 'select_inference_route', capture_route)
        payload = _payload(event_id='one-batch-route', asset_id=asset.id)
        payload['payload']['runtime_context'] = {'inference_route': {'mode': 'online', 'endpoint_id': 'forged'}}
        response = client.post('/api/content-ingress/events', headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run is not None, response.text
        assert len(selected) == 1, 'budget and execution must not independently reselect the route'
        runtime = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)['runtime_context']
        assert runtime['inference_route'] == selected[0]
        assert runtime['inference_route']['mode'] == 'batch'
        assert run.doubao_endpoint_id == endpoint.endpoint_id
        manifest = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)['workflow']['manifest']
        inputs = runtime['input_manifest']
        budget, limits = real_execution_plan_for_manifest(db, manifest, inference_route=selected[0], input_manifest=inputs)
        assert runtime['real_execution_budget'] == budget
        assert runtime['real_execution_node_limits'] == limits
        assert version.canonical_hash == original_hash
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('backend', ['sqlite', 'mysql'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('partial', [False, True])
def test_113_migration_adds_nullable_mode_without_upgrading_old_authority(monkeypatch, backend, partial):
    from sqlalchemy import inspect
    from app.migrations import runner, mysql_baseline
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    with engine.begin() as db:
        db.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)')
        db.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES (112, 'existing')")
        db.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE category_incremental_mechanism_bindings (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY' +
            (', model_execution_mode VARCHAR(20))' if partial else ')'))
        db.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE category_incremental_mechanism_events (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)')
        db.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO category_incremental_mechanism_bindings (id) VALUES (1)')
        if partial:
            db.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO category_incremental_mechanism_bindings VALUES (2, 'simulated')")
        if backend == 'sqlite':
            upgrades = [m for m in runner.MIGRATIONS if m.version == 113]
            assert len(upgrades) == 1, 'migration 113 missing'
            upgrade = lambda: upgrades[0].up(db)
        else:
            class Connection:
                def exec_driver_sql(self, query, parameters=()):
                    return db.exec_driver_sql(query.replace('%s', '?'), parameters)
            connection = Connection()
            monkeypatch.setattr(mysql_baseline, '_require_mysql', lambda c: None)
            monkeypatch.setattr(mysql_baseline, 'inspect', lambda c: inspect(db))
            upgrade = lambda: mysql_baseline.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=113)
        upgrade()
        upgrade()
        values = db.exec_driver_sql('SELECT model_execution_mode FROM category_incremental_mechanism_bindings ORDER BY id').scalars().all()
        assert values == ([None, 'simulated'] if partial else [None])
        assert {'previous_model_execution_mode', 'next_model_execution_mode'} <= {
            column['name'] for column in inspect(db).get_columns('category_incremental_mechanism_events')}
        if backend == 'mysql':
            assert db.exec_driver_sql('SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations').scalar_one() == 113
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_fence_query_count.py =====
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine,event
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.database import Base
from app.incremental_automation import _lock_definition
from app.workflow_registry import create_workflow_definition

@pytest.mark.parametrize('shared',[False,True])
def test_definition_fence_uses_one_authoritative_read(tmp_path,shared):
    engine=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'fence.db'))
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        row=create_workflow_definition(db,workflow_key='query-count',name='test',description='',
            owner='test',allowed_categories=['model_3d_su'],created_by='test')
        ident=row.id;db.commit()
    statements=[]
    def observe(conn,cursor,statement,parameters,context,many):
        if statement.lstrip().upper().startswith('SELECT') and 'workflow_definitions' in statement:
            statements.append(statement)
    event.listen(engine,'before_cursor_execute',observe)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        assert _lock_definition(db,ident,shared=shared).id==ident
        assert len(statements)==1,'fence must not precede its locking read with another full read'
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_historical_workflow_identity.py =====
"""Legacy raw-node identities retain full package-manifest drift protection."""
from contextlib import contextmanager
import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import ConnectorContractVersion, ContentIngressEvent, MaterialPackage, ProductionRun
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, WorkflowRuntimeError, create_production_run
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _headers, _payload, _close
from test_incremental_autorun import _enable_real_incremental_policy


@contextmanager
def packaged_request(monkeypatch, identity_kind):
    from app.incremental_autorun import _bounded_idempotency_key
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        version, _ = _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch, batch_size=2)
        # Exercise legitimate connector enrichment from registered SQL contracts;
        # no connection is opened because this test only creates the native run.
        for contract in db.scalars(select(ConnectorContractVersion)):
            contract.adapter_key = 'sql-envelope-v1'
        nodes = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
        for node in nodes:
            if node['kind'] in {'upstream_connector', 'downstream_projection'}:
                node['config'].pop('connector_snapshot', None)
                node['config'].pop('connector_contract_version_id', None)
        version.step_manifest_json = json.dumps(nodes, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
        version.canonical_hash = (
            hashlib.sha256(version.step_manifest_json.encode()).hexdigest()
            if identity_kind == 'legacy_step_manifest_sha256' else
            hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(manifest).encode()).hexdigest()
            if identity_kind == 'canonical_manifest_sha256' else 'f' * 64)
        db.commit()
        response = client.post('/api/content-ingress/events', headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
                              json=_payload(event_id='historical-identity', asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()['routing_status'] == 'awaiting_batch', response.text
        package = db.get(MaterialPackage, response.json()['material_package_id'])
        source = json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json)
        event = db.scalar(select(ContentIngressEvent))
        request = ProductionRunRequest(workflow_version_id=version.id,
            idempotency_key=_bounded_idempotency_key(event.event_id, source['snapshot_hash']),
            category_key='space_image', queue_class='production_batch',
            runtime_context={'run_mode': 'incremental', 'material_package_id': package.id,
                             'incremental_workflow_identity': {'identity_kind': 'caller-forged'}},
            input_manifest=source['input_manifest'], owner='synthetic-identity-test',
            source_type='content_ingress_event', source_id=event.id, model_execution_mode='real')
        yield db, version, source, request, package
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('identity_kind', ['canonical_manifest_sha256', 'legacy_step_manifest_sha256'])
def test_verified_historical_identity_allows_full_frozen_sql_connector_manifest(monkeypatch, identity_kind):
    with packaged_request(monkeypatch, identity_kind) as (db, version, source, request, _):
        original_hash = version.canonical_hash
        persisted = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
        frozen = source['workflow_snapshot']['workflow']['manifest']
        assert persisted != frozen, 'fixture must exercise legitimate contract enrichment'
        for node in frozen['nodes']:
            if node['kind'] in {'upstream_connector', 'downstream_projection'}:
                assert node['config']['connector_snapshot']['adapter_key'] == 'sql-envelope-v1'
        run = create_production_run(db, request, actor='synthetic')
        runtime = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)['runtime_context']
        assert runtime['incremental_workflow_identity'] == {
            'identity_kind': identity_kind, 'saved_hash': original_hash,
            'persisted_manifest_hash': hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(persisted).encode()).hexdigest(),
            'frozen_manifest_hash': hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(frozen).encode()).hexdigest(),
        }
        assert version.canonical_hash == original_hash


@pytest.mark.parametrize('tamper', ['nodes', 'edges', 'resource_policy', 'package_digest'])
def test_historical_identity_still_rejects_complete_manifest_drift(monkeypatch, tamper):
    with packaged_request(monkeypatch, 'legacy_step_manifest_sha256') as (db, version, source, request, package):
        if tamper == 'nodes':
            nodes = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
            next(node for node in nodes if node['kind'] == 'prompt')['config']['max_tokens'] += 1
            version.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
        elif tamper == 'edges':
            edges = json.loads(version.edge_manifest_json)
            edges.pop()
            version.edge_manifest_json = canonical_json(edges)
        elif tamper == 'resource_policy':
            policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
            policy['max_parallel'] = 17
            version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        else:
            from sqlalchemy.orm.attributes import set_committed_value
            source['workflow_snapshot']['workflow']['manifest']['resource_policy']['max_parallel'] = 17
            # Simulate corruption of a loaded row without disabling the database
            # immutability trigger or altering the persisted source package.
            set_committed_value(package, 'mechanism_snapshot_json', canonical_json(source))
        db.flush()
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as error:
            create_production_run(db, request, actor='synthetic')
        assert error.value.code == 'incremental_snapshot_mismatch'
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)) is None


def test_unverifiable_saved_identity_is_rejected_even_when_source_echoes_it(monkeypatch):
    with packaged_request(monkeypatch, 'invalid') as (db, _, _, request, _):
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as error:
            create_production_run(db, request, actor='synthetic')
        assert error.value.code == 'incremental_snapshot_mismatch'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_home_performance.py =====
"""Incremental home stays usable while its ledger rollup loads separately."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.incremental_mechanisms_api import build_incremental_mechanisms_router
from app.incremental_observability import cost_summary
from app.models import ProductionRun, WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowVersion
from test_incremental_automation import _operator
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _close, _fixture
from test_incremental_evidence import evidence_db


def test_status_cards_do_not_wait_for_costs_or_change_controls(monkeypatch):
    import app.incremental_observability as observability
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        client = _operator(db)
        full = client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards").json()
        assert full["items"] and all(c["costs"] is not None for c in full["items"])
        def unavailable(*args, **kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("ledger unavailable")
        monkeypatch.setattr(observability, "cost_summary", unavailable)
        status = client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards?include_costs=false")
        assert status.status_code == 200
        value = status.json()
        assert value["can_control"] == full["can_control"]
        assert value["window_hours"] == full["window_hours"]
        for before, after in zip(full["items"], value["items"], strict=True):
            assert after == {**before, "costs": None}
        # Default contract still includes real costs; no invented zeroes on failure.
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="ledger unavailable"):
            client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards")
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_cost_endpoint_matches_full_cards_and_validates_input():
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        client = _operator(db)
        card = client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards").json()["items"][0]
        path = f'/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/{card["category_key"]}/{card["workflow_definition_id"]}/costs'
        response = client.get(path)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.json()["costs"] == card["costs"]
        assert datetime.fromisoformat(response.json()["updated_at"]).tzinfo is not None
        assert client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/0/costs").status_code == 422
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

def test_ledger_grouping_keeps_frozen_route_time_scope_and_unresolved_costs(evidence_db, monkeypatch):
    import app.incremental_observability as observability
    db, version = evidence_db
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 24, 8, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    class Frozen(datetime):
        @classmethod
        def now(cls, tz=None):
            return now
    monkeypatch.setattr(observability, "datetime", Frozen)
    def seed(key, route, *, age=1, category="space_image", source="content_ingress_event", statuses=("succeeded",)):
        runtime = {} if route == "missing" else {"incremental_route": {"destination": route}}
        run = ProductionRun(run_key=key, idempotency_key=key,
            workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id, workflow_version_id=version.id,
            snapshot_json=json.dumps({"runtime_context": runtime, "frozen_manifest": "x" * 100000}),
            snapshot_hash="a"*64, category_key=category, source_type=source, source_id=1,
            requested_by="test", owner="test", status="succeeded", created_at=now-timedelta(days=2))
        db.add(run); db.flush()
        for index, status in enumerate(statuses):
            unknown = status == "unknown"
            db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id, step_attempt_id=1,
                invocation_key=f"{key}-{index}", node_instance_id="score", provider="test", model_id="test",
                status="failed" if unknown else status,
                error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown" if unknown else "",
                actual_cost_micros=0 if unknown or status=="reserved" else 10,
                estimated_cost_micros=20, input_tokens=100, output_tokens=5, cached_input_tokens=25,
                endpoint_id="endpoint" if route=="formal" else None,
                created_at=now-timedelta(hours=age)))
        db.flush()
    seed("formal", "formal", statuses=("succeeded","succeeded","reserved","unknown"))
    seed("shadow", "shadow", age=24)
    seed("missing", "missing")
    seed("null", None)
    seed("literal-null", "null")
    seed("old", "formal", age=25)
    seed("other-category", "formal", category="model_3d_su")
    seed("stock-source", "formal", source="baseline_regression")
    result = cost_summary(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id)
    destinations = result["by_destination"]
    assert destinations["formal"] == dict(calls=4, settled_micros=20, reserved_estimate_micros=40,
        unresolved_calls=2, input_tokens=400, output_tokens=20, cached_input_tokens=100)
    assert destinations["shadow"]["calls"] == 1
    assert destinations["legacy"]["calls"] == 2
    assert destinations["null"]["calls"] == 1
    assert result["by_endpoint"]["endpoint"]["calls"] == 4
    assert result["by_endpoint"]["direct"]["calls"] == 4
    assert result["cache"] == dict(input_tokens=800, cached_input_tokens=200, hit_ratio=0.25)
    assert result["invoice_reconciled"] is False


def test_recent_preview_preserves_lane_scope_and_separate_latest_timestamp(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_automation import automation_cards
    db, version = evidence_db
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)
    for i in range(12):
        # Highest timestamp deliberately belongs to the earliest ID: last_run_at
        # must not silently become the timestamp of the highest run ID.
        db.add(ProductionRun(run_key=f"preview-{i}", idempotency_key=f"preview-{i}",
            workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id, workflow_version_id=version.id,
            snapshot_json="{}", snapshot_hash="a" * 64, category_key="space_image",
            source_type="content_ingress_event", source_id=i, requested_by="test", owner="test",
            status="succeeded", created_at=now - timedelta(minutes=i)))
    for source, category in (("baseline_regression", "space_image"), ("content_ingress_event", "model_3d_su")):
        key = f"excluded-{source}-{category}"
        db.add(ProductionRun(run_key=key, idempotency_key=key,
            workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id, workflow_version_id=version.id,
            snapshot_json="{}", snapshot_hash="b" * 64, category_key=category,
            source_type=source, source_id=50, requested_by="test", owner="test",
            status="succeeded", created_at=now + timedelta(hours=1)))
    db.flush()
    card = next(c for c in automation_cards(db, include_costs=False)["items"]
        if c["workflow_definition_id"] == version.workflow_definition_id and c["category_key"] == "space_image")
    assert [r["run_key"] for r in card["recent_runs"]] == [f"preview-{i}" for i in range(11, 6, -1)]
    assert card["last_run_at"] == now
    assert card["metrics"]["runs"] == 12


def test_drain_union_keeps_unknown_review_and_duplicate_semantics(evidence_db):
    from app.incremental_routing import drain_state
    from app.models import ModelInvocationReview
    db, version = evidence_db
    run = ProductionRun(run_key="drain", idempotency_key="drain",
        workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id, workflow_version_id=version.id,
        snapshot_json="{}", snapshot_hash="a" * 64, category_key="space_image",
        source_type="content_ingress_event", source_id=1, requested_by="test", owner="test", status="succeeded")
    db.add(run); db.flush()
    for i, (status, unknown, reviewed) in enumerate((
        ("reserved", True, False), ("failed", True, True),
        ("failed", True, False), ("reserved", True, True), ("succeeded", False, False),
    )):
        call = WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id, step_attempt_id=1,
            invocation_key=f"drain-{i}", node_instance_id="score", provider="test", model_id="test",
            status=status, error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown" if unknown else "")
        db.add(call); db.flush()
        if reviewed:
            db.add(ModelInvocationReview(invocation_id=call.id, invocation_fingerprint="a" * 64,
                evidence_reference="test", reason="test", actor="test"))
    db.flush()
    value = drain_state(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id)
    assert value["provider_unresolved"] == 3
    assert value["provider_reviewed_billing_pending"] == 2
    assert not value["drained"]


===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_ingress_lock_order.py =====
from sqlalchemy import event
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close, _headers, _payload


def test_ingress_locks_workflow_before_writing_content_record(monkeypatch):
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    statements = []
    def trace(connection, cursor, statement, parameters, context, many):
        statements.append(statement.lower())
    event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", trace)
    try:
        result = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
            json=_payload(event_id="ingress-lock-order", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert result.status_code == 200, result.text
        workflow_lock = next(i for i, statement in enumerate(statements) if statement.startswith("update workflow_definitions"))
        source_write = next(i for i, statement in enumerate(statements) if statement.startswith("insert into content_records"))
        assert workflow_lock < source_write, "Ingress and delivery must both lock workflow before content record"
    finally:
        event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", trace)
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_isolated_driver_20260909.py =====
"""Isolated worker credential/budget boundaries use persisted SQLite evidence."""
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from app.models import ModelConfig

PATH=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]/'scripts/incremental-isolated-driver-20260909.py'
def module():
    assert PATH.exists(), 'isolated incremental driver missing'
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('isolated_driver',PATH)
    mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod

def seed_fixture_anchor_metadata(db):
    from app.models import Asset
    from app.inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import ANCHORS
    for a in ANCHORS:
        db.add(Asset(id=a['asset_id'],original_name=a['stored_name'],stored_name=a['stored_name'],
            mime_type=a['mime_type'],size_bytes=1,sha256=a['sha256'],category_key='space_image'))

def test_memory_credential_never_persists_during_unrelated_commit(tmp_path):
    mod=module()
    engine=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'db.sqlite'))
    ModelConfig.__table__.create(engine)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(ModelConfig.__table__.insert().values(id=1,name='test',encrypted_api_key=None))
    factory=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    mod.install_memory_credentials(factory, lambda identity: 'test-only-secret-reference')
    with factory() as db:
        model=db.get(ModelConfig,1)
        assert model.encrypted_api_key=='test-only-secret-reference'
        model.name='changed'
        db.commit()
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        assert conn.execute(select(ModelConfig.encrypted_api_key)).scalar_one() is None
    with factory() as db:
        db.get(ModelConfig,1).encrypted_api_key='must-not-persist'
        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='credential'):
            db.commit()
    engine.dispose()

def test_reserved_cost_includes_failed_and_unknown_runs():
    mod=module()
    rows=[{'real_execution_budget':{'max_cost_micros':21_000_000}},
          {'real_execution_budget':{'max_cost_micros':20_000_000}}]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='budget'):
        mod.require_budget(rows,cap_micros=40_000_000)
    assert mod.require_budget(rows[:1],cap_micros=40_000_000)==21_000_000
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='budget'):
        mod.require_budget([{}],cap_micros=40_000_000)

def test_metadata_import_copies_frozen_graph_without_secrets_or_runtime_rows():
    mod=module()
    from app.database import Base
    from app.models import WorkflowVersion, ProductionRun, ScriptVersion, Asset, CategoryEvaluationV3Config
    from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions
    source=create_engine('sqlite://')
    target=create_engine('sqlite://')
    Base.metadata.create_all(source);Base.metadata.create_all(target)
    with Session(source) as db:
        version=seed_incremental_versions(db,count=1)[0]
        seed_fixture_anchor_metadata(db)
        version_id=version.id;original_hash=version.canonical_hash
        import json
        cfg=db.scalar(select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config))
        contract=json.loads(cfg.contract_json)
        contract['anchor_fixture']={'asset_id':99}
        cfg.contract_json=json.dumps(contract)
        db.add(Asset(id=99,original_name='anchor.jpg',stored_name='anchor.jpg',mime_type='image/jpeg',size_bytes=1,sha256='a'*64,category_key='space_image'))
        model=db.scalar(select(ModelConfig));model.encrypted_api_key='never-copy-this'
        db.commit()
    with source.connect() as src, target.begin() as dst:
        receipt=mod.copy_execution_metadata(src,dst,version_ids=[version_id],asset_ids=[])
    with Session(target) as db:
        assert db.get(WorkflowVersion,version_id).canonical_hash==original_hash
        assert db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).encrypted_api_key is None
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)) is None
        assert db.get(Asset,99) is not None
        assert db.get(Asset,99).sha256 == 'a'*64
        assert db.scalar(select(ScriptVersion)) is not None
    assert receipt['secret_columns_copied']==0
    source.dispose();target.dispose()

@pytest.mark.parametrize('source_category', ['space_image', 'historical_category'])
def test_sql_payload_preserves_material_type_and_duplicate_ingress(tmp_path, monkeypatch, source_category):
    mod=module()
    import json
    import socket
    from app.database import Base
    from app.models import Asset, WorkflowVersion, ContentIngressEvent
    from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions
    def deny(*args,**kwargs):raise AssertionError('network forbidden')
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket,'connect',deny)
    source=create_engine('sqlite://')
    Base.metadata.create_all(source)
    with Session(source) as db:
        v=seed_incremental_versions(db,count=1)[0]
        seed_fixture_anchor_metadata(db)
        from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config
        from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import split_legacy_revision
        active=db.scalar(select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config))
        slices=split_legacy_revision({'category_key':'space_image','revision':1,'contract_hash':'c'*64,
            'contract':json.loads(active.contract_json),'classification_map':json.loads(active.classification_map_json),
            'subcategory_dimensions':json.loads(active.subcategory_dimensions_json)})
        policy=json.loads(v.resource_policy_json)
        policy['legacy_projection_snapshot']={'schema_version':'legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1','available':True,
            'workflow_fields':slices.workflow_fields,'presence':sorted(slices.presence)}
        v.resource_policy_json=json.dumps(policy)
        nodes=json.loads(v.step_manifest_json)
        for node in nodes:
            if node['kind']=='prompt':
                schema={'type':'object','properties':{'score':{'type':'integer','minimum':0,'maximum':100}},'required':['score'],'additionalProperties':False}
                node['config']['output_contract']={'mode':'json_schema','strict':True,'schema':schema};node['output_schema']=schema
        v.step_manifest_json=json.dumps(nodes)
        db.add(Asset(id=10,original_name='x.jpg',stored_name='x.jpg',mime_type='image/jpeg',size_bytes=1,sha256='f'*64,category_key=source_category))
        db.commit()
        plan={'groups':[{'category_key':'space_image','workflow_version_id':v.id,'workflow_hash':v.canonical_hash,'samples':[{'item_id':1,'asset_id':10,'workflow_input':{'material_type':'全景图'}}]}]}
    receipt=mod.create_isolated(source,tmp_path/'isolated',plan,execution_mode='simulated')
    assert receipt['real_execution_performed'] is False
    with Session(source) as db:
        assert db.get(Asset,10).category_key == source_category
        assert db.get(Asset,10).sha256 == 'f'*64
    result=mod.poll_isolated(tmp_path/'isolated',execution_mode='simulated')
    assert result['source_rows']==1
    result=mod.poll_isolated(tmp_path/'isolated',execution_mode='simulated')
    assert result['source_rows']==0
    local=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'isolated/platform.sqlite'))
    with Session(local) as db:
        event=db.scalar(select(ContentIngressEvent))
        assert json.loads(event.payload_json)['material_type']=='全景图'
        assert len(db.scalars(select(ContentIngressEvent)).all())==1
    result=mod.drain_isolated(tmp_path/'isolated',execution_mode='simulated')
    assert result['run_statuses']=={'succeeded':1}
    assert result['outbox_statuses']=={'delivered':1}
    assert result['downstream_rows']==1
    assert result['model_calls']==[]
    upstream=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'isolated/upstream.sqlite'))
    with upstream.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO changes_0 SELECT 2,'second-item',source_version,category_key,occurred_at,event_type,asset_id,source_payload FROM changes_0 WHERE seq=1")
    mod.poll_isolated(tmp_path/'isolated',execution_mode='simulated')
    mod.stop_isolated(tmp_path/'isolated',reason='supervisor_error')
    stopped=json.loads((tmp_path/'isolated/stopped.json').read_text())
    assert stopped['automatic_resume_forbidden'] is True
    assert mod.isolated_status(tmp_path/'isolated')['run_statuses']=={'succeeded':1,'canceled':1}
    upstream.dispose();source.dispose();local.dispose()

def test_sibling_subsets_share_one_nonrefundable_40_yuan_reservation(tmp_path):
    mod=module()
    mod.reserve_shared_budget(tmp_path/'subset-a',[('run-a',21_000_000)])
    mod.reserve_shared_budget(tmp_path/'subset-a',[('run-a',21_000_000)])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='budget'):
        mod.reserve_shared_budget(tmp_path/'subset-b',[('run-b',20_000_000)])


def test_supervisor_terminates_only_owned_child_after_wall_deadline(tmp_path):

    import sys,time
    before=time.monotonic()
    result=mod.supervise_worker([sys.executable,'-c','import time; time.sleep(30)'],

    assert result['timed_out'] is True
    assert time.monotonic()-before<5

def test_status_callback_error_stops_child_and_returns_cleanup_outcome():
    mod=module()
    import sys,time
    def broken_status():raise OSError('read-only status unavailable')
    before=time.monotonic()
    result=mod.supervise_worker([sys.executable,'-c','import time; time.sleep(30)'],
        deadline_seconds=5,tick_interval_seconds=0.01,on_tick=broken_status)
    assert result['supervisor_error'] is True
    assert result['worker_returncode'] is not None
    assert time.monotonic()-before<5


def test_source_connection_enforces_readonly_transaction(tmp_path):
    mod=module()
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
    engine=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'source.sqlite'))
    with engine.begin() as conn:conn.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE evidence (id INTEGER)')
    with mod.readonly_source_connection(engine) as conn:
        assert conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM evidence').scalar_one()==0
        with pytest.raises(OperationalError,match='readonly'):
            conn.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO evidence VALUES (1)')
    engine.dispose()

def test_batch_gate_rejects_missing_online_and_budget_drift():
    mod=module()
    route={'schema_version':'inference-route-v1','mode':'batch','endpoint_id':'ep-bi-20260909000000-test','foundation_model':'doubao-seed-2-0-lite','endpoint_revision':1}
    budget={'max_cost_micros':10};limits={'prompt_a':{'max_cost_micros':10}}
    context={'inference_route':route,'real_execution_budget':budget,'real_execution_node_limits':limits}
    mod.validate_batch_context(context,route,budget,limits)
    for changed in ({**context,'inference_route':None},
                    {**context,'inference_route':{**route,'mode':'online'}},
                    {**context,'real_execution_budget':{'max_cost_micros':1}}):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='batch'):
            mod.validate_batch_context(changed,route,budget,limits)


def test_prepare_batch_route_refuses_online_only_definition():
    mod=module()
    from app.database import Base
    from app.models import WorkflowDefinition,WorkflowVersion
    from app.doubao_endpoint_models import DoubaoInferenceEndpoint
    from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions
    engine=create_engine('sqlite://');Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version=seed_incremental_versions(db,count=1)[0]
        seed_fixture_anchor_metadata(db)
        db.add(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(id=1,endpoint_id='ep-20260909000000-test',display_name='online',category_key='space_image',foundation_model='doubao-seed-2-0-lite'))
        definition=db.get(WorkflowDefinition,version.workflow_definition_id);definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id=1
        db.flush()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='batch'):
            mod.prepare_batch_routes(db,[version.id])
        db.add(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(id=2,endpoint_id='ep-bi-20260909000000-test',display_name='batch',category_key='space_image',foundation_model='doubao-seed-2-0-lite'))
        definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id=2;db.flush()
        routes=mod.prepare_batch_routes(db,[version.id])
        assert routes[str(version.workflow_definition_id)]['endpoint_id']=='ep-bi-20260909000000-test'
    engine.dispose()


def _stub_drain_environment(mod, monkeypatch, active, deliver):
    from contextlib import contextmanager
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import app.outbox_delivery_tick as delivery
    class Factory:
        kw = {'bind': SimpleNamespace(dispose=lambda: None)}
        @contextmanager
        def __call__(self):
            yield SimpleNamespace(commit=lambda: None, scalar=lambda query: 1 if active() else None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod, '_local_factory', lambda *a, **kw: Factory())
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod, 'isolated_status', lambda root: {'run_statuses': {'queued': 1} if active() else {'succeeded': 1}})
    monkeypatch.setattr(delivery, 'deliver_due_outbox_events', lambda *a, **kw: deliver())


def test_slow_probe_call_does_not_block_other_worker_or_delivery(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import threading
    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    mod = module()
    release = threading.Event(); fast_done = threading.Event(); delivered = threading.Event()
    fast_steps = []
    def step(identity, **kwargs):
        if identity.endswith('-0'):
            assert release.wait(5)
            return False
        fast_steps.append(1)
        if len(fast_steps) >= 2:
            fast_done.set()
        return len(fast_steps) <= 2
    _stub_drain_environment(mod, monkeypatch, lambda: False,
        lambda: delivered.set() if fast_done.is_set() else None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, 'process_runtime_step_once', step)
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as runner:
        future = runner.submit(mod.drain_isolated, tmp_path, max_rounds=10)
        try:
            assert fast_done.wait(1), 'fast worker is blocked behind the slow peer'
            assert delivered.wait(1), 'outbox waits until every model call finishes'
        finally:
            release.set()
        future.result(timeout=5)


def test_probe_idle_tick_waits_for_retryable_work(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import threading
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    mod = module(); finished = threading.Event(); lock = threading.Lock(); calls = []
    def step(identity, **kwargs):
        with lock:
            calls.append(identity)
            if len(calls) == 3:
                finished.set()
                return True
            return False
    _stub_drain_environment(mod, monkeypatch, lambda: not finished.is_set(), lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, 'process_runtime_step_once', step)
    result = mod.drain_isolated(tmp_path, max_rounds=10)
    assert finished.is_set(), 'temporary no-work result discarded pending retry'
    assert result['run_statuses'] == {'succeeded': 1}

def test_unified_budget_preserves_external_and_old_reservations(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    mod=module()
    root=tmp_path/'new-wave'
    mod.reserve_shared_budget(tmp_path/'old-wave',[('old',22_878_288)])
    import sqlite3,hashlib,json
    with sqlite3.connect(tmp_path/'incremental-shared-allocation.sqlite') as conn:
        rows=conn.execute('select identity,cost from reservations order by identity').fetchall()
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod,'_LEGACY_RESERVATION_DIGEST',hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(rows,separators=(',',':')).encode()).hexdigest())
    mod.authorize_unified_budget(root, external_reservations={
        'baseline:316':16_169_520,'baseline:318':16_169_520,'baseline:321':16_169_520})
    assert mod.shared_budget_cap(root)==200_000_000
    assert mod.reserve_shared_budget(root,[('new',100_000_000)])==171_386_848
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='budget'):
        mod.reserve_shared_budget(root,[('overflow',30_000_000)])
    assert mod.reserve_shared_budget(root,[('new',100_000_000)])==171_386_848
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='changed'):
        mod.authorize_unified_budget(root,external_reservations={'baseline:316':1})
