def test_async_probe_consumers_share_bounded_runtime_and_deliver(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    import asyncio
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    mod=module();active=set();seen=[];delivered=[];barrier=None
    async def step(identity,*,runtime,global_limit):
        nonlocal barrier
        if identity in seen:return False
        seen.append(identity);active.add(identity)
        assert global_limit==4
        if barrier is None:barrier=asyncio.Event()
        if len(seen)==4:barrier.set()
        await asyncio.wait_for(barrier.wait(),1)
        active.remove(identity)
        return True
    _stub_drain_environment(mod,monkeypatch,lambda:bool(active),lambda:delivered.append(True))
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker,'process_runtime_step_async',step)
    mod.drain_isolated(tmp_path,max_rounds=10,async_slots=4)
    assert len(seen)==4 and delivered

@pytest.mark.parametrize('slots',[-1,25,4096,True])
def test_async_probe_rejects_unvalidated_slots_before_database_access(tmp_path,monkeypatch,slots):
    mod=module()
    def forbidden(*a,**kw):raise AssertionError('database accessed before limit validation')
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod,'_local_factory',forbidden)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='0..24'):
        mod.drain_isolated(tmp_path,async_slots=slots)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_level_override_replay.py =====
"""人工优先：列表改过的等级不能被随后的字段纠偏重放冲掉（复审 09-26 第 8 条）。

列表改等级只写人工覆盖（manual_final_level / human_override），随后在详情改一个
字段会走 append_node_correction_and_replay：DAG 重新归并出模型等级，旧代码把
level/scoring_json 覆盖回模型等级，并按模型等级带 human_correction 重投下游。
"""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.connector_delivery import DownstreamSafetyEvidence, SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter
from app.database import Base
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    EvaluationResult,
    ProductionRun,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
)
from app.readonly_sources import SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter, create_upstream_source_contract
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions
from test_readonly_sources import _contract_args

CATEGORY = "space_image"


@pytest.fixture
def pipeline(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import app.incremental_materials as materials
    import app.outbox_delivery_tick as delivery_tick
    import app.upstream_poll_tick as source_tick
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import split_legacy_revision
    from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches
    from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'platform.sqlite'}",
                           connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 30})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        run_migrations(conn)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    upstream = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'upstream.sqlite'}")
    downstream = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'downstream.sqlite'}")
    with upstream.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE changes (seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, content_id TEXT, source_version TEXT, category_key TEXT, occurred_at TEXT, event_type TEXT, asset_id INTEGER)")
    with downstream.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE results (business_idempotency_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL, payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL, external_record_key TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, review_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto', published_at DATETIME)")
    with sessions() as db:
        version = seed_incremental_versions(db, category_key=CATEGORY, count=1)[0]
        nodes = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
        for node in nodes:
            if node["kind"] == "prompt":
                schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {"score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100}},
                          "required": ["score"], "additionalProperties": False}
                node["config"]["output_contract"] = {"mode": "json_schema", "strict": True, "schema": schema}
                node["output_schema"] = schema
        active = db.scalar(select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == CATEGORY))
        slices = split_legacy_revision({"category_key": CATEGORY, "revision": 1, "contract_hash": "c" * 64,
                                        "contract": json.loads(active.contract_json),
                                        "classification_map": json.loads(active.classification_map_json),
                                        "subcategory_dimensions": json.loads(active.subcategory_dimensions_json)})
        policy_json = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy_json["legacy_projection_snapshot"] = {"schema_version": "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1", "available": True,
                                                     "workflow_fields": slices.workflow_fields, "presence": sorted(slices.presence)}
        version.resource_policy_json = json.dumps(policy_json)
        version.step_manifest_json = json.dumps(nodes)
        Image.new("RGB", (32, 32), "white").save(tmp_path / "local.jpg")
        raw = (tmp_path / "local.jpg").read_bytes()
        definition_id = version.workflow_definition_id
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key=CATEGORY, workflow_version_id=version.id, expected_lock_version=None, reason="t", actor="t")
        policy = configure_policy(db, category_key=CATEGORY, workflow_definition_id=definition_id, expected_revision=0, mode="split",
                                  ratio_percent=100, ownership_contract="isolated-local-tables-only", reason="t", actor="t")
        set_admission(db, category_key=CATEGORY, workflow_definition_id=definition_id, expected_revision=policy["revision"], paused=False, reason="t", actor="t")
        asset = Asset(original_name="local.jpg", stored_name="local.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=len(raw),
                      sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), category_key=CATEGORY)
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        asset_id = asset.id
        mapping = {x: x for x in ("content_id", "source_version", "category_key", "occurred_at", "event_type", "asset_id")} | {"change_cursor": "seq"}
        create_upstream_source_contract(db, **_contract_args(category_key=CATEGORY, field_mappings=mapping,
            cursor_definition={"mode": "change", "field": "change_cursor", "failure_policy": "isolate"}, poll_interval_seconds=1, page_size=5))
        fingerprint = db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction == "downstream")).schema_fingerprint
        db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def readonly():
        with upstream.connect() as conn:
            conn.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
            yield conn

    source = SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter(connection_factory=readonly, table_name="changes", field_mappings=mapping, schema_fingerprint="f" * 64,
                                      cursor_definition={"mode": "change", "field": "change_cursor", "failure_policy": "isolate"})
    sink = SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(connection_factory=downstream.begin, table_name="results",
                                        evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(True, fingerprint, "isolated-sqlite-test"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(source_tick, "resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter", lambda _: source)
    monkeypatch.setattr(delivery_tick, "resolve_configured_downstream_adapter", lambda _: sink)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    monkeypatch.setattr(materials, "_storage_dir", lambda: tmp_path)
    stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    def tick():
        with sessions() as db:
            source_tick.poll_due_upstream_sources(db, actor="t", now=stamp + timedelta(hours=1))
            dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="t", now=stamp + timedelta(hours=1))
            db.commit()
        for _ in range(200):
            if not executor.process_runtime_step_once("override-worker"):
                break
        with sessions() as db:
            delivery_tick.deliver_due_outbox_events(db, worker_id="override")
            db.commit()

    with upstream.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO changes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", (1, "1", "v1", CATEGORY, stamp.isoformat(), "content.created", asset_id))
    tick()
    yield sessions, downstream, tick
    engine.dispose()
    upstream.dispose()
    downstream.dispose()


def _downstream_row(downstream):
    with downstream.connect() as conn:
        row = conn.exec_driver_sql("SELECT * FROM results").mappings().one()
    return dict(row), json.loads(row["payload_json"])


def _latest_projection(db):
    event = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).order_by(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id.desc()))
    return event, load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob)


def _find(value, key):
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        if key in value:
            return value[key]
        for child in value.values():
            found = _find(child, key)
            if found is not None:
                return found
    return None


def _correct_level(sessions):
    from app.incremental_correction import correct_incremental_level, selectable_levels

    with sessions() as db:
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run.status == "succeeded"
        result = db.scalar(select(EvaluationResult).where(EvaluationResult.production_run_id == run.id))
        model_level = result.level
        manual = next(level for level in selectable_levels(run) if level != model_level)
        correct_incremental_level(db, evaluation_id=result.id, level=manual, expected_level=model_level,
                                  reason="运营改等级", actor="运营甲", idempotency_key="lvl-1")
        db.commit()
        return run.id, result.id, model_level, manual


def test_field_correction_after_list_level_keeps_the_human_level(pipeline):
    sessions, downstream, tick = pipeline
    run_id, evaluation_id, model_level, manual = _correct_level(sessions)
    tick()
    from app.incremental_correction import correct_incremental_field

    with sessions() as db:
        correct_incremental_field(db, evaluation_id=evaluation_id, node_instance_id="prompt_b", field_path="score",
                                  value=90, reason="运营改分项", actor="运营乙", idempotency_key="field-1")
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        result = db.get(EvaluationResult, evaluation_id)
        assert result.level == manual
        scoring = json.loads(result.scoring_json)
        assert scoring["level"] == manual and scoring["manual_final_level"] == manual
        assert json.loads(result.normalized_result_json)["level"] == manual
        event, payload = _latest_projection(db)
        assert event.review_state == "reviewed"
        assert _find(payload, "final_level") == manual
        override = _find(payload, "human_override")
        assert override["level"] == manual and override["actor"] == "运营甲"
    tick()
    row, delivered = _downstream_row(downstream)
    assert row["review_state"] == "reviewed" and row["revision"] == 3
    assert _find(delivered, "final_level") == manual


def test_a_later_level_field_correction_supersedes_the_list_override():
    # 本次纠偏本身就是改等级时，由它取代先前的列表覆盖；之后的字段纠偏不再复活旧覆盖。
    from app.incremental_correction import active_level_override

    history = [
        {"node_type": "final_level", "new_value": "L2", "old_value": "L4", "corrector": "甲",
         "reason": "r", "correction_key": "k1"},
    ]
    override = active_level_override(history, {}, [])
    assert override["level"] == "L2" and override["previous_level"] == "L4"
    history.append({"node_type": "workflow_node", "node_path": "level", "level_override_superseded": True})
    assert active_level_override(history, {}, []) is None
    history.append({"node_type": "final_level", "new_value": "L1", "old_value": "L3", "corrector": "乙",
                    "reason": "r2", "correction_key": "k2"})
    assert active_level_override(history, {}, [])["level"] == "L1"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_local_simulation.py =====
"""Zero-network, persisted source -> native scheduler -> SQL sink acceptance."""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
from PIL import Image
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app.database import Base
from app.models import Asset, ProductionRun, RuntimeOutboxEvent, UpstreamReadRun, ConnectorContractVersion, ContentRecord, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
from app.readonly_sources import create_upstream_source_contract, SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter
from app.connector_delivery import SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter, DownstreamSafetyEvidence
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions
from test_readonly_sources import _contract_args


@pytest.mark.parametrize('category,invalid_contract', [('space_image',False), ('knowledge_graph',False), ('inspiration_image',False), ('model_3d_su',False), pytest.param('inspiration_image',True,id='invalid-contract-rejected')])
def test_persisted_zero_cost_incremental_pipeline(tmp_path, monkeypatch, category, invalid_contract):
    blocked_connections = []
    def no_network(*args, **kwargs):
        blocked_connections.append(True)
        raise AssertionError('external network forbidden in zero-cost simulation')
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket, 'connect', no_network)
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket, 'create_connection', no_network)
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    import app.upstream_poll_tick as source_tick
    import app.outbox_delivery_tick as delivery_tick
    from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches

    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "platform.sqlite"}', connect_args={'check_same_thread':False, 'timeout':30})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as conn: run_migrations(conn)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    upstream = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "upstream.sqlite"}')
    upstream_reader = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "upstream.sqlite"}')
    downstream = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "downstream.sqlite"}')
    with upstream.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE changes (seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, content_id TEXT, source_version TEXT, category_key TEXT, occurred_at TEXT, event_type TEXT, asset_id INTEGER)')
    with downstream.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE results (business_idempotency_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL, payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL, external_record_key TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, review_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto', published_at DATETIME)")
    with sessions() as db:
        version = seed_incremental_versions(db, category_key=category, count=1)[0]
        nodes=json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
        for node in nodes:
            if node['kind']=='prompt':
                schema={'type':'object','properties':{'score':{'type':'integer','minimum':0,'maximum':100}},'required':['score'],'additionalProperties':False}
                if category=='inspiration_image' and node['node_instance_id']=='prompt_b':
                    # 灵感图收尾对调用 B 严格：只认基座八维或「aesthetic_score + dimensions」最低形状，
                    # 两样都不像就 fail-closed（8d0ab469）。模拟也得给出合法的 B 形状，而不是只有 score。
                    # 美感分还必须带非空可见证据（b_aesthetic_foundation），所以给 aesthetic_evidence 至少一条（纯基础分形状读这个字段）。
                    schema={'type':'object','properties':{'aesthetic_score':{'type':'integer','minimum':0,'maximum':100},
                                                          'dimensions':{'type':'object','properties':{},'required':[],'additionalProperties':False},
                                                          'aesthetic_evidence':{'type':'array','minItems':1,'items':{'type':'string','minLength':1}}},
                            'required':['aesthetic_score','dimensions','aesthetic_evidence'],'additionalProperties':False}
                node['config']['output_contract']={'mode':'json_schema','strict':True,'schema':schema}
                node['output_schema']=schema
        from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config
        from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import split_legacy_revision
        active=db.scalar(select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key==category))
        slices=split_legacy_revision({'category_key':category,'revision':1,'contract_hash':'c'*64,'contract':json.loads(active.contract_json),'classification_map':json.loads(active.classification_map_json),'subcategory_dimensions':json.loads(active.subcategory_dimensions_json)})
        policy_json=json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy_json['legacy_projection_snapshot']={'schema_version':'legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1','available':True,'workflow_fields':slices.workflow_fields,'presence':sorted(slices.presence)}
        if invalid_contract: policy_json.pop('legacy_projection_snapshot')
        version.resource_policy_json=json.dumps(policy_json)
        version.step_manifest_json=json.dumps(nodes)
        Image.new('RGB',(32,32),'white').save(tmp_path/'local.jpg')
        raw=(tmp_path/'local.jpg').read_bytes()
        definition_id = version.workflow_definition_id
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key=category, workflow_version_id=version.id, expected_lock_version=None, reason='zero-cost local simulation', actor='simulation')
        policy = configure_policy(db, category_key=category, workflow_definition_id=definition_id, expected_revision=0, mode='split', ratio_percent=100, ownership_contract='isolated-local-tables-only', reason='simulation', actor='simulation')
        set_admission(db, category_key=category, workflow_definition_id=definition_id, expected_revision=policy['revision'], paused=False, reason='simulation', actor='simulation')
        asset = Asset(original_name='local.jpg', stored_name='local.jpg', mime_type='image/jpeg', size_bytes=len(raw), sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), category_key=category)
        db.add(asset);db.flush();asset_id=asset.id
        mapping={x:x for x in ('content_id','source_version','category_key','occurred_at','event_type','asset_id')} | {'change_cursor':'seq'}
        contract=create_upstream_source_contract(db, **_contract_args(category_key=category,field_mappings=mapping,cursor_definition={'mode':'change','field':'change_cursor','failure_policy':'isolate'},poll_interval_seconds=1,page_size=5))
        target=db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction=='downstream'))
        fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint
        db.commit()
    @contextmanager
    def readonly():
        with upstream_reader.connect() as conn:
            conn.exec_driver_sql('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
            yield conn
    source=SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter(connection_factory=readonly, table_name='changes',field_mappings=mapping,schema_fingerprint='f'*64,cursor_definition={'mode':'change','field':'change_cursor','failure_policy':'isolate'})
    sink=SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(connection_factory=downstream.begin,table_name='results',evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(True,fingerprint,'isolated-sqlite-test'))
    monkeypatch.setattr(source_tick,'resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter',lambda _:source)
    monkeypatch.setattr(delivery_tick,'resolve_configured_downstream_adapter',lambda _:sink)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor,'SessionLocal',sessions)
    import app.incremental_materials as materials
    monkeypatch.setattr(materials,'_storage_dir',lambda:tmp_path)
    stamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    steps=0
    def tick(*, expect_source_error=False, child_worker=False, concurrent=False):
        nonlocal steps
        with sessions() as db:
            summary=source_tick.poll_due_upstream_sources(db,actor='simulation',now=stamp+timedelta(hours=1))
            assert bool(summary['errors'])==expect_source_error,summary
            dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db,actor='simulation',now=stamp+timedelta(hours=1));db.commit()
        if child_worker:
            child_code = """
import os,sys,socket,json
from pathlib import Path
os.environ['DATA_DIR']=str(Path(sys.argv[1]).parent/'child-data')
def deny(*a,**k): raise AssertionError('network forbidden')
socket.socket.connect=deny
socket.create_connection=deny
from sqlalchemy import create_engine,select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app.models import ProductionRun
import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
engine=create_engine('sqlite:///'+sys.argv[1])
worker.SessionLocal=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
with worker.SessionLocal() as db:
 assert all(r.model_execution_mode=='simulated' for r in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)))
count=0
while worker.process_runtime_step_once('fresh-process-simulation'):
 count+=1
 assert count<200
print(json.dumps({'steps':count}))
engine.dispose()
"""
            child=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-c',child_code,str(tmp_path/'platform.sqlite')],cwd=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=60)
            assert child.returncode==0,child.stderr
            steps+=json.loads(child.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])['steps']
        else:
            with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4 if concurrent else 1) as pool:
                for _ in range(200):
                    worked=list(pool.map(executor.process_runtime_step_once,[f'local-simulation-{i}' for i in range(4 if concurrent else 1)]))
                    steps+=sum(worked)
                    if not any(worked):break
                else:raise AssertionError('workflow did not drain within bounded steps')
        with sessions() as db:
            delivery_tick.deliver_due_outbox_events(db,worker_id='local-simulation');db.commit()
    # Repeated arrivals use the same source timestamp: only seq determines order.
    for batch in range(3):
        if category == 'model_3d_su':
            from app.factory_claim_contract import parse_claim_response
            # Synthetic factory response -> offline validated staging table.
            # res_type=6 and ll_id->content_id are fixture choices, NOT a live
            # mapping or evidence that claim is read-only. No image is fetched.
            response={'code':1,'msg':'fixture','request_id':f'fixture-{batch}','data':[
                {'task_id':10000+batch*4+i+1,'res_type':6,'ll_id':batch*4+i+1,
                 'img_id':30000+batch*4+i+1,'class_id_1':10,
                 'image_url':'https://example.com/image.jpg'} for i in range(4)]}
            tasks=parse_claim_response(response,requested_limit=4,allowed_res_types={6})
            assert len(tasks)==4
        with upstream.begin() as conn:
            for i in range(4):
                n=tasks[i].ll_id if category == 'model_3d_su' else batch*4+i+1
                conn.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO changes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(n,str(n),'v1',category,stamp.isoformat(),'content.created',asset_id))
        tick(child_worker=batch==1,concurrent=batch==2)
        if invalid_contract:
            with sessions() as db:
                assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==4
                # The projection now rejects invalid scoring evidence before
                # enqueueing delivery, rather than canceling it afterwards.
                assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent))==0
                assert {r.error_code for r in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun))}=={'native_projection_invalid'}
                assert all(r.status=='failed' for r in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)))
                from app.models import DownstreamPublishLedger
                assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(DownstreamPublishLedger))==0
            with downstream.connect() as conn:
                assert conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM results').scalar_one()==0
            assert not blocked_connections
            print('LOCAL_SIMULATION_REJECTED invalid_contract=4 downstream_rows=0')
            engine.dispose();upstream.dispose();upstream_reader.dispose();downstream.dispose()
            return
        if batch==1:
            engine.dispose()  # New connections must continue persisted source cursor.
    tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        runs=db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all()
        assert len(runs)==12
        assert all(r.status=='succeeded' for r in runs),[(r.status,r.error_code,r.error_message) for r in runs]
        outbox=db.scalars(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)).all()
        assert len(outbox)==12
        assert all(r.status=='delivered' for r in outbox),[(r.status,r.last_error_code) for r in outbox]
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation))==0
        assert all(r.model_execution_mode=='simulated' for r in runs)
        last=db.scalar(select(UpstreamReadRun).order_by(UpstreamReadRun.id.desc()))
        assert json.loads(last.next_cursor_json)=={'change_cursor':12}
    with downstream.connect() as conn:
        assert conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM results').scalar_one()==12
    # Pause admission: durable backlog resumes without replaying source events.
    from app.incremental_routing import current_policy
    from app.models import IncrementalAdmissionBacklog, IncrementalEntityAssignment, IncrementalShadowResult
    scope=dict(category_key=category,workflow_definition_id=definition_id)
    def admission(paused):
        with sessions() as db:
            policy=current_policy(db,**scope)
            set_admission(db,**scope,expected_revision=policy.revision,paused=paused,reason='simulation',actor='simulation');db.commit()
    def arrival(seq, entity, version='v1', event_type='content.created', selected_asset=asset_id):
        with upstream.begin() as conn:
            conn.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO changes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(seq,entity,version,category,stamp.isoformat(),event_type,selected_asset))
    admission(True)
    arrival(13,'paused')
    tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==12
        assert db.scalar(select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog)).status=='pending'
    admission(False);tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==13
    # Missing asset is isolated; later valid rows continue; restoring dependency replays exactly once.
    from app.source_recovery_models import SourceQuarantineRow
    from app.source_recovery import replay_source_row
    arrival(14,'isolated',selected_asset=999)
    arrival(15,'after-bad')
    tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        bad=db.scalar(select(SourceQuarantineRow))
        assert bad.status=='quarantined'
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==14
        (tmp_path/'restored.jpg').write_bytes(raw)
        db.add(Asset(id=999,original_name='restored.jpg',stored_name='restored.jpg',mime_type='image/jpeg',size_bytes=len(raw),sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),category_key=category))
        db.flush()
        replay_source_row(db,row_id=bad.id,source_contract_id=bad.source_contract_id,category_key=category,expected_lock_version=bad.lock_version,reason='dependency restored',actor='simulation')
        assert bad.status=='replayed';db.commit()
    tick()
    # Material arrives after metadata; normal scheduler resumes this same source version.
    arrival(16,'late-material',selected_asset=None);tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        record=db.scalar(select(ContentRecord).where(ContentRecord.source_content_id=='late-material'))
        assert record.status=='awaiting_material'
        job=materials.submit_manifest(db,source_system='fixture-3d',submission_id='late-image',occurred_at=stamp,payload={'content_id':'late-material','content_version':'v1','category_key':category,'material':{'asset_id':asset_id}},actor='simulation');db.commit()
        materials.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='simulation',now=stamp+timedelta(hours=1));db.commit()
        assert job.status=='completed'
    tick()
    # Human correction uses the real correction service and does not pause unrelated work.
    from app.workflow_correction import append_node_correction_and_replay
    with sessions() as db:
        first=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id))
        # 灵感图的 B 段是「aesthetic_score + dimensions」形状，纠的是美感分；其余类目的模拟 B 段只有 score
        b_field='aesthetic_score' if category=='inspiration_image' else 'score'
        result=append_node_correction_and_replay(db,run_id=first.id,node_instance_id='prompt_b',field_path=b_field,value=90,reason='local human correction',actor='simulation')
        assert result.downstream_publish_status=='queued';db.commit()
    arrival(17,'during-correction');tick()
    with downstream.connect() as conn:
        reviewed=conn.exec_driver_sql("SELECT * FROM results WHERE review_state='reviewed'").mappings().all()
        assert len(reviewed)==1 and reviewed[0]['revision']==2
        reviewed_key=reviewed[0]['business_idempotency_key']
    arrival(18,'1',version='v2',event_type='content.updated');tick()
    with downstream.connect() as conn:
        row=conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT * FROM results WHERE business_idempotency_key=?',(reviewed_key,)).mappings().one()
        assert row['review_state']=='auto' and row['revision']==3
    arrival(19,'1',version='v3',event_type='content.deleted');tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(ContentRecord).where(ContentRecord.source_content_id=='1')).status=='deleted'
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==18
        assert current_policy(db,**scope).admission_paused is False
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation))==0
    # Changed ratio affects new entities; established ownership stays stable.
    with sessions() as db:
        policy=current_policy(db,**scope)
        configure_policy(db,**scope,expected_revision=policy.revision,mode='split',ratio_percent=50,ownership_contract='isolated-local-tables-only',reason='gray adjustment',actor='simulation');db.commit()
    for seq in range(20,40): arrival(seq,'gray-'+str(seq))
    for _ in range(5):tick(concurrent=True)
    with sessions() as db:
        assignments=db.scalars(select(IncrementalEntityAssignment).where(IncrementalEntityAssignment.entity_id.like('gray-%'))).all()
        assert len(assignments)==20
        assert {a.destination for a in assignments}=={'formal','old'}
        assert all((a.destination=='formal')==(a.bucket<5000) for a in assignments)
        assert db.scalar(select(IncrementalEntityAssignment).where(IncrementalEntityAssignment.entity_id=='1')).destination=='formal'
    # Source outage keeps its cursor; restoring the adapter consumes the pending event.
    original_fetch=source.fetch_page
    def unavailable(*args,**kwargs):raise OSError('injected source outage')
    monkeypatch.setattr(source,'fetch_page',unavailable)
    arrival(40,'outage-recovery')
    tick(expect_source_error=True)
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(ContentRecord).where(ContentRecord.source_content_id=='outage-recovery')) is None
    monkeypatch.setattr(source,'fetch_page',original_fetch)
    tick(concurrent=True)
    # Force selected ownership for readback retry; sink accepts bytes but drops acknowledgement once.
    with sessions() as db:
        policy=current_policy(db,**scope)
        configure_policy(db,**scope,expected_revision=policy.revision,mode='split',ratio_percent=100,ownership_contract='isolated-local-tables-only',reason='retry scenario',actor='simulation');db.commit()
    original_readback=sink.read_back
    monkeypatch.setattr(sink,'read_back',lambda keys:())
    arrival(41,'readback-recovery');tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        # 读回没确认按投递重试（10-01 事件 71215），不再直接进死信；读回恢复后自动送达，无需人工重放。
        pending=db.scalars(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status=='queued')).all()
        assert len(pending)==1 and pending[0].last_error_code=='downstream_readback_mismatch'
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status=='dead_lettered'))==0
        pending[0].available_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc);db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(sink,'read_back',original_readback);tick()
    original_deliver=sink.deliver
    monkeypatch.setattr(sink,'deliver',unavailable)
    arrival(42,'downstream-outage');tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        queued=db.scalars(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status=='queued')).all()
        assert len(queued)==1
        # Retryable delivery failures now back off (review 09-26) instead of
        # being due again immediately; fast-forward past the backoff.
        assert queued[0].available_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)>stamp
        queued[0].available_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc);db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(sink,'deliver',original_deliver);tick()
    if category == 'model_3d_su':
        # The staging source's existing scheduled-poll switch: this is NOT
        # validation of a future external claim lease or pause fence.
        with sessions() as db:
            db.get(type(contract),contract.id).poll_interval_seconds=None;db.commit()
        arrival(43,'upstream-switch');tick()
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(ContentRecord).where(ContentRecord.source_content_id=='upstream-switch')) is None
            db.get(type(contract),contract.id).poll_interval_seconds=1;db.commit()
        tick()
        from app.incremental_automation import set_automation_control
        with sessions() as db:
            paused=set_automation_control(db,**scope,enabled=False,expected_revision=0,reason='isolated switch test',actor='simulation');db.commit()
            before_runs=db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))
        arrival(44,'execution-switch');tick()
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(ContentRecord).where(ContentRecord.source_content_id=='execution-switch')) is not None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==before_runs
            set_automation_control(db,**scope,enabled=True,expected_revision=paused['revision'],reason='isolated switch resume',actor='simulation');db.commit()
        tick()
        from app.connector_governance import set_connector_pause_state
        target_scope=dict(category_key=category,mechanism_key='',contract_key=target.contract_key)
        with sessions() as db:
            paused=set_connector_pause_state(db,**target_scope,state='paused',expected_revision=None,reason='isolated sink pause',actor='simulation');db.commit()
            pause_revision=paused.revision
        with downstream.connect() as conn:
            before_rows=conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM results').scalar_one()
        arrival(45,'downstream-switch');tick()
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status=='queued'))==1
        with downstream.connect() as conn:
            assert conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM results').scalar_one()==before_rows
        engine.dispose()  # Pause and outbox survive a new connection/session.
        with sessions() as db:
            set_connector_pause_state(db,**target_scope,state='active',expected_revision=pause_revision,reason='isolated sink resume',actor='simulation');db.commit()
        tick();tick()  # Repeated delivery must not duplicate the target row.
        with downstream.connect() as conn:
            assert conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM results').scalar_one()==before_rows+1
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status!='delivered'))==0
        total_runs=db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.status!='succeeded'))==0
        from app.models import EvaluationResult
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(EvaluationResult))==total_runs
    with downstream.connect() as conn:
        total_rows=conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM results').scalar_one()
        assert total_rows==total_runs-1 # v2 overwrites v1's same business key.
    evidence_scenarios=['pause_resume_backlog','bad_row_isolation_replay','late_material_resume','human_correction_continuity','new_version_auto_overwrites_old_review','delete_no_new_evaluation','gray_ratio_new_entities_stable_existing_owner']
    assert not blocked_connections
    evidence={'category':category,'initial_arrivals':12,'initial_completed_runs':12,'initial_delivered_rows':12,'total_source_changes':45 if category=='model_3d_su' else 42,'total_completed_runs':total_runs,'total_downstream_rows':total_rows,'fresh_worker_process_verified':True,'concurrent_workers':4,'source_outage_recovered':True,'readback_dead_letter_replay_idempotent':True,'downstream_outage_auto_retry':True,'scenarios':evidence_scenarios,'steps':steps,'model_calls':0,'external_network_attempts':0,'restart_cursor_verified':True,'evidence_kind':'synthetic','factory_claim_live':False,'independent_switches_verified':category=='model_3d_su'}
    (tmp_path/'evidence.json').write_text(json.dumps(evidence,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
    print('LOCAL_SIMULATION '+json.dumps(evidence,ensure_ascii=False))
    engine.dispose();upstream.dispose();upstream_reader.dispose();downstream.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_materials.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import io
import json

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from pathlib import Path
from app.models import Asset, AuditEvent, ContentIngressEvent, EvaluationCategoryProfile
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def service():
    from app import incremental_materials
    return incremental_materials


@pytest.fixture
def material_env(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON', json.dumps({'fixture': {'allowed_hosts': ['images.example.test'], 'max_bytes': 1024, 'max_images': 4}}))
    engine = create_engine('sqlite:///' + str(tmp_path / 'jobs.sqlite'))
    svc = service()
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    seed_incremental_versions(db, category_key='design_case', count=1)
    db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == 'design_case')).allowed_mime_types_json = '["image/jpeg","image/png"]'
    db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_storage_dir', lambda: tmp_path / 'images')
    yield svc, db
    db.close(); engine.dispose()


def png(color='red'):
    buf = io.BytesIO(); Image.new('RGB', (5, 5), color).save(buf, format='PNG'); return buf.getvalue()


def payload():
    return {'content_id': 'case-1', 'content_version': 'upstream-v7', 'category_key': 'design_case',
            'case': {'id': 'case-1', 'expected_image_ids': ['a', 'b'], 'images': [
                {'cloud_id': 'a', 'url': 'https://images.example.test/a.png'},
                {'cloud_id': 'b', 'url': 'https://images.example.test/b.png'}]}}


def submit(svc, db, body=None, ident='submission-1'):
    return svc.submit_manifest(db, source_system='fixture', submission_id=ident,
        occurred_at=datetime(2026, 9, 6, tzinfo=timezone.utc), payload=body or payload(), actor='test')


def test_material_api_exists():
    svc = service()
    assert callable(svc.submit_manifest)


def test_duplicate_identity_and_immutable_source_version(material_env):
    svc, db = material_env
    first = submit(svc, db); db.commit()
    assert submit(svc, db).id == first.id
    changed = payload(); changed['case']['images'][0]['url'] += '?changed=1'
    with pytest.raises(svc.MaterialError, match='conflict'):
        submit(svc, db, changed)
    with pytest.raises(svc.MaterialError, match='conflict'):
        submit(svc, db, changed, ident='other-submission')


def test_partial_download_survives_restart_then_real_ingress_dispatch(material_env, monkeypatch):
    svc, db = material_env
    calls = []
    def download(url, policy):
        calls.append(url)
        if url.endswith('b.png') and calls.count(url) == 1: raise OSError('temporary')
        return png('red' if url.endswith('a.png') else 'blue')
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', download)
    job = submit(svc, db); db.commit()
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=now); db.commit()
    db.refresh(job)
    assert job.status == 'retry_wait'
    assert len(json.loads(job.resolved_json)) == 1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ContentIngressEvent.id))) == 0
    job_id = job.id; db.close()
    with Session(db.bind, expire_on_commit=False) as restarted:
        svc.process_due_material_jobs(restarted, actor='worker', now=now + timedelta(minutes=1)); restarted.commit()
        done = restarted.get(type(job), job_id)
        assert done.status == 'completed'
        event = restarted.get(ContentIngressEvent, done.event_id)
        body = json.loads(event.payload_json)
        assert body['content_version'] == 'upstream-v7'
        assert body['case']['ready'] is True
        assert len(body['case']['images']) == 2
        assert done.routing_status
    assert calls.count('https://images.example.test/a.png') == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['hash', 'size', 'invalid_image'])
def test_invalid_material_never_enters_ingress(material_env, monkeypatch, kind):
    svc, db = material_env
    body = payload()
    if kind == 'hash': body['case']['images'][0]['sha256'] = 'f' * 64
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: b'x' * 1025 if kind == 'size' else (b'bad' if kind == 'invalid_image' else png()))
    job = submit(svc, db, body); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'failed'
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ContentIngressEvent.id))) == 0

def test_retry_is_bounded_and_operator_compensation_retains_resolved_images(material_env, monkeypatch):
    svc, db = material_env
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: (_ for _ in ()).throw(OSError('unavailable')))
    job = submit(svc, db); db.commit(); now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for i in range(4):
        svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=now + timedelta(hours=i)); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'failed' and job.attempt_count == 3
    svc.retry_material_job(db, job.id, actor='operator', reason='来源恢复'); db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda url, _: png('red' if url.endswith('a.png') else 'blue'))
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=now + timedelta(hours=5)); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed'


def test_api_submit_read_and_role_guards(material_env):
    from fastapi import FastAPI
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.incremental_material_api import build_incremental_material_router
    _, db = material_env
    user = SimpleNamespace(username='operator', role='admin', is_admin=True)
    app = FastAPI(); app.include_router(build_incremental_material_router(lambda: user))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        result = client.post('/api/incremental-materials/jobs', json={'source_system':'fixture', 'submission_id':'api-1',
            'occurred_at':'2026-09-06T00:00:00Z', 'payload': payload()})
        assert result.status_code == 200, result.text
        assert result.json()['status'] == 'queued'
        user.role = 'viewer'
        user.is_admin = False
        assert client.get('/api/incremental-materials/jobs').status_code == 200
        assert client.post('/api/incremental-materials/jobs', json={'source_system':'fixture', 'submission_id':'api-2',
            'occurred_at':'2026-09-06T00:00:00Z', 'payload': payload()}).status_code == 403

def test_admission_pause_uses_existing_durable_backlog(material_env, monkeypatch):
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy

    assert db.scalar(select(MaterialPackage).where(MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id == job.event_id)) is None


def test_transactional_claim_rolls_back_on_crash_and_has_one_winner(material_env, monkeypatch):
    svc, db = material_env
    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    import time
    calls = []
    def download(url, policy):
        calls.append(url); time.sleep(.03)
        return png('red' if url.endswith('a.png') else 'blue')
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', download)
    job = submit(svc, db); db.commit()
    engine = db.bind
    def tick():
        with Session(engine) as worker:
            result = svc.process_due_material_jobs(worker, actor='parallel'); worker.commit(); return result
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        results = list(pool.map(lambda _: tick(), range(2)))
    db.refresh(job)
    assert job.status == 'completed'
    assert sum(len(r['processed_ids']) for r in results) == 1
    assert len(calls) == 2

def test_existing_local_asset_reused_and_verified(material_env, monkeypatch):
    svc, db = material_env
    directory = svc._storage_dir(); directory.mkdir()
    data = png(); (directory / 'existing.png').write_bytes(data)
    asset = Asset(original_name='existing.png', stored_name='existing.png', mime_type='image/png',
                  size_bytes=len(data), sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), category_key='design_case')
    db.add(asset); db.flush()
    body = {'content_id':'single', 'content_version':'1', 'category_key':'design_case', 'material':{'asset_id':asset.id}}
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: pytest.fail('must reuse local file'))
    job = submit(svc, db, body); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed'
    assert json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'] == asset.id
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(Asset.id))) == 1


def test_same_bytes_skip_an_asset_whose_original_expired(material_env, monkeypatch):
    """保留期删掉原图的旧素材不能卡住去重：跳过它复用下一份还在的，不再落新拷贝。"""
    svc, db = material_env
    directory = svc._storage_dir(); directory.mkdir()
    data = png()
    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    expired = Asset(original_name='incremental.png', stored_name='material-expired.png', mime_type='image/png',
                    size_bytes=len(data), sha256=digest, category_key='design_case')
    (directory / 'material-alive.png').write_bytes(data)
    alive = Asset(original_name='incremental.png', stored_name='material-alive.png', mime_type='image/png',
                  size_bytes=len(data), sha256=digest, category_key='design_case')
    db.add_all([expired, alive]); db.flush()
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: data)
    body = {'content_id': 'single', 'content_version': '1', 'category_key': 'design_case',
            'material': {'url': 'https://images.example.test/a.png'}}
    job = submit(svc, db, body); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed'
    assert json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'] == alive.id
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(Asset.id))) == 2


def test_worker_crash_rolls_back_claim_and_next_tick_resumes(material_env, monkeypatch):
    svc, db = material_env
    job = submit(svc, db); db.commit(); job_id = job.id
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: (_ for _ in ()).throw(SystemExit('crash')))
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit): svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker')
    db.rollback()
    assert db.get(type(job), job_id).status == 'queued'
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda url, _: png('red' if url.endswith('a.png') else 'blue'))
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed'

def test_http_503_then_200_retries_through_download_transport(material_env, monkeypatch):
    """Exercise HTTP status handling with an injected byte-stream transport."""
    svc, db = material_env
    import socket
    counts = []
    class Socket:
        def close(self): pass
    class Response:
        def __init__(self, status, data): self.status = status; self.stream = io.BytesIO(data)
        def getheader(self, key, default=None): return default
        def read(self, size): return self.stream.read(size)
    class Connection:
        def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs): self.sock = None; self.path = None
        def request(self, method, path, headers): self.path = path
        def getresponse(self):
            counts.append(self.path)
            return Response(503 if len(counts) == 1 else 200, png('red' if self.path.endswith('a.png') else 'blue'))
        def close(self): pass
    class Context:
        def wrap_socket(self, raw, server_hostname): return raw
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc.socket, 'getaddrinfo', lambda *_args, **_kwargs: [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, '', ('93.184.216.34', 443))])
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc.socket, 'create_connection', lambda *_args, **_kwargs: Socket())
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc.ssl, 'create_default_context', lambda: Context())
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc.http.client, 'HTTPSConnection', Connection)
    job = submit(svc, db); db.commit(); now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=now); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'retry_wait'
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=now + timedelta(minutes=1)); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed'
    assert counts == ['/a.png', '/a.png', '/b.png']


def test_retry_compare_update_rejects_stale_operator_state(material_env, monkeypatch):
    svc, db = material_env
    job = submit(svc, db); job.status = 'failed'; db.commit()
    with Session(db.bind, expire_on_commit=False) as stale_db:
        stale = stale_db.get(type(job), job.id)
        stale_db.commit()
        svc.retry_material_job(db, job.id, actor='first', reason='来源恢复'); db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setattr(stale_db, 'scalar', lambda *_args, **_kwargs: stale)
        with pytest.raises(svc.MaterialError, match='retry_status_conflict'):
            svc.retry_material_job(stale_db, job.id, actor='second', reason='重复恢复')
        stale_db.rollback()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('damage', ['missing', 'corrupt'])
def test_resolved_image_missing_on_restart(material_env,monkeypatch,damage):
    svc,db=material_env
    def first(url,policy):
        if url.endswith('b.png'):raise OSError('temporary')
        return png('red')
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_download',first)
    job=submit(svc,db);db.commit();now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='worker',now=now);db.commit()
    resolved=json.loads(job.resolved_json)
    asset=db.get(Asset,resolved['a']['asset_id'])
    path = svc._storage_dir()/asset.stored_name
    if damage == 'missing': path.unlink()
    else: path.write_bytes(png('yellow'))
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_download',lambda url,policy:png('blue'))
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='worker',now=now+timedelta(minutes=1));db.commit()
    assert job.status != 'completed', f'completed with missing previously resolved file: {job.routing_status}'
    assert json.loads(job.resolved_json) == resolved
    assert job.event_id is None
    path.write_bytes(png('red'))
    svc.retry_material_job(db, job.id, actor='operator', reason='原图从备份恢复'); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=now+timedelta(minutes=2)); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed'

def test_old_asset_makes_incomplete_revision_ready(material_env,monkeypatch):
    svc,db=material_env
    from app.label_governance import ingest_content_event
    directory=svc._storage_dir();directory.mkdir()
    data=png('yellow');(directory/'old.png').write_bytes(data)
    import hashlib
    asset=Asset(original_name='old.png',stored_name='old.png',mime_type='image/png',size_bytes=len(data),sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),category_key='design_case')
    db.add(asset);db.flush()
    old={'content_id':'case-1','content_version':'v6','category_key':'design_case','asset_id':asset.id}
    ingest_content_event(db,event_id='v6',schema_version='content-ingress-v1',event_type='content.created',source_system='fixture',occurred_at=datetime(2026,9,5,tzinfo=timezone.utc),payload=old,received_by='test')
    body=payload();body['case']['ready']=False
    incomplete,record,_=ingest_content_event(db,event_id='v7-incomplete',schema_version='content-ingress-v1',event_type='content.updated',source_system='fixture',occurred_at=datetime(2026,9,6,tzinfo=timezone.utc),payload=body,received_by='test')
    assert record.status=='ready'
    original_payload = incomplete.payload_json
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_download',lambda url,policy:png('red' if url.endswith('a.png') else 'blue'))
    job=submit(svc,db);db.commit();svc.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='worker');db.commit()
    event=db.get(ContentIngressEvent,job.event_id)
    assert event.status != 'stale', f'job={job.status} routing={job.routing_status}; completion ignored while original incomplete retains old anchor'
    assert incomplete.payload_json == original_payload
    assert job.status == 'completed'
    assert json.loads(event.payload_json)['content_version'] == 'upstream-v7'
    assert record.asset_id != asset.id


def test_slow_verified_prefix_does_not_starve_remaining_images(material_env, monkeypatch):
    svc,db=material_env
    body=payload();body['case']['expected_image_ids'].append('c')
    body['case']['images'].append({'cloud_id':'c','url':'https://images.example.test/c.png'})
    clock=[0.0]
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc.time,'monotonic',lambda:clock[0])
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_download',lambda url,policy:png({'a.png':'red','b.png':'blue','c.png':'yellow'}[url.rsplit('/',1)[-1]]))
    original=svc._resolve
    def slow_resolve(*args):
        result=original(*args);clock[0]+=11;return result
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_resolve',slow_resolve)
    job=submit(svc,db,body);db.commit();now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for index in range(5):
        svc.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='worker',now=now+timedelta(minutes=index));db.commit()
        db.expire_all()
        if job.status=='completed':break
    assert job.status=='completed', 'verified prefix consumed every tick; later images starved'
    assert len(json.loads(job.resolved_json))==3
    event=db.get(ContentIngressEvent,job.event_id)
    assert 'validation_json' not in event.payload_json

def test_final_material_validation_detects_changed_cached_prefix(material_env,monkeypatch):
    svc,db=material_env
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_download',lambda url,policy:png('red' if url.endswith('a.png') else 'blue'))
    original=svc._resolve
    damaged=[False]
    def change_first_after_second(*args):
        asset=original(*args)
        resolved=json.loads(args[1].resolved_json)
        if 'a' in resolved and asset.id!=resolved['a']['asset_id'] and not damaged[0]:
            first=db.get(Asset,resolved['a']['asset_id'])
            (svc._storage_dir()/first.stored_name).write_bytes(png('yellow'))
            damaged[0]=True
        return asset
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_resolve',change_first_after_second)
    job=submit(svc,db);db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='worker');db.commit()
    assert damaged[0]
    assert job.status!='completed'
    assert job.event_id is None


def test_cached_validation_is_invalidated_when_material_policy_changes(material_env,monkeypatch):
    svc,db=material_env
    body=payload()
    clock=[0.0];monkeypatch.setattr(svc.time,'monotonic',lambda:clock[0])
    buffer=io.BytesIO();Image.new('RGB',(5,5),'blue').save(buffer,format='JPEG')
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_download',lambda url,policy:png('red') if url.endswith('a.png') else buffer.getvalue())
    original=svc._resolve
    def slow_resolve(*args):
        asset=original(*args);clock[0]+=11;return asset
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc,'_resolve',slow_resolve)
    job=submit(svc,db,body);db.commit();now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='worker',now=now);db.commit()
    assert job.status=='queued'
    profile=db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key=='design_case'))
    profile.allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]';db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='worker',now=now+timedelta(minutes=1));db.commit()
    assert job.status=='failed'
    assert job.error_code=='material_type_invalid'
    assert job.event_id is None


def test_originals_go_to_the_mounted_store_and_wait_when_it_is_missing(material_env, monkeypatch):
    """原图子目录（机械盘挂载点）到位就写进去；标记文件不在说明没挂上，任务重试而不是写回固态盘。"""
    svc, db = material_env
    root = svc._storage_dir(); root.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.setenv(svc.ORIGINAL_STORE_ENV, 'originals')
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: png())
    body = {'content_id': 'single', 'content_version': '1', 'category_key': 'design_case',
            'material': {'url': 'https://images.example.test/a.png'}}
    job = submit(svc, db, body); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'retry_wait' and job.error_code == 'material_original_store_unavailable'
    assert not any(root.rglob('material-*'))
    (root / 'originals').mkdir(); (root / 'originals' / svc.ORIGINAL_STORE_MARKER).write_text('')
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=10)); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed'
    asset = db.get(Asset, json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'])
    assert asset.stored_name.startswith('originals/material-')
    assert (root / asset.stored_name).is_file()


def test_pixel_cap_is_per_source_and_reports_its_own_error(material_env, monkeypatch):
    """任务 91（9842×7382）被拒成 material_image_invalid：像素上限抛的 MaterialError 被同一个 try 吞掉了。
    现在上限按来源配（默认 4000 万），超限报 material_pixels_exceeded；灵感图放宽后大图用缩样解码通过。"""
    svc, db = material_env
    big = io.BytesIO(); Image.new('RGB', (8000, 6000), 'red').save(big, format='JPEG', quality=30)
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: big.getvalue())
    body = {'content_id': 'big', 'content_version': '1', 'category_key': 'design_case',
            'material': {'url': 'https://images.example.test/big.jpg'}}
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON', json.dumps({'fixture': {'allowed_hosts': ['images.example.test'], 'max_bytes': 25 * 1024 * 1024, 'max_images': 4}}))
    db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == 'design_case')).allowed_mime_types_json = '["image/jpeg","image/png"]'
    db.commit()
    job = submit(svc, db, body); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'failed' and job.error_code == 'material_pixels_exceeded'
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON', json.dumps({'fixture': {'allowed_hosts': ['images.example.test'], 'max_bytes': 25 * 1024 * 1024, 'max_images': 4, 'max_pixels': 120_000_000}}))
    svc.retry_material_job(db, job.id, actor='operator', reason='来源放宽像素上限'); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker', now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=10)); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed', job.error_code
    asset = db.get(Asset, json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'])
    assert (asset.width, asset.height) == (8000, 6000)


def test_images_above_the_downscale_threshold_are_scaled_into_it_before_storage(material_env, monkeypatch):
    """任务 91（9842×7382，7270 万像素）进料过了 1.2 亿上限，却被冻结在机制版本里的 Qwen205 能力档案
    （max_source_pixels 6000 万）拦下。老大定：图片压缩后通过处理——超过来源 downscale_pixels 的图在进料时
    缩到阈值内、重编码 JPEG 再入库，留一条审计记录原尺寸与原 sha256。"""
    svc, db = material_env
    big = io.BytesIO(); Image.new('RGB', (9000, 8000), 'red').save(big, format='JPEG', quality=30)
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: big.getvalue())
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON', json.dumps({'fixture': {'allowed_hosts': ['images.example.test'], 'max_bytes': 25 * 1024 * 1024, 'max_images': 4, 'max_pixels': 120_000_000, 'downscale_pixels': 60_000_000}}))
    body = {'content_id': 'huge', 'content_version': '1', 'category_key': 'design_case',
            'material': {'url': 'https://images.example.test/huge.jpg'}}
    job = submit(svc, db, body); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job.status == 'completed', job.error_code
    asset = db.get(Asset, json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'])
    # JPEG 只用 DCT 缩样（1/2）解码，不全尺寸展开：9000×8000 → 4500×4000（1800 万像素），进料容器 512 MiB 预算内
    assert (asset.width, asset.height) == (4500, 4000) and asset.mime_type == 'image/jpeg'
    stored = (tmp_root(svc) / asset.stored_name).read_bytes()
    assert hashlib.sha256(stored).hexdigest() == asset.sha256 and Image.open(io.BytesIO(stored)).size == (asset.width, asset.height)
    event = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == 'incremental_material_downscaled'))
    payload = json.loads(event.payload_json)
    assert (payload['source_width'], payload['source_height']) == (9000, 8000) and payload['source_sha256'] == hashlib.sha256(big.getvalue()).hexdigest()
    assert (payload['width'], payload['height']) == (asset.width, asset.height) and int(event.subject_id) == asset.id
    # 阈值为 0（默认）不缩：同一张图按原尺寸入库
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON', json.dumps({'fixture': {'allowed_hosts': ['images.example.test'], 'max_bytes': 25 * 1024 * 1024, 'max_images': 4, 'max_pixels': 120_000_000}}))
    body2 = dict(body, content_id='huge-raw')
    job2 = submit(svc, db, body2, ident='submission-2'); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    assert job2.status == 'completed', job2.error_code
    asset2 = db.get(Asset, json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job2.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'])
    assert (asset2.width, asset2.height) == (9000, 8000)


def tmp_root(svc):
    return Path(svc._storage_dir())


def _source_env(monkeypatch, **extra):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON', json.dumps({'fixture': {'allowed_hosts': ['images.example.test'], 'max_bytes': 25 * 1024 * 1024, 'max_images': 4, **extra}}))


def _intake(svc, db, monkeypatch, data, ident):
    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, '_download', lambda *_: data)
    job = submit(svc, db, {'content_id': ident, 'content_version': '1', 'category_key': 'design_case',
                           'material': {'url': 'https://images.example.test/' + ident + '.img'}}, ident=ident); db.commit()
    svc.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor='worker'); db.commit()
    return job


def test_downscale_keeps_exif_orientation_and_composites_transparency_on_white(material_env, monkeypatch):
    """评审 R2：缩图前先按 EXIF 方向摆正、透明图合成白底，否则模型和审核页看到的方向/背景和原图不一致。"""
    svc, db = material_env
    _source_env(monkeypatch, max_pixels=120_000_000, downscale_pixels=20_000)
    rotated = io.BytesIO(); img = Image.new('RGB', (400, 200), 'red'); exif = img.getexif(); exif[0x0112] = 6
    img.save(rotated, format='JPEG', quality=50, exif=exif.tobytes())
    job = _intake(svc, db, monkeypatch, rotated.getvalue(), 'rotated')
    assert job.status == 'completed', job.error_code
    asset = db.get(Asset, json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'])
    assert (asset.width, asset.height) == (100, 200)  # 1/2 缩样后 200×100，再按 Orientation=6 转成竖图
    transparent = io.BytesIO(); Image.new('RGBA', (300, 300), (255, 0, 0, 0)).save(transparent, format='PNG')
    job = _intake(svc, db, monkeypatch, transparent.getvalue(), 'transparent')
    assert job.status == 'completed', job.error_code
    asset = db.get(Asset, json.loads(db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id).payload_json)['asset_id'])
    stored = Image.open(io.BytesIO((tmp_root(svc) / asset.stored_name).read_bytes()))
    assert stored.size == (141, 141) and asset.mime_type == 'image/jpeg'
    assert all(channel > 250 for channel in stored.getpixel((70, 70)))  # 全透明红 → 白底，不是不透明红


def test_formats_without_draft_decoding_keep_a_fixed_pixel_budget(material_env, monkeypatch):
    """评审 R1：PNG/WEBP 没有 DCT 缩样，验证与缩图都要全尺寸展开；3000 万像素以上直接拒，不随来源 max_pixels 放宽。"""
    svc, db = material_env
    _source_env(monkeypatch, max_pixels=120_000_000, downscale_pixels=60_000_000)
    big_png = io.BytesIO(); Image.new('RGB', (6000, 5100), 'blue').save(big_png, format='PNG', compress_level=1)
    job = _intake(svc, db, monkeypatch, big_png.getvalue(), 'bigpng')
    assert job.status == 'failed' and job.error_code == 'material_pixels_exceeded'
    assert svc.NON_JPEG_MAX_PIXELS == 30_000_000

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_mechanisms.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import threading

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent,
)
from app.incremental_mechanisms import (
    IncrementalMechanismError,
    list_incremental_mechanism_events,
    resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue,
    rollback_incremental_mechanism,
    set_incremental_mechanism,
)
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def test_incremental_enqueue_fails_closed_without_pointer(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'missing-pointer.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            seed_incremental_versions(db, count=1)
            with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError) as exc_info:
                resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, "space_image")
            assert exc_info.value.code == "incremental_mechanism_not_configured"
            assert exc_info.value.status_code == 409
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_pointer_switch_is_atomic_audited_and_rollbackable(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'pointer-switch.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
            first_version, second_version = seed_incremental_versions(db)
            first = set_incremental_mechanism(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                workflow_version_id=first_version.id,
                expected_lock_version=None,
                reason="首次启用",
                actor="admin",
            )
            assert first.lock_version == 1
            second = set_incremental_mechanism(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                workflow_version_id=second_version.id,
                expected_lock_version=first.lock_version,
                reason="新机制回归通过",
                actor="admin",
            )
            assert second.workflow_version_id == second_version.id
            assert second.lock_version == 2
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)
                .where(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key
                    == "space_image"
                )
            ) == 1
            events = list_incremental_mechanism_events(db, "space_image")
            assert [event.action for event in events] == ["set", "set"]
            assert events[-1].previous_workflow_version_id == first_version.id

            rolled_back = rollback_incremental_mechanism(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                event_id=events[-1].id,
                expected_lock_version=second.lock_version,
                reason="新机制线上指标异常",
                actor="admin",
            )
            assert rolled_back.workflow_version_id == first_version.id
            assert rolled_back.lock_version == 3
            assert list_incremental_mechanism_events(db, "space_image")[-1].action == (
                "rollback"
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_stale_switch_is_rejected_without_new_audit_event(tmp_path) -> None:

                    actor="admin",
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "incremental_pointer_conflict"
            assert len(list_incremental_mechanism_events(db, "space_image")) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_resolver_freezes_the_selected_complete_mechanism(tmp_path) -> None:

def test_workflow_the_real_incremental_worker_cannot_run_is_not_activated(

) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'unsupported-worker.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = seed_incremental_versions(db, count=1)[0]
            nodes = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
            prompt_c = {
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "display_name": "提示词 C",
                "config": {
                    "prompt_version_id": 999,
                    "model_registry_id": 999,
                    "max_tokens": 1024,
                    "thinking_level": "high",
                    "component_snapshot": {"content": {"stage": "C"}},
                    "model_snapshot": {"model_registry_id": 999},
                },
            }
            nodes.insert(1, prompt_c)
            version.step_manifest_json = json.dumps(nodes, ensure_ascii=False)
            version.edge_manifest_json = json.dumps(
                [
                    {"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt_c"},
                    {"from": "prompt_c", "to": "downstream"},
                ]
            )
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError) as exc_info:
