            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding
                )
            ) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_concurrent_initial_pointer_has_one_winner(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'pointer-race.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 10},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            versions = seed_incremental_versions(db)
            from app.workflow_composer_runtime import ensure_composer_fixture_version

            ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, "upstream_connector")
            ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, "downstream_projection")
            version_ids = [version.id for version in versions]
            db.commit()

        barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
        results: list[int] = []

        def worker(version_id: int) -> None:
            with Session(engine) as db:
                barrier.wait()
                try:
                    set_incremental_mechanism(
                        db,
                        category_key="space_image",
                        workflow_version_id=version_id,
                        expected_lock_version=None,
                        reason="并发首次启用",
                        actor=f"admin-{version_id}",
                    )
                    db.commit()
                    results.append(200)
                except IncrementalMechanismError as exc:
                    db.rollback()
                    results.append(exc.status_code)

        threads = [
            threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(version_id,))
            for version_id in version_ids
        ]
        for thread in threads:
            thread.start()
        for thread in threads:
            thread.join()

        assert sorted(results) == [200, 409]
        with Session(engine) as db:
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)
                .where(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key
                    == "space_image"
                )
            ) == 1
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent)
            ) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_locked_enqueue_refreshes_a_cached_binding(tmp_path):
    from sqlalchemy import update
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'locked-binding.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
            first, second = seed_incremental_versions(db)
            binding = set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key='space_image',
                workflow_version_id=first.id, expected_lock_version=None,
                reason='initial', actor='test')
            db.commit()
            db.execute(update(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
                CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.id == binding.id).values(
                workflow_version_id=second.id, lock_version=2).execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
            db.commit()
            assert binding.workflow_version_id == first.id
            fresh = resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, 'space_image', locked_refresh=True)
            assert fresh.workflow_version_id == second.id
            assert fresh.binding_lock_version == 2
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_mechanisms_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.incremental_mechanisms_api import build_incremental_mechanisms_router
from app.models import User
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def test_pointer_api_is_readable_but_only_admin_can_activate() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        versions = seed_incremental_versions(db)
        version_ids = [version.id for version in versions]
        db.commit()

    current_role = {"value": "manager"}

    def require_user() -> User:
        role = current_role["value"]
        return User(
            id=1,
            username=role,
            password_hash="x",
            display_name=role,
            role=role,
            is_admin=role == "admin",
        )

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    test_app = FastAPI()
    test_app.include_router(build_incremental_mechanisms_router(require_user))
    test_app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    client = TestClient(test_app)
    try:
        empty = client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/space_image")
        assert empty.status_code == 200
        assert empty.json() == {"category_key": "space_image", "binding": None}

        forbidden = client.put(
            "/api/incremental-mechanisms/space_image",
            json={
                "workflow_version_id": version_ids[0],
                "expected_lock_version": None,
                "reason": "回归通过",
            },
        )
        assert forbidden.status_code == 403
        assert forbidden.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "incremental_mechanism_activation_forbidden"
        )

        current_role["value"] = "admin"
        activated = client.put(
            "/api/incremental-mechanisms/space_image",
            json={
                "workflow_version_id": version_ids[0],
                "expected_lock_version": None,
                "reason": "存量回归通过",
                "model_execution_mode": "real",
            },
        )
        assert activated.status_code == 200, activated.text
        binding = activated.json()["binding"]
        assert binding["workflow_version_id"] == version_ids[0]
        assert binding["lock_version"] == 1
        assert binding["workflow_version"] == "1"
        assert binding['model_execution_mode'] == 'real'

        switched = client.put(
            "/api/incremental-mechanisms/space_image",
            json={
                "workflow_version_id": version_ids[1],
                "expected_lock_version": 1,
                "reason": "新机制纠偏完成",
                "model_execution_mode": "simulated",
            },
        )
        assert switched.status_code == 200, switched.text
        assert switched.json()["binding"]["workflow_version_id"] == version_ids[1]
        assert switched.json()['binding']['model_execution_mode'] == 'simulated'

        events = client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/space_image/events")
        assert events.status_code == 200
        assert [item["action"] for item in events.json()["items"]] == [
            "set",
            "set",
        ]

        rolled_back = client.post(
            "/api/incremental-mechanisms/space_image/rollback",
            json={
                "event_id": events.json()["items"][-1]["id"],
                "expected_lock_version": 2,
                "reason": "增量异常回滚",
            },
        )
        assert rolled_back.status_code == 200, rolled_back.text
        assert rolled_back.json()["binding"]["workflow_version_id"] == version_ids[0]
        assert rolled_back.json()['binding']['model_execution_mode'] == 'real'
    finally:
        client.close()
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_native_compatibility.py =====
"""Zero-cost regression for native incremental activation."""
import json
import hashlib
import socket

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.incremental_mechanisms import IncrementalMechanismError, require_incremental_compatible_workflow
from app.models import ModelConfig, WorkflowVersion
from app.strict_prompt_release import materialize_strict_vision_candidates
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from app.workflow_v3 import validate_managed_workflow_v3
from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database
from app.audit import canonical_json


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def forbid_network(monkeypatch):
    def blocked(*args, **kwargs):
        raise AssertionError("network forbidden in zero-cost incremental fixtures")
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket, "connect", blocked)
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket, "create_connection", blocked)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("category", ["inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"])
def test_native_incremental_accepts_independent_prompt_parameters(tmp_path, category):
    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            receipt = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(db, actor="test", apply=True)
            item = next(row for row in receipt.workflows if row.category_key == category)
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, item.candidate_workflow_id)
            nodes = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
            for node in nodes:
                if node["kind"] == "prompt":
                    is_a = node["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a"
                    node["config"]["thinking_enabled"] = is_a
                    node["config"]["thinking_level"] = "medium" if is_a else "none"
            version.step_manifest_json = json.dumps(nodes)
            policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
            policy["run_modes"] = ["evaluation", "stock_regression", "incremental"]
            version.resource_policy_json = json.dumps(policy)
            db.flush()
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
            version.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(manifest).encode()).hexdigest()
            assert not validate_managed_workflow_v3(db, manifest)
            before = (version.step_manifest_json, version.canonical_hash)
            selected, accepted = require_incremental_compatible_workflow(
                db, category_key=category, workflow_version_id=version.id
            )
            assert selected.id == version.id
            assert accepted == manifest
            assert (version.step_manifest_json, version.canonical_hash) == before
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("gate_type", ["upstream_type_short_circuit", "classification_level_map"])
def test_incremental_ingress_runs_native_short_circuit(monkeypatch, gate_type):
    from sqlalchemy import func, select
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuleModuleVersion, WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowNodeAsset
    from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    from tests.test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _payload, _headers, _close
    from tests.test_upstream_type_short_circuit import _frozen_node
    from tests.test_classification_level_map import _content, _precheck
    from app.classification_level_map import short_circuit_rules_from_classification_map

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        panorama = gate_type == "upstream_type_short_circuit"
        content = build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit() if panorama else _content()
        gate = _frozen_node(content)
        gate.update(display_name="全景前置", error_policy={"kind": "fail"}, retry_policy={"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0}, evidence_fields=[])
        gate["node_instance_id"] = gate_type
        gate["config"].update(asset_key=gate_type, module_type_key=gate_type)
        gate["config"]["component_snapshot"]["module_type_key"] = gate_type
        component = WorkflowNodeAsset(category_key="space_image", asset_key=gate_type, kind="rule_module", display_name="短路规则", module_type_key=gate_type, created_by="test")
        db.add(component)
        db.flush()
        module = RuleModuleVersion(asset_id=component.id, version=1, module_type_key=gate_type, content_json=canonical_json(content), content_hash="a" * 64, source_working_copy_revision=1, created_by="test")
        db.add(module)
        db.flush()
        gate["config"]["rule_module_version_id"] = module.id
        gate["config"]["component_snapshot"]["version_id"] = module.id
        nodes = [n for n in json.loads(version.step_manifest_json) if n["kind"] != "rule_module"]
        nodes.insert(1 if panorama else 2, gate)
        for n in nodes:
            if n["kind"] == "prompt":
                n["config"]["execution_role"] = "classification" if n["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a" else "grading"
                if not panorama and n["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a":
                    n["config"]["short_circuit_rules"] = short_circuit_rules_from_classification_map(content)
        version.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
        version.edge_manifest_json = canonical_json([{"from": a["node_instance_id"], "to": b["node_instance_id"]} for a, b in zip(nodes, nodes[1:])])
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["level_scale"] = {"enabled_levels": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"]}
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        version.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)).encode()).hexdigest()
        db.flush()
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_version_id=version.id, expected_lock_version=1, reason="fixture native gate", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        payload = _payload(event_id="native-panorama", asset_id=asset.id)
        payload["payload"]["material_type"] = "全景图"
        response = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "SessionLocal", sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False))
        if not panorama:
            from app.workflow_runtime import claim_next_runtime_step, complete_runtime_step
            assert worker.process_runtime_step_once("classification-upstream")
            attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(db, "classification-fixture")
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            assert attempt.step_key == "prompt_a"
            complete_runtime_step(db, attempt.id, attempt.lease_token, {"output": _precheck("交通工具")})
            db.commit()
        class ForbiddenClient:
            def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
                raise AssertionError("panorama must not call a model")
        for _ in range(10):
            if not worker.process_runtime_step_once("panorama-fixture", model_client_type=ForbiddenClient):
                break
        db.expire_all()
        attempts = {a.step_key: a for a in db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))}
        assert attempts["prompt_a"].status == ("skipped" if panorama else "succeeded")
        assert attempts["prompt_b"].status == "skipped"
        decision = json.loads(attempts[gate_type if panorama else "prompt_a"].output_manifest_json)["short_circuit_decision"]
        assert decision["target_level"] == "L2"
        from app.workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result
        result = reduce_native_workflow_result(persisted_workflow_manifest(version), {key: json.loads(a.output_manifest_json or "{}") for key, a in attempts.items()})
        assert result["level"] == "L2"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation)) == 0
        assert run.status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_native_incremental_rejects_disabled_frozen_source_model(tmp_path):
    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            receipt = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(db, actor="test", apply=True)
            item = next(row for row in receipt.workflows if row.category_key == "inspiration_image")
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, item.candidate_workflow_id)
            policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
            policy["run_modes"] = ["incremental"]
            version.resource_policy_json = json.dumps(policy)
            prompt = next(n for n in json.loads(version.step_manifest_json) if n["kind"] == "prompt")
            db.get(ModelConfig, prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"]["source_model_config_id"]).active = False
            db.flush()
            with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError, match="模型"):
                require_incremental_compatible_workflow(db, category_key="inspiration_image", workflow_version_id=version.id)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_native_client_receives_independent_thinking_controls(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    import hashlib
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from PIL import Image
    from sqlalchemy import select
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.models import ProductionRun
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
    from tests.test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _headers, _payload, _close
    from tests.test_incremental_autorun import _enable_real_incremental_policy
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    import app.workflow_model_executor as executor

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        version, _model = _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch)
        nodes = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
        for node in nodes:
            if node["kind"] == "prompt":
                enabled = node["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a"
                node["config"].update(thinking_enabled=enabled, thinking_level="medium" if enabled else "none")
        version.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
        version.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)).encode()).hexdigest()
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_version_id=version.id, expected_lock_version=1, reason="independent thinking fixture", actor="test")
        path = tmp_path / "asset-1.jpg"
        Image.new("RGB", (640, 480), "white").save(path)
        raw = path.read_bytes()
        asset.stored_name, asset.width, asset.height = path.name, 640, 480
        asset.size_bytes, asset.sha256 = len(raw), hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        db.commit()
        response = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="native-thinking", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        calls = []
        class FakeProvider:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config
            async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                calls.append((self.config.thinking_mode, options.get("reasoning_effort")))
                return DoubaoResponse(parsed={"label": "fixture", "score": 88}, raw_text='{"label":"fixture","score":88}', raw_payload={"id": f"req-{len(calls)}"}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id=f"req-{len(calls)}", attempt_count=1, output_budget=2048, reasoning_effort=options.get("reasoning_effort"), thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=120, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=140)
        monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(bind=engine))
        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "get_settings", lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path, nas_maps_root=None))
        for _ in range(20):
            if not worker.process_runtime_step_once("thinking-fixture", model_client_type=FakeProvider):
                break
        db.expire_all()
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run.status == "succeeded", (run.error_code, run.error_message, calls)
        frozen_nodes = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
        assert [(n["config"]["thinking_enabled"], n["config"]["thinking_level"]) for n in frozen_nodes if n["kind"] == "prompt"] == [(True, "medium"), (False, "none")]
        # The fixture provider uses auto+medium; disabled B must lose reasoning.
        assert calls == [("auto", "medium"), ("disabled", None)]
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_readiness.py =====
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.incremental_readiness import automation_readiness_blocker


def fixture():
    target = SimpleNamespace(id=1, contract_key="output", direction="downstream", status="active",
        adapter_key="sql-envelope-v1", logical_locator="readiness_fixture", contract_hash="a" * 64)
    db = SimpleNamespace(get=lambda *args: target)
    manifest = {"resource_policy": {"incremental_autorun": {"enabled": True, "model_execution_mode": "real"}},
        "nodes": [{"kind": "downstream_projection", "config": {"connector_key": "output", "connector_snapshot": {
            "connector_contract_id": 1, "contract_key": "output", "direction": "downstream", "contract_hash": "a" * 64}}}]}
    return db, target, manifest


def test_real_formal_requires_deployment_configuration_without_network(monkeypatch):
    db, _, manifest = fixture()
    monkeypatch.delenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_READINESS_FIXTURE", raising=False)
    assert "连接配置" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_READINESS_FIXTURE", "sqlite:////does-not-exist/do-not-create.db")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_READINESS_FIXTURE_TABLE", "result_envelopes")
    assert automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None) is None


def test_shadow_local_sink_and_simulation_do_not_require_formal_connection():
    db, _, manifest = fixture()
    manifest["nodes"][0]["config"] = {}
    assert automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, {"mode": "shadow"}) is None
    manifest["resource_policy"]["incremental_autorun"]["model_execution_mode"] = "simulated"
    assert automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None) is None


def test_real_readiness_rejects_unconfigured_retired_and_mismatched_contract():
    db, target, manifest = fixture()
    manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]["connector_snapshot"]["connector_contract_id"] = True
    assert "冻结" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)
    manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]["connector_snapshot"]["connector_contract_id"] = 1
    target.status = "retired"
    assert "启用" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)
    target.status = "active"
    target.contract_key = "other"
    assert "不一致" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)


def test_shadow_cannot_claim_ready_for_external_continuation():
    db, _, manifest = fixture()
    manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]["completion_mode"] = "await_follow_up"
    assert "外部续跑" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, {"mode": "shadow"})


def test_invalid_deployment_dsn_is_redacted(monkeypatch):
    db, _, manifest = fixture()
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_READINESS_FIXTURE", "invalid://secret:not-for-output@host/db")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_READINESS_FIXTURE_TABLE", "results")
    blocker = automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)
    assert blocker and "not-for-output" not in blocker and "invalid://" not in blocker


def _ready_env(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_READINESS_FIXTURE", "sqlite:////does-not-exist/do-not-create.db")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_READINESS_FIXTURE_TABLE", "result_envelopes")


def test_a_version_that_pins_the_contract_by_id_alone_is_ready(monkeypatch):
    """换下游只改 ``connector_contract_version_id``、不留快照的版本也算钉住了。

    2026-09-16 线上 v299（factory-prod-downstream-v3）就是这个形状：切到正式下游时
    把 id 换成 11 并把上一版留下的合同 8 快照摘掉了。真实投递一直按合同 11 正常发，
    卡片却因为只认快照而长期显示「待就绪」，自动运行开关也被一并禁用。
    """

    db, _, manifest = fixture()
    _ready_env(monkeypatch)
    config = manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]
    config.pop("connector_snapshot")
    config["connector_contract_version_id"] = 1
    assert automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None) is None


def test_pinning_nothing_at_all_is_still_refused(monkeypatch):
    db, _, manifest = fixture()
    _ready_env(monkeypatch)
    manifest["nodes"][0]["config"].pop("connector_snapshot")
    assert "缺少冻结合同" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)


def test_an_id_that_disagrees_with_the_snapshot_is_refused(monkeypatch):
    """两处都钉了却指向不同合同，说明改版本时只挪了一半——这才是真的该拦。"""

    db, _, manifest = fixture()
    _ready_env(monkeypatch)
    manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]["connector_contract_version_id"] = 2
    assert "不一致" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)


def test_an_id_only_pin_still_has_to_match_the_node_connector_key(monkeypatch):
    db, target, manifest = fixture()
    _ready_env(monkeypatch)
    config = manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]
    config.pop("connector_snapshot")
    config["connector_contract_version_id"] = 1
    target.contract_key = "somewhere-else"
    assert "不一致" in automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, None)


def test_inspiration_readiness_is_closed_by_default_and_never_creates_journal(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    import json
    from test_inspiration_factory_delivery import CONFIG
    from app.inspiration_factory_delivery import INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT
    db,target,manifest=fixture()
    target.adapter_key='inspiration-factory-submit-v1'
    target.category_key='inspiration_image'
    target.schema_fingerprint=INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT
    target.field_mapping_json=json.dumps(CONFIG)
    target.logical_locator='readiness_fixture'
    tmp_path.chmod(0o700)
    journal=tmp_path/'submit.sqlite'
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_SUBMIT_JOURNAL',str(journal))
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_READINESS_FIXTURE','https://factory-api-test.3d66.com/api/factory')
    monkeypatch.delenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED',raising=False)
    monkeypatch.delenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED',raising=False)
    assert '等待运营' in automation_readiness_blocker(db,manifest,None)
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED','1')
    assert automation_readiness_blocker(db,manifest,None) is None
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_READINESS_FIXTURE','https://factory-api.3d66.com/api/factory')
    assert '仅开放测试接口' in automation_readiness_blocker(db,manifest,None)
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED','0')
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED','1')
    assert automation_readiness_blocker(db,manifest,None) is None
    assert not journal.exists()
    target.field_mapping_json='{}'
    assert '等级映射' in automation_readiness_blocker(db,manifest,None)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_reassessment.py =====
import hashlib
import json
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem, WorkflowVersion, CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, ContentIngressEvent, MaterialPackage, AuditEvent
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from app.incremental_reassessment import preview, create, assert_no_replacement
from app.failure_console import FailureConsoleError
from test_incremental_autorun import _enable_real_incremental_policy
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _headers, _payload, _close

@pytest.fixture
def failed(monkeypatch):
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    first, _ = _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch)
    response = client.post('/api/content-ingress/events', headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id='reassess-one',asset_id=asset.id))
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    old = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
    old.status = 'failed'
    for attempt in db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt)):
        attempt.status = 'failed'
    for item in db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem)):
        item.status = 'dead_lettered'
    newer = db.scalars(select(WorkflowVersion).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id)).all()[1]
    newer.step_manifest_json = first.step_manifest_json
    newer.resource_policy_json = first.resource_policy_json
    newer.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(newer)).encode()).hexdigest()
    binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding))
    set_incremental_mechanism(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_version_id=newer.id,expected_lock_version=binding.lock_version,reason='new candidate',actor='test')
    db.commit()
    yield db, old, newer
    _close(engine,db)


def test_reassessment_new_run_same_fact_frozen_old_and_idempotent(failed):
    db, old, newer = failed
    snapshot = old.snapshot_json
    packages = [(p.id,p.mechanism_snapshot_json,p.status) for p in db.scalars(select(MaterialPackage))]
    quote = preview(db, old.id)
    child = create(db,run_id=old.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],reason='verified old schema failure',actor='test')
    db.commit()
    assert child.id != old.id and child.source_run_id == old.id
    assert child.source_id == old.source_id and child.workflow_version_id == newer.id
    assert json.loads(child.snapshot_json)['runtime_context']['input_manifest'] == json.loads(snapshot)['runtime_context']['input_manifest']
    assert old.snapshot_json == snapshot and old.status == 'failed'
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ContentIngressEvent)) == 1
    assert [(db.get(MaterialPackage,i).id,db.get(MaterialPackage,i).mechanism_snapshot_json,db.get(MaterialPackage,i).status) for i,_,_ in packages] == packages
    assert create(db,run_id=old.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],reason='verified old schema failure',actor='test').id == child.id
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError,match='新运行'):
        assert_no_replacement(db,old)


def test_reassessment_stale_quote_cannot_create(failed):
    db,old,_ = failed
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError,match='重新预览'):
        create(db,run_id=old.id,quote_hash='wrong',reason='test',actor='test')
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun)) == 1
    assert not db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action=='incremental_reassessment_authorized'))


def test_reassessment_cannot_be_forged_with_source_run_id(failed, monkeypatch):
    from app.workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
    import app.incremental_reassessment as module
    db,old,_ = failed
    quote=preview(db,old.id)
    original=module.append_audit_event
    monkeypatch.setattr(module,'append_audit_event',lambda *args,**kwargs:None)
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError,match='独立服务端授权'):
        create(db,run_id=old.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],reason='test',actor='test')
    db.rollback()
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun)) == 1


def test_old_recovery_replay_and_manual_resolution_blocked(failed):
    from app.failure_recovery import recovery_preview
    from app.workflow_runtime import retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint
    from app.runtime_queue import replay_dead_letter
    from app.failure_console import resolve_failure
    db,old,_=failed
    quote=preview(db,old.id)
    create(db,run_id=old.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],reason='test',actor='test')
    db.commit()
    item=db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).join(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==old.id))
    for action in (
        lambda:recovery_preview(db,old.id),
        lambda:retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db,old.id,actor='test'),
        lambda:replay_dead_letter(db,item.id,actor='test',reason='test'),
        lambda:resolve_failure(db,run_id=old.id,level='L3',score=50,fields={},reason='test',actor='test',idempotency_key='manual-conflict'),
    ):
        with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError,match='新运行'):
            action()
        db.rollback()


def test_reassessment_requires_settled_calls_and_no_existing_delivery(failed):
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    from app.failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution

def test_settlement_waits_for_real_delivery_and_keeps_old_failure(failed):
    from app.incremental_reassessment import settle
    from app.models import RuntimeOutboxEvent, DownstreamPublishLedger
    from app.runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox, claim_outbox_event, complete_outbox_event, DeliveryReceipt
    from app.failure_console import list_failures
    from test_runtime_outbox import _request
    db,old,newer=failed
    quote=preview(db,old.id)
    child=create(db,run_id=old.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],reason='test',actor='test')
    child.status='succeeded';db.flush()
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError,match='回执'):
        settle(db,run_id=old.id,actor='test')
    event=enqueue_runtime_outbox(db,_request(newer))
    db.add(DownstreamPublishLedger(outbox_event_id=event.id,target_contract_key=event.target_contract_key,
        business_idempotency_key=event.business_idempotency_key,mechanism_version_id=newer.id,
        origin_run_key=child.run_key,trigger_kind='auto_run',payload_hash=event.payload_hash))
    db.flush()
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError,match='回执'):
        settle(db,run_id=old.id,actor='test')
    claimed=claim_outbox_event(db,worker_id='test')
    complete_outbox_event(db,event.id,claimed.lease_token,DeliveryReceipt(business_idempotency_key=event.business_idempotency_key,
        mechanism_version_id=newer.id,payload_hash=event.payload_hash,external_record_key='accepted-test'))
    result=settle(db,run_id=old.id,actor='test');db.commit()
    assert old.status=='failed' and result['new_run_id']==child.id
    assert list_failures(db,resolved=False)['total']==0
    rows=list_failures(db,resolved=True)['items']
    assert len(rows)==1 and rows[0]['reassessment']['delivered']
    assert settle(db,run_id=old.id,actor='test')==result

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_routing.py =====
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import WorkflowVersion
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close


def test_frozen_entity_assignment_and_ratio_changes():
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, admit_entity, set_admission
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
        policy = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="验收", actor="test")
        assert admit_entity(db, **scope, source_system="fixture", entity_id="case-1") is None
        policy = set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=policy["revision"], paused=False, reason="开始", actor="test")
        identity = dict(**scope, source_system="fixture", entity_id="case-1")
        first = admit_entity(db, **identity)
        assert first["destination"] == "shadow"
        configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=policy["revision"], mode="shadow", ratio_percent=0, reason="缩小", actor="test")
        assert admit_entity(db, **identity) == first
        assert admit_entity(db, **scope, source_system="fixture", entity_id="case-2")["destination"] == "excluded"
        db.commit()
        db.expire_all()
        assert admit_entity(db, **identity) == first
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_split_requires_ownership_evidence_and_policy_cas():
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import IncrementalMechanismError
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
        for ratio in (-1, 101, True, 1.2):
            with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError):
                configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=ratio, reason="测试", actor="test")
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError, match="归属"):
            configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="split", ratio_percent=10, reason="测试", actor="test")
        configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=10, reason="测试", actor="test")
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError, match="版本"):
            configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=20, reason="测试", actor="test")
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

def test_sqlite_v102_upgrade_creates_routing_without_touching_binding():
    from app.models import IncrementalRoutingPolicy, IncrementalEntityAssignment, IncrementalShadowResult
    from app.migrations import run_migrations
    from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        db.close()
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            for model in (IncrementalShadowResult, IncrementalEntityAssignment, IncrementalRoutingPolicy):
                model.__table__.drop(connection)
            connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 103"))
            run_migrations(connection)
            run_migrations(connection)
            assert "incremental_entity_assignments" in inspect(connection).get_table_names()
            assert connection.execute(text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM category_incremental_mechanism_bindings")).scalar() == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_shadow_projection_is_persistent_idempotent_and_cannot_lose_route(monkeypatch):
    import json
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
    from app.models import ProductionRun, IncrementalShadowResult, RuntimeOutboxEvent
    from app.workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request, WorkflowRuntimeError
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _headers, _payload
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
        p = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="测试", actor="test")
        set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=p["revision"], paused=False, reason="测试", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        assert ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="shadow-result", asset_id=asset.id)).status_code == 200
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        node = next(n for n in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] if n["kind"] == "downstream_projection")
        for _ in range(2):
            assert build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, run, step_key=node["node_instance_id"], output_manifest={"score": 88}) is None
        db.commit()
        assert len(db.scalars(select(IncrementalShadowResult)).all()) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)) is None
        frozen = SimpleNamespace(**{key: getattr(run, key) for key in ("source_type", "source_id", "category_key", "workflow_definition_id")})
        snapshot["runtime_context"].pop("incremental_route")
        frozen.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError, match="归属"):
            build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, frozen, step_key=node["node_instance_id"], output_manifest={"score": 99})
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_handover_requires_pause_drain_and_migrates_only_selected_entities():
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission, admit_entity, handover
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import IncrementalMechanismError
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
        p = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="测试", actor="test")
        p = set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=p["revision"], paused=False, reason="测试", actor="test")
        first = admit_entity(db, **scope, source_system="fixture", entity_id="a")
        other = admit_entity(db, **scope, source_system="fixture", entity_id="b")
        args = dict(**scope, mode="split", ratio_percent=100, ownership_contract="RD-03-fixture", watermark="source-seq:42", assignment_ids=[first["assignment_id"]], destination="formal", reason="本地切换", actor="test")
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError, match="暂停"):
            handover(db, **args, expected_revision=p["revision"])
        p = set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=p["revision"], paused=True, reason="排空", actor="test")
        p = handover(db, **args, expected_revision=p["revision"])
        assert p["admission_paused"] is True
        p = set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=p["revision"], paused=False, reason="测试", actor="test")
        moved = admit_entity(db, **scope, source_system="fixture", entity_id="a")
        assert moved["ownership_epoch"] == first["ownership_epoch"] + 1
        assert moved["destination"] == "formal"
        assert admit_entity(db, **scope, source_system="fixture", entity_id="b") == other
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_policy_api_permissions_cards_and_explicit_admission():
    from test_incremental_automation import _operator
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        root = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{definition}"
        payload = dict(expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=25, reason="本地配置")
        assert _operator(db, "manager").put(root + "/policy", json=payload).status_code == 403
        client = _operator(db)
        response = client.put(root + "/policy", json=payload)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["admission_paused"] is True
        assert client.put(root + "/policy", json=payload).status_code == 409
        response = client.put(root + "/admission", json=dict(expected_revision=1, paused=False, reason="本地启动"))
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        card = next(c for c in client.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards").json()["items"] if c["bound"])
        assert card["routing"]["policy"]["ratio_percent"] == 25
        assert card["routing"]["formal_takeover_percent"] == 0
        assert card["routing"]["actual_sample_percent"] is None
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_physical_delivery_rejects_shadow_outbox_before_adapter_io(monkeypatch):
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
    from app.models import ProductionRun, ConnectorContractVersion
    from app.runtime_outbox import RuntimeOutboxRequest, enqueue_runtime_outbox
    from app.connector_delivery import deliver_outbox_batch, FixtureDownstreamAdapter, ConnectorDeliveryError
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _headers, _payload
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id)
        p = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="测试", actor="test")
        set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=p["revision"], paused=False, reason="测试", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        assert ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="shadow-fence", asset_id=asset.id)).status_code == 200
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        target = db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction == "downstream"))
        poisoned = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, RuntimeOutboxRequest(event_key="forged-shadow", target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=target.id, mechanism_version_id=version.id, business_idempotency_key="fixture:a",
            payload={"origin_run_key": run.run_key, "result": {"score": 88}}))
        class NoIO(FixtureDownstreamAdapter):
            def verify_target(self):
                pytest.fail("影子结果不得触发正式连接器 I/O")
        result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="fence-test", adapter=NoIO(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint))
        db.commit()
        db.expire_all()
        assert result.blocked_event_ids == (poisoned.id,)
        assert poisoned.status == "dead_lettered"
        assert poisoned.last_error_code == "incremental_destination_fenced"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_resume_recovers_explicit_events_and_duplicate_never_replays_models(monkeypatch):
    from test_incremental_automation import _operator
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _headers, _payload
    from app.models import ContentIngressEvent, ProductionRun
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        root = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{definition}"
        client = _operator(db)
        assert client.put(root + "/policy", json=dict(expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="测试")).status_code == 200
        response = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="recover-admission", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.json()["routing_status"] == "routing_admission_paused"
        event = db.scalar(select(ContentIngressEvent))
        assert client.put(root + "/admission", json=dict(expected_revision=1, paused=False, reason="就绪")).status_code == 200
        args = dict(event_ids=[event.id], reason="补受理", expected_revision=2)
        response = client.post(root + "/recover", json=args)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run is not None
        assert client.post(root + "/recover", json=args).status_code == 200
        assert len(db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all()) == 1
        trace = client.get(root + "/trace", params={"source_system": "upstream-sim", "entity_id": "asset-1"})
        assert trace.status_code == 200, trace.text
        assert trace.json()["events"][0]["runs"][0]["run_key"] == run.run_key
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("guard", ["new_source", "human_review"])
def test_formal_projection_cannot_overwrite_new_source_or_review(monkeypatch, guard):
    import json
    from datetime import datetime, timedelta
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
    from app.models import ProductionRun
    from app.workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request, WorkflowRuntimeError
    from app.runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _headers, _payload
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
        p = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="split", ratio_percent=100, ownership_contract="local-RD03", reason="测试", actor="test")
        set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=p["revision"], paused=False, reason="测试", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        payload = _payload(event_id="formal-old", asset_id=asset.id)
        assert ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload).status_code == 200
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        step = next(n["node_instance_id"] for n in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] if n["kind"] == "downstream_projection")
        if guard == "new_source":
            payload["event_id"] = "formal-new"
            payload["payload"]["content_version"] = "v2"
            payload["occurred_at"] = (datetime.fromisoformat(payload["occurred_at"].replace("Z", "+00:00")) + timedelta(seconds=1)).isoformat()
            assert ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload).status_code == 200
        else:
            reviewed = build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, run, step_key=step, output_manifest={"score": 99}, review_state="reviewed")
            enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, reviewed)
            db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError, match="源版本|人工"):
            build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, run, step_key=step, output_manifest={"score": 1})
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_rebinding_without_policy_cannot_bypass_existing_shadow_ownership(monkeypatch):
    import hashlib
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.models import WorkflowDefinition, ProductionRun
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission, admit_entity
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism, get_incremental_mechanism_binding
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _headers, _payload
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        first, second = db.scalars(select(WorkflowVersion).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id)).all()
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=first.workflow_definition_id)
        p = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="测试", actor="test")
        set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=p["revision"], paused=False, reason="测试", actor="test")
        admit_entity(db, **scope, source_system="upstream-sim", entity_id="asset-1")
        other = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key="other.incremental", name="另一工作流", owner="test", created_by="test", allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]')
        db.add(other); db.flush()
        second.workflow_definition_id = other.id
        second.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(second)).encode()).hexdigest()
        binding = get_incremental_mechanism_binding(db, "space_image")
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_version_id=second.id,
            expected_lock_version=binding.lock_version, reason="更换工作流", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        response = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="rebound", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "routing_handover_required"
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)) is None
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_shared_fence_mysql.py =====
"""Opt-in real MySQL lock acceptance; use an isolated loopback maintenance DB.

Reuses the guarded random-schema fixture, never a production schema.
"""
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from test_case_image_grades_mysql84_acceptance import isolated_mysql84
from app.incremental_automation import automation_paused, set_automation_control, _lock_definition
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.incremental_routing import validate_run_route
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.models import IncrementalAutomationControl
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


@pytest.fixture
def workflow(isolated_mysql84):
    engine=isolated_mysql84
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version=seed_incremental_versions(db,count=1)[0]
        set_incremental_mechanism(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_version_id=version.id,
            expected_lock_version=None,reason='isolated lock test',actor='test')
        ids=(version.id,version.workflow_definition_id)
        db.commit()
    return engine,ids


@pytest.mark.parametrize('existing_control',[False,True])
def test_two_dispatch_checks_share_fence_but_pause_waits(workflow,existing_control):
    engine,(version,definition)=workflow
    if existing_control:
        with Session(engine) as seed:
            seed.add(IncrementalAutomationControl(category_key='space_image',
                workflow_definition_id=definition,enabled=True,revision=1,reason='seed',updated_by='test'))
            seed.commit()
    scope=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_version_id=version)
    pause=dict(category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=definition,enabled=False,
        expected_revision=1 if existing_control else 0,reason='pause fence',actor='test')
    with Session(engine) as first,Session(engine) as second,Session(engine) as writer:
        second.execute(text('SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout=1'))
        writer.execute(text('SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout=1'))
        assert automation_paused(first,**scope) is False
        # A second independent dispatch must not wait for the first to commit.
        assert automation_paused(second,**scope) is False
        with pytest.raises(OperationalError) as blocked:
            set_automation_control(writer,**pause)
        assert blocked.value.orig.args[0]==1205
        writer.rollback();first.commit();second.commit()
        set_automation_control(writer,**pause);writer.commit()
        assert automation_paused(first,**scope) is True


def test_route_readers_coexist_but_ownership_writer_is_fenced(workflow):
    engine,(_,definition)=workflow
    run=SimpleNamespace(workflow_definition_id=definition,snapshot_json='{"runtime_context":{}}',
        source_type='manual',source_id=None,category_key='space_image')
    with Session(engine) as first,Session(engine) as second,Session(engine) as writer:
        second.execute(text('SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout=1'))
        writer.execute(text('SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout=1'))
        assert validate_run_route(first,run) is None
        assert validate_run_route(second,run) is None
        with pytest.raises(OperationalError) as blocked:
            _lock_definition(writer,definition)
        assert blocked.value.orig.args[0]==1205
        writer.rollback();first.commit();second.commit()
        assert _lock_definition(writer,definition).id==definition
        with pytest.raises(OperationalError) as blocked_reader:
            validate_run_route(second,run)
        assert blocked_reader.value.orig.args[0]==1205
        second.rollback();writer.commit()
        assert validate_run_route(second,run) is None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_sla.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import ContentIngressEvent, ProductionRun, RuntimeDispatchItem
from app.runtime_queue import ClaimRequest, claim_runtime_item
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _close, _fixture, _headers, _payload


def intake(client, db, asset, headers, number):
    payload = _payload(event_id=f'sla-{number}', asset_id=asset.id)
    payload['payload']['content_id'] = f'sla-material-{number}'
    response = client.post('/api/content-ingress/events', headers=headers, json=payload)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(
        ProductionRun.run_key == response.json()['production_run_key']))


def test_real_creation_freezes_authoritative_first_enqueue_not_upstream_time(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '1')
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES', 'space_image')
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        run = intake(client, db, asset, _headers(monkeypatch), 1)
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
        frozen = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)['runtime_context']['incremental_sla']
        first = event.received_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        assert frozen == {'version': 'incremental-sla-v1', 'service_class': 'high',
                          'source_event_id': event.id, 'first_queued_at': first.isoformat(),
                          'deadline_at': (first + timedelta(hours=5)).isoformat()}
        assert first != event.occurred_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        old_snapshot = run.snapshot_json
        monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES', 'other_category')
        dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem))
        dispatch.available_at = first + timedelta(hours=6)
        db.commit()
        # Re-delivery and requeue do not create a new clock or rewrite the run.
        assert intake(client, db, asset, _headers(monkeypatch), 1).id == run.id
        assert run.snapshot_json == old_snapshot
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_mixed_real_queue_reserves_every_fifth_claim_for_oldest_normal(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '1')
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
        monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES', 'other_category')
        normal = [intake(client, db, asset, headers, i) for i in (1, 2)]
        monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES', 'space_image')
        high = [intake(client, db, asset, headers, i) for i in range(3, 8)]
        claims = []
        for i in range(7):
            claim = claim_runtime_item(db, ClaimRequest(
                worker_id=f'sla-{i}', global_limit=32,
                now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=1)))
            assert claim is not None
            claims.append(claim.run_id)
            db.commit()
        assert claims[:5] == [r.id for r in high[:4]] + [normal[0].id]
        assert claims[5:] == [high[4].id, normal[1].id]  # borrowing, no idle quota
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_sla_default_off_and_caller_cannot_self_assign_priority(monkeypatch):
    from app.incremental_sla import enabled, freeze_incremental_sla
    monkeypatch.delenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', raising=False)
    assert not enabled()
    assert freeze_incremental_sla(None, SimpleNamespace(source_type='operator')) is None
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', 'true')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='0 or 1'):
        enabled()
