def test_redline_hit_screenshot_short_circuits():
    result = _run(_precheck(reason=["是截图"]))
    assert result["redline"]["hit"] is True
    assert result["redline"]["hit_rules"] == ["screenshot"]
    assert result["resolved"] is None
    agg = result["result"]
    assert agg["hard_reject"] is True
    assert agg["terminated_at"] == "redline"
    assert agg["level"] == "L5"
    assert agg["score"] == 20
    _assert_json_serializable(result)


def test_architecture_grade5_real_photo_class_one_100_l1():
    result = _run(_precheck(category="建筑设计", trait="实景照片", confidence=0.95))
    assert result["redline"]["hit"] is False
    assert result["resolved"]["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_ONE
    assert result["resolved"]["resolved_by"] == "mapped"
    agg = result["result"]
    assert agg["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_ONE
    assert agg["base_score"] == 40
    assert agg["dimension_max"] == 60
    assert agg["score"] == 100
    assert agg["level"] == "L1"
    assert agg["hard_reject"] is False
    _assert_json_serializable(result)


def test_product_grade5_class_two_capped_80():
    result = _run(_precheck(category="产品设计", trait="实景照片", confidence=0.95))
    assert result["resolved"]["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_TWO
    agg = result["result"]
    assert agg["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_TWO
    assert agg["base_score"] == 20
    assert agg["dimension_max"] == 60
    # 20 + 60 = 80, at (and never above) the class_two track cap of 80.
    assert agg["score"] <= 80
    assert agg["score"] == 80
    assert agg["level"] == "L2"
    _assert_json_serializable(result)


def test_low_confidence_falls_back_to_class_three_default():
    # 建筑设计 would map to class_one, but confidence < min_confidence (0.6)
    # forces the default track (class_three).
    result = _run(_precheck(category="建筑设计", trait="实景照片", confidence=0.4))
    assert result["resolved"]["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_THREE
    assert result["resolved"]["resolved_by"] == "low_confidence"
    assert result["resolved"]["needs_review"] is True
    agg = result["result"]
    assert agg["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_THREE
    assert agg["base_score"] == 40
    assert agg["dimension_max"] == 30
    # 40 + 30 = 70 = track cap; all-5 grades → no deductions.
    assert agg["score"] == 70
    assert agg["level"] == "L2"
    _assert_json_serializable(result)


def test_media_type_penalty_is_disabled_for_new_inspiration_contract():
    real = _run(_precheck(category="建筑设计", trait="实景照片", confidence=0.95))
    ai = _run(_precheck(category="建筑设计", trait="AI图", confidence=0.95))
    # Both resolve to class_one; only the media type differs.
    assert real["result"]["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_ONE
    assert ai["result"]["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_ONE
    # 新体系只保留 trait 供下游消费，不按媒介扣分。
    assert real["result"]["score"] == 100
    assert ai["result"]["score"] == 100
    assert real["result"]["score"] == ai["result"]["score"]
    assert ai["result"]["media_penalty_enabled"] is False
    assert any(s["step"] == "media_skipped" for s in ai["result"]["steps"])
    _assert_json_serializable(ai)


def test_out_of_scope_routes_to_class_three():
    result = _run(_precheck(category="任意", scope_status="out_of_scope", confidence=0.95))
    assert result["resolved"]["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_THREE
    assert result["resolved"]["resolved_by"] == "out_of_scope"


def test_evaluate_one_is_deterministic():
    precheck = _precheck(category="建筑设计", trait="实景照片", confidence=0.95)
    first = _run(precheck)
    second = _run(precheck)
    assert first == second


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 3. 方案 A 真实 6/5 维度：key/权重/结构断言 + hard_defects 一票压分。
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

_WEIGHT_TOLERANCE = 1e-9


def _common_dims(config: dict) -> list[dict]:
    return config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]


def test_class_one_two_have_six_real_dimensions_with_raw_business_weights():
    configs = build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()
    for track_key in (TRACK_CLASS_ONE, TRACK_CLASS_TWO):
        config = configs[track_key]
        dims = _common_dims(config)
        assert [d["key"] for d in dims] == list(_CLASS_ONE_TWO_KEYS)
        assert config["dimension_max"] == 60
        # 全部维度在 common_group，specific_group 为空。
        assert config["common_group"]["group_weight"] == 1.0
        assert _common_dims and not config["specific_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]
        assert [d["weight"] for d in dims] == [0.10, 0.10, 0.05, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15]
        assert abs(sum(d["weight"] for d in dims) - 0.60) <= _WEIGHT_TOLERANCE
        for d in dims:
            assert d["grade_points"] == {"1": 0.0, "2": 25.0, "3": 50.0, "4": 75.0, "5": 100.0}


def test_class_three_has_five_real_dimensions_with_raw_business_weights():
    config = build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()[TRACK_CLASS_THREE]
    dims = _common_dims(config)
    assert [d["key"] for d in dims] == list(_CLASS_THREE_KEYS)
    assert config["dimension_max"] == 30
    assert config["common_group"]["group_weight"] == 1.0
    assert not config["specific_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]
    assert abs(sum(d["weight"] for d in dims) - 0.30) <= _WEIGHT_TOLERANCE
    # 业务权重每维 0.06，对应 100 分维度分中的 6 分。
    for d in dims:
        assert abs(d["weight"] - 0.06) <= _WEIGHT_TOLERANCE


def test_contract_freezes_level_boundaries_and_versions_rev3_rev4_actions():
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    assert contract["spec_version"] == "inspiration-v3-aesthetic-evidence-v4-quality-gates-20260807"
    assert contract["level_thresholds"] == [
        {"min_score": 90, "level": "L1"},
        {"min_score": 75, "level": "L2"},
        {"min_score": 60, "level": "L3"},
        {"min_score": 0, "level": "L4"},
    ]
    modifiers = contract["common_modifiers"]
    assert modifiers["format_version"] == "common-modifiers-v2"
    assert modifiers["media_type_penalty"]["enabled"] is False
    veto = modifiers["high_score_veto"]
    assert veto["policy_version"] == "hard-defect-severity-v1"
    assert veto["tiers"]["A"]["cap_to"] == 20
    assert veto["tiers"]["B"]["cap_to"] == 60
    assert veto["tiers"]["record_only"]["action"] == "record_only"

    rev3 = build_inspiration_v3_rev3_contract()
    assert rev3["spec_version"] == "inspiration-v2-human-calibrated-20260805"
    legacy_veto = rev3["common_modifiers"]["high_score_veto"]
    assert (legacy_veto["threshold"], legacy_veto["cap_to"]) == (80, 79)
    assert len(legacy_veto["rules"]) == 10


def test_level_mapping_boundaries_80_is_l2_and_81_is_l1():
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_rev3_contract()
    precheck = _precheck(category="建筑设计", trait="实景照片")
    score_80 = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract, precheck, {"deductions": {"d": 20}}, track_key=TRACK_CLASS_ONE
    )
    score_81 = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract, precheck, {"deductions": {"d": 19}}, track_key=TRACK_CLASS_ONE
    )
    assert (score_80["score"], score_80["level"]) == (80, "L2")
    assert (score_81["score"], score_81["level"]) == (81, "L1")


def test_rev3_high_score_veto_replays_cap_79():
    # 历史 rev3 一类全 grade5 命中硬伤仍按冻结行为压至 79。
    result = _run(
        _precheck(
            category="建筑设计",
            trait="实景照片",
            confidence=0.95,
            hard_defects=["garish_color"],
        ),
        contract=build_inspiration_v3_rev3_contract(),
    )
    agg = result["result"]
    assert agg["track_key"] == TRACK_CLASS_ONE
    assert agg["score"] == 79
    # raw_level（未压分前）仍是 L1，压分后落 L2。
    assert agg["raw_level"] == "L1"
    assert agg["level"] == "L2"
    veto_cap = next(c for c in agg["caps"] if c["cap"] == "high_score_veto")
    assert "79" in veto_cap["reason"]
    _assert_json_serializable(result)


def test_high_score_veto_not_triggered_without_hard_defects():
    # 无 hard_defects → 不压分，维持 100/L1（验证压分是条件性的）。
    result = _run(_precheck(category="建筑设计", trait="实景照片", confidence=0.95))
    assert result["result"]["score"] == 100
    assert result["result"]["level"] == "L1"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_comparison_release.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import PromptVersion, WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database


def _manifest(db: Session, key: str) -> dict:
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == key)
    )
    assert definition is not None
    workflow = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id
        )
    )
    assert workflow is not None and workflow.status == "draft"
    return persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)


def test_ab_and_single_keep_identical_non_prompt_evaluation_chain(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="system:comparison-test", apply=True)
            db.commit()
            ab = _manifest(db, "inspiration_image.latest_ab_comparison")
            single = _manifest(db, "inspiration_image.latest_single_comparison")

            def non_prompt_signature(manifest: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, int | None]]:
                return [
                    (
                        node["node_instance_id"],
                        node["kind"],
                        node["config"].get("rule_module_version_id"),
                    )
                    for node in manifest["nodes"]
                    if node["kind"] != "prompt"
                ]

            assert non_prompt_signature(ab) == non_prompt_signature(single)
            assert ab["resource_policy"]["source_provenance"] == single[
                "resource_policy"
            ]["source_provenance"]
            single_node = next(
                node for node in single["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, single_node["config"]["prompt_version_id"])
            assert prompt is not None
            assert "A/B 两阶段职责" in prompt.system_prompt
            assert single_node["config"]["thinking_level"] == "none"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_exemption_consistency.py =====
"""run416: an explicitly exempt visual fact is not a conflicting redline."""
from copy import deepcopy
import pytest
from app.schema_adapter import adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck
from app.inspiration_semantic_consistency import _latest_redline_policy
from test_inspiration_semantic_consistency import _consistent_payload


def _case(flag=True, reason=False, evidence=False):
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload['redline_triggered']['multi_image_collage'] = True
    payload['redline_exempted'] = {'multi_image_collage': flag}
    payload['reason'] = ['是多拼图'] if reason else []
    payload['decisive_evidence']['redline_triggered'] = (
        [{'key': 'multi_image_collage', 'evidence': '同一作品的局部展示'}] if evidence else [])
    policy = _latest_redline_policy()
    next(r for r in policy['rules'] if r['key']=='multi_image_collage')['exemption_flag'] = 'multi_image_collage'
    return payload, policy


@pytest.mark.parametrize('reason,evidence', [(False, False), (True, True), (True, False), (False, True)])
def test_frozen_exemption_accepts_fact_with_or_without_elimination_reason(reason, evidence):
    payload, policy = _case(reason=reason,evidence=evidence)
    original=deepcopy(payload)
    result=adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(payload,redline_policy=policy,image_defects_enabled=False)
    assert result['decisive_signal_validation']=={'status':'valid','reasons':[]}
    assert payload==original
    assert result['redline_triggered']==original['redline_triggered']
    assert result['reason']==original['reason']
    assert result['decisive_evidence']==original['decisive_evidence']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('flag',[False, 'true', 1, None])
def test_missing_or_nonboolean_exemption_remains_fail_closed(flag):
    payload,policy=_case(flag)
    result=adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(payload,redline_policy=policy,image_defects_enabled=False)
    assert result['decisive_signal_validation']['status']=='needs_review'
    assert 'missing_evidence:redline:multi_image_collage' in result['review_reasons']


def test_unfrozen_exemption_and_other_redlines_are_not_silenced():
    payload,policy=_case()
    next(r for r in policy['rules'] if r['key']=='multi_image_collage').pop('exemption_flag')
    assert adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(payload,redline_policy=policy,image_defects_enabled=False)['needs_review']
    payload,policy=_case()
    payload['redline_triggered']['screenshot']=True
    result=adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(payload,redline_policy=policy,image_defects_enabled=False)
    assert 'missing_evidence:redline:screenshot' in result['review_reasons']


def test_exemption_does_not_accept_malformed_boolean_or_evidence():
    payload,policy=_case()
    payload['redline_triggered']['multi_image_collage']='true'
    payload['decisive_evidence']['redline_triggered']=[{'key':'multi_image_collage','evidence':''}]
    result=adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(payload,redline_policy=policy,image_defects_enabled=False)
    assert 'invalid:redline_triggered:multi_image_collage' in result['review_reasons']
    assert 'invalid:evidence:redline:multi_image_collage' in result['review_reasons']


def test_exemption_does_not_hide_negative_fact_conflict_or_another_rule_on_same_signal():
    payload,policy=_case(reason=True)
    payload['redline_triggered']['multi_image_collage']=False
    result=adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(payload,redline_policy=policy,image_defects_enabled=False)
    assert 'redline_reason_conflict:multi_image_collage' in result['review_reasons']
    payload,policy=_case()
    policy['rules'].append({'key':'other_collage','match_any':['是多拼图'],'enabled':True})
    result=adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(payload,redline_policy=policy,image_defects_enabled=False)
    assert 'missing_evidence:redline:multi_image_collage' in result['review_reasons']

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_factory_access.py =====
"""Testing a factory API must never enable that factory's production writes."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

from app.factory_test_claim import (INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL as PRODUCTION_URL,
    INSPIRATION_TEST_URL as TEST_URL, ClaimGateError)
from app.inspiration_factory_access import (
    ENABLED_ENV, TEST_ENABLED_ENV, inspiration_endpoint_enabled,
)


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def closed(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv(ENABLED_ENV, raising=False)
    monkeypatch.delenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, raising=False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('deployment', ['production', 'test', 'local'])
def test_api_environment_is_independent_of_platform_host(monkeypatch, deployment):
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV', deployment)
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(TEST_URL)
    monkeypatch.setenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    assert inspiration_endpoint_enabled(TEST_URL)
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(PRODUCTION_URL)
    for other in (None, {}, TEST_URL+'/', TEST_URL+'?environment=test',
                  'http://userapitest.3d66.com/api/factory', 'http://192.168.1.137/api/factory'):
        assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(other)
    monkeypatch.setenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, '0')
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(TEST_URL)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    assert inspiration_endpoint_enabled(PRODUCTION_URL)


def test_production_intake_and_platform_controls_stay_closed_without_io(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from app.inspiration_factory_intake import stream_cycle
    from app.factory_material_intake import material_platform_controls
    monkeypatch.setenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    def forbidden(*args, **kwargs):
        pytest.fail('production must be rejected before network, database or control access')
    path = tmp_path/'absent'/'journal.sqlite'
    assert stream_cycle(path, sessions=forbidden, controls=forbidden,
        upstream=PRODUCTION_URL, daily_limit_micros=10, per_task_micros=1,
        send=forbidden)['network_calls'] == 0
    assert not any(material_platform_controls(forbidden, journal=path,
        workflow_version_id=1, upstream_contract_id=1, downstream_contract_id=2,
        expected_workflow_hash='a'*64, expected_mode='real', confirm_enabled=True,
        expected_upstream=PRODUCTION_URL, category_key='inspiration_image').values())
    assert not path.parent.exists()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('action', ['confirmClaim', 'submit'])
def test_low_level_production_writes_cannot_bypass_endpoint_switch(monkeypatch, action):
    from app import inspiration_factory_transport as transport
    monkeypatch.setenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    def forbidden(*args, **kwargs):
        pytest.fail('no HTTP opener may be constructed')
    monkeypatch.setattr(transport, 'build_opener', forbidden)
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError, match='operator_verification_pending'):
        transport.send_inspiration_request(PRODUCTION_URL, {
            'service':'LgtInternalLLM', 'action':action, 'time':1, 'key':'test', 'body':{},
        })


def test_cli_test_switch_rejects_production_config_before_database_import(tmp_path):
    config = {key:1 for key in (
        'workflow_version_id','upstream_contract_id','downstream_contract_id',
        'daily_limit_micros','per_task_micros','max_requests_ceiling','max_pending','per_cycle')}
    config.update(journal=str(tmp_path/'journal.sqlite'), upstream=PRODUCTION_URL,
                  expected_workflow_hash='a'*64, expected_mode='real', confirm_enabled=True)
    path = tmp_path/'config.json'
    path.write_text(json.dumps(config)); path.chmod(0o600)
    env = dict(os.environ, DATABASE_URL='invalid://must-not-be-opened',
               **{TEST_ENABLED_ENV:'1', ENABLED_ENV:'0'})
    script = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]/'scripts/inspiration-factory-stream.py'
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(script), '--enable-process', '--config', str(path)],
        env=env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    assert result.returncode == 2 and 'API endpoint is not enabled' in result.stderr
    assert 'sqlalchemy' not in result.stderr and not (tmp_path/'journal.sqlite').exists()


def test_inspiration_test_domain_is_inspiration_only(monkeypatch):
    from app.factory_test_claim import (
        INSPIRATION_TEST_URL, INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS, UPSTREAMS,
        inspiration_upstream_prefixes, upstream_prefixes,
    )
    assert INSPIRATION_TEST_URL == 'https://factory-api-test.3d66.com/api/factory'
    assert INSPIRATION_TEST_URL not in UPSTREAMS and INSPIRATION_TEST_URL in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS
    # 评审 T1：灵感图只认自己的两个域名，3DSU 的 userapi/userapitest/mock 一个都不继承
    assert not set(UPSTREAMS) & set(INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS) and len(INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS) == 2
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError):
        upstream_prefixes(INSPIRATION_TEST_URL)
    prefixes = inspiration_upstream_prefixes(INSPIRATION_TEST_URL)
    others = {p for url, p in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS.items() if url != INSPIRATION_TEST_URL}
    assert prefixes.identity == 'factory-lgt-test' and prefixes not in others
    assert len({p.identity for p in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS.values()}) == len(INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS)
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(INSPIRATION_TEST_URL)
    monkeypatch.setenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    assert inspiration_endpoint_enabled(INSPIRATION_TEST_URL)
    assert inspiration_endpoint_enabled(TEST_URL)
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(PRODUCTION_URL)
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(INSPIRATION_TEST_URL + '/')


def test_inspiration_test_domain_resolves_for_inspiration_locator_only(monkeypatch):
    from app.factory_submit_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError, resolve_locator_endpoint
    from app.factory_test_claim import INSPIRATION_TEST_URL, INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_LGT_TEST', INSPIRATION_TEST_URL)
    assert resolve_locator_endpoint('lgt-test', allowed=INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS) == INSPIRATION_TEST_URL
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError):
        resolve_locator_endpoint('lgt-test')


def test_inspiration_identity_uses_own_prefixes_for_test_domain():
    from app.factory_test_claim import INSPIRATION_TEST_URL, TEST_URL as THREE_D_TEST_URL
    from app.inspiration_factory_identity import content_identity, validate_frozen_identity
    task = {'task_id': 7, 'cloud_id': 3, 'project_id': 'TPWL-8905-anli', 'model_source': 2,
            'image_url': 'https://img.example.com/7.jpg'}
    content, version, submission = content_identity(task, url=INSPIRATION_TEST_URL)
    assert content == 'inspiration-factory-lgt-test:3:TPWL-8905-anli:task:7'
    assert version == 'inspiration-lgt-test-task:7' and submission == 'inspiration-lgt-test-task:7'
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError):
        content_identity(task, url=THREE_D_TEST_URL)
    frozen = {'source_system': 'inspiration-factory-material', 'category_key': 'inspiration_image',
              'source_content_id': content, 'source_version': version}
    assert validate_frozen_identity(frozen, task)['project_id'] == 'TPWL-8905-anli'


def test_inspiration_production_domain_needs_formal_switch_only(monkeypatch):
    from app.factory_test_claim import (
        INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL, INSPIRATION_TEST_URL, INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS, UPSTREAMS,
        inspiration_upstream_prefixes,
    )
    assert INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL == 'https://factory-api.3d66.com/api/factory'
    assert INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS and INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL not in UPSTREAMS
    prefixes = inspiration_upstream_prefixes(INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)
    assert prefixes.identity == 'factory-lgt-prod' and prefixes != inspiration_upstream_prefixes(INSPIRATION_TEST_URL)
    assert len({p.identity for p in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS.values()}) == len(INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS)
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)
    monkeypatch.setenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    assert inspiration_endpoint_enabled(INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('three_d_url', ['https://userapitest.3d66.com/api/factory', 'https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory'])
def test_three_d_domains_are_never_inspiration_targets(monkeypatch, three_d_url):
    """评审 T1：灵感图端点变量误配成 3DSU 域名时，进料、投递、放行都要拒绝，不能把灵感图结果打到 3DSU 工厂。"""
    from app.factory_submit_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError, resolve_locator_endpoint
    from app.factory_test_claim import INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS, inspiration_upstream_prefixes
    from app.inspiration_factory_delivery import InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    monkeypatch.setenv(TEST_ENABLED_ENV, '1')
    assert not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(three_d_url)
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError):
        inspiration_upstream_prefixes(three_d_url)
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_LGT_MISCONFIGURED', three_d_url)
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError):
        resolve_locator_endpoint('lgt-misconfigured', allowed=INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(three_d_url, level_mapping={}, review_result_values={}, journal_path='/nonexistent')


def test_long_project_id_is_compacted_only_when_the_identity_would_overflow():
    from app.factory_test_claim import INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL
    from app.inspiration_factory_identity import content_identity, identity_fits, validate_frozen_identity
    big = 2**63 - 1  # 合同允许的最大 ID，19 位
    task = {'task_id': big, 'cloud_id': big, 'project_id': 'X' * 120, 'model_source': 2,
            'image_url': 'https://img.example.com/1.jpg'}
    content, version, submission = content_identity(task, url=INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)
    assert len(content) <= 160 and identity_fits(content, version, submission)
    assert content == content_identity(dict(task), url=INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)[0]
    assert content != content_identity(dict(task, project_id='Y' * 120), url=INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)[0]
    frozen = {'source_system': 'inspiration-factory-material', 'category_key': 'inspiration_image',
              'source_content_id': content, 'source_version': version}
    assert validate_frozen_identity(frozen, task)['project_id'] == 'X' * 120
    short = dict(task, task_id=7, cloud_id=3, project_id='TPWL-8905-anli')
    assert content_identity(short, url=INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL)[0] == 'inspiration-factory-lgt-prod:3:TPWL-8905-anli:task:7'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_factory_cli.py =====
"""Operator hold cannot be bypassed by merely installing/enabling the script."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import pytest


@pytest.mark.parametrize('process_flag,environment',[(False,'1'),(True,'0'),(True,None)])
def test_disabled_cli_never_loads_configuration_or_database(tmp_path,process_flag,environment):
    script=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]/'scripts/inspiration-factory-stream.py'
    env=dict(os.environ,DATABASE_URL='invalid://must-not-be-opened')
    if environment is None:env.pop('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED',None)
    else:env['LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED']=environment
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(script),'--config',str(tmp_path/'absent.json'),
        *(['--enable-process'] if process_flag else [])],env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=10)
    assert json.loads(result.stdout)==dict(enabled=False,network_calls=0,stop='operator_verification_pending')


class _Clock:
    def __init__(self): self.t = 0.0
    def now(self): return self.t
    def sleep(self, s): self.t += s


def test_intake_loop_runs_several_cycles_and_sums_inspiration_counters():
    """09-30 并发复盘：单次调用只跑一轮是进料天花板（约 820 条/小时）。循环内每轮都是完整 cycle，
    上游没料或到 pending 上限立刻收手；灵感图特有计数逐轮累加。"""
    from app.inspiration_factory_intake import run_intake_loop
    clock = _Clock()
    outs = iter([dict(material_enqueued=10, confirmed=10, network_calls=40, identity_rejected=0, material_rejected=1),
                 dict(material_enqueued=10, confirmed=10, network_calls=40, identity_rejected=1, material_rejected=0),
                 dict(material_enqueued=3, confirmed=3, network_calls=12, stop='pending_limit')])
    def cycle():
        clock.t += 5
        return next(outs)
    out = run_intake_loop(cycle, loop_seconds=50, interval_seconds=2, clock=clock.now, sleep=clock.sleep)
    assert out['loops'] == 3 and out['material_enqueued'] == 23 and out['network_calls'] == 92
    assert out['identity_rejected'] == 1 and out['material_rejected'] == 1 and out['stop'] == 'pending_limit'
    empty = run_intake_loop(lambda: dict(material_enqueued=0, network_calls=4), loop_seconds=50, interval_seconds=2,
                            clock=_Clock().now, sleep=lambda s: None)
    assert empty['loops'] == 1  # 上游没料：不空转


@pytest.mark.parametrize('extra', [{'loop_seconds': 56}, {'loop_seconds': '5'}, {'cycle_interval_seconds': 0}, {'unknown_key': 1}])
def test_cli_rejects_bad_optional_loop_config_before_touching_the_database(tmp_path, extra):
    script = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]/'scripts/inspiration-factory-stream.py'
    config = {k: 1 for k in ('workflow_version_id','upstream_contract_id','downstream_contract_id','daily_limit_micros',
                             'per_task_micros','max_requests_ceiling','max_pending','per_cycle')}
    config.update(journal='/tmp/x.sqlite', upstream='https://factory-api.3d66.com/api/factory', expected_workflow_hash='0'*64,
                  expected_mode='real', confirm_enabled=True, **extra)
    path = tmp_path/'cfg.json'; path.write_text(json.dumps(config)); path.chmod(0o600)
    env = dict(os.environ, DATABASE_URL='invalid://must-not-be-opened', LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED='1')
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(script), '--config', str(path), '--enable-process'],
                            env=env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    assert result.returncode == 2 and ('loop' in result.stderr or 'cycle interval' in result.stderr or 'exact inspiration configuration' in result.stderr)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_factory_contract.py =====
"""Offline contract tests using the four 2026-09-24 inspiration Apifox texts."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app import inspiration_factory_contract as c


IDENTITY = {"task_id": 10001, "cloud_id": 20001, "project_id": 30001,
            "image_url": "https://img.example.com/inspiration/10001.jpg", "model_source": 2}
MAPPING = {level: {"grade": grade, "review_result": result}
           for level, grade, result in (
               ("L1", 7, "pass"), ("L2", 8, "pass"), ("L3", 9, "pass"),
               ("L4", 10, "reject"), ("L5", 11, "reject"))}
A_FIELDS = {"title": "现代客厅设计", "seo_title": "现代客厅灵感图",
            "class_text": "室内设计-客厅", "style": "现代",
            "tags": ["客厅", "现代", "室内设计"], "cons": "", "design": "空间层次清晰",
            "reason": "", "trait": "实景照片", "image_defects": ""}


def envelope(data):
    return {"code": 200, "msg": "请求成功", "data": data}


def output():
    return {"inspiration_task": deepcopy(IDENTITY), "a_fields": deepcopy(A_FIELDS),
            "final_score": 86, "final_level": "L1"}


def readback(body=None):
    body = body or c.build_submit_body(output(), level_mapping=MAPPING)
    return {**IDENTITY, "status": 4, "result_status": 1, "review_result": 1,
            "error_message": None,
            **{key: deepcopy(body[key]) for key in c.SUBMIT_FIELDS}}


def test_json_envelope_signature_and_action_bodies():
    for action, body in (("claim", {"limit": 10}), ("getTask", {"task_id": 10001}),
                         ("confirmClaim", {"task_ids": [10001]}),
                         ("submit", c.build_submit_body(output(), level_mapping=MAPPING)),
                         ("submit", {"task_id": 10001, "result_status": "failed",
                                     "error_message": "image unavailable"})):
        sent = c.request_envelope(action, body, 1727000000)
        assert sent == {"service": "LgtInternalLLM", "action": action, "time": 1727000000,
                        "key": hashlib.md5(("LgtInternalLLM" + action + "1727000000")[::-1].encode()).hexdigest(),
                        "body": body}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("action,body,now", [
    ("claim", {"limit": 0}, 1), ("claim", {"limit": 101}, 1),
    ("claim", {"limit": True}, 1), ("claim", {"limit": 1.0}, 1),
    ("claim", {"limit": 1}, True), ("getTask", {"task_id": -1}, 1),
    ("confirmClaim", {"task_ids": [1, 1]}, 1),
    ("confirmClaim", {"task_ids": [True]}, 1),
    ("confirmClaim", {"task_ids": list(range(1, 102))}, 1),
    ("submit", {"task_id": 1, "result_status": "failed", "error_message": ""}, 1),
    ("delete", {}, 1),
])
def test_bad_requests_fail_closed(action, body, now):
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.request_envelope(action, body, now)


def test_claim_candidates_and_exact_task_source_filter():
    row = {key: IDENTITY[key] for key in ("task_id", "cloud_id", "project_id", "image_url")}
    row.update(width=1920, height=1080, file_size=524288)
    assert c.parse_claim(envelope([row])) == [row]
    assert c.parse_claim(envelope([])) == []
    # Claim does not expose model_source. getTask must prove source 2 before confirm.
    task = c.parse_task(envelope(readback()), IDENTITY)
    assert task["model_source"] == 2 and task["status"] == 4
    assert task["tags"] == A_FIELDS["tags"]
    assert "unpublished_field" not in c.parse_claim(envelope([{**row, "unpublished_field": "private"}]))[0]


def test_public_task_identity_keeps_only_verified_five_fields():
    assert c.task_identity({**IDENTITY, "unexpected": "ignored"}) == IDENTITY
    for bad in ({**IDENTITY, "model_source": 1},
                {**IDENTITY, "task_id": True},
                {**IDENTITY, "image_url": ""}):
        with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
            c.task_identity(bad)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mutation", [
    lambda row: row.update(task_id=True), lambda row: row.update(cloud_id=-1),
    lambda row: row.update(width=False), lambda row: row.update(height=0),
    lambda row: row.update(file_size=float("nan")),
    lambda row: row.update(image_url="file:///etc/passwd"),
    lambda row: row.pop("project_id"),
])
def test_malformed_claim_rows_are_rejected_without_echo(mutation):
    row = {key: IDENTITY[key] for key in ("task_id", "cloud_id", "project_id", "image_url")}
    row.update(width=1920, height=1080, file_size=524288)
    mutation(row)
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError) as exc:
        c.parse_claim(envelope([row]))
    assert "img.example.com" not in str(exc.value)


def test_duplicate_claim_and_bad_envelopes_are_rejected():
    row = {key: IDENTITY[key] for key in ("task_id", "cloud_id", "project_id", "image_url")}
    row.update(width=1920, height=1080, file_size=524288)
    for response in (envelope([row, row]), {"code": True, "data": [row]},
                     {"code": 1, "data": [row]}, envelope({"wrong": []})):
        with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
            c.parse_claim(response)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field,value", [
    ("task_id", 999), ("cloud_id", 999), ("project_id", 999),
    ("image_url", "https://img.example.com/other.jpg"), ("model_source", 1),
    ("status", 6), ("status", True), ("score", float("inf")), ("tags", [1]),
])
def test_gettask_mismatch_other_source_and_bad_types_rejected(field, value):
    row = readback()
    row[field] = value
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.parse_task(envelope(row), IDENTITY)


def test_gettask_missing_field_and_unknown_identity_rejected():
    row = readback()
    row.pop("trait")
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.parse_task(envelope(row), IDENTITY)
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.parse_task(envelope(readback()), {**IDENTITY, "model_source": 1})


def test_confirm_exact_partition_distinguishes_new_and_already_claimed():
    data = {"confirmed_task_ids": [1], "already_claimed_task_ids": [2],
            "rejected_task_ids": [3]}
    assert c.parse_confirm(envelope(data), [1, 2, 3]) == data
    for wrong in (
        {**data, "confirmed_task_ids": [1, 2]},
        {**data, "rejected_task_ids": []},
        {**data, "already_claimed_task_ids": [2, 2]},
        {**data, "rejected_task_ids": [3, 4]},
        {**data, "rejected_task_ids": [True]},
    ):
        with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
            c.parse_confirm(envelope(wrong), [1, 2, 3])


def test_submit_preserves_three_tags_and_explicit_clears():
    out = output()
    out["a_fields"].update(title="", tags=[], reason="", cons="")
    out.update(final_score=0, final_level="L5")
    body = c.build_submit_body(out, level_mapping=MAPPING,
                               human_override={"override_source": "human_correction"})
    assert body["title"] == "" and body["score"] == 0
    # 空 tags 不再原样提交（工厂会挂到超时），改为分类词 + 风格词兜底；其余显式清空照旧保留
    assert body["tags"] == c.fallback_tags(out["a_fields"]) and body["tags"]
    assert body["reason"] == "" and body["grade"] == 11
    assert body["override_source"] == "human_correction"
    assert "actor" not in body and "override_reason" not in body


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mutate", [
    lambda out: out.update(final_score=True),
    lambda out: out.update(final_score=float("nan")),
    lambda out: out.update(final_score=-1),
    lambda out: out.update(final_level="L6"),
    lambda out: out["inspiration_task"].update(model_source=1),
    lambda out: out["a_fields"].pop("trait"),
    lambda out: out["a_fields"].update(tags=["ok", 3]),
    lambda out: out["a_fields"].update(title=False),
])
def test_submit_bad_frozen_output_rejected(mutate):
    out = output()
    mutate(out)
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.build_submit_body(out, level_mapping=MAPPING)


def test_mapping_must_cover_all_five_levels_without_guessing():
    for mapping in (None, {"L1": MAPPING["L1"]},
                    {**MAPPING, "L5": {"grade": True, "review_result": "reject"}},
                    {**MAPPING, "L5": {"grade": 11, "review_result": 1}}):
        with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
            c.build_submit_body(output(), level_mapping=mapping)
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.build_submit_body(output(), level_mapping=MAPPING, human_override={"actor": "someone"})


def test_submit_receipt_requires_accepted_and_terminal_status():
    assert c.parse_submit(envelope({"task_id": 10001, "status": 4, "accepted": True}), 10001) == {
        "task_id": 10001, "status": 4, "accepted": True}
    assert c.parse_submit(envelope({"task_id": 10001, "status": 5, "accepted": True}), 10001)["status"] == 5
    for data in ({"task_id": 10001, "status": 4, "accepted": False},
                 {"task_id": 10001, "status": 1, "accepted": True},
                 {"task_id": 10002, "status": 4, "accepted": True},
                 {"task_id": 10001, "status": True, "accepted": True},
                 {"task_id": 10001, "status": 4, "accepted": 1}):
        with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
            c.parse_submit(envelope(data), 10001)


def test_readback_checks_every_business_field_and_requires_explicit_review_code():
    body = c.build_submit_body(output(), level_mapping=MAPPING)
    parsed = c.parse_task(envelope(readback(body)), IDENTITY)
    assert c.matches_readback(parsed, body, expected_review_result=1)
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.matches_readback(parsed, body)
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.matches_readback(parsed, body, expected_review_result=True)
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.matches_readback({key: value for key, value in parsed.items() if key != "cloud_id"},
                           body, expected_review_result=1)
    for field in (*c.SUBMIT_FIELDS, "review_result", "result_status", "status", "error_message"):
        changed = deepcopy(parsed)
        changed[field] = ("drift" if field not in {"score", "grade", "review_result", "result_status", "status", "tags"}
                          else ["drift"] if field == "tags" else 2)
        assert not c.matches_readback(changed, body, expected_review_result=1), field
    cleared = c.build_submit_body({**output(), "a_fields": {**A_FIELDS, "title": "", "tags": []},
                                   "final_score": 0}, level_mapping=MAPPING)
    assert c.matches_readback(c.parse_task(envelope(readback(cleared)), IDENTITY),
                              cleared, expected_review_result=1)


def test_readback_ignores_surrounding_whitespace_the_factory_strips():
    """09-30 任务 16851：seo_title 带前导空格，工厂存的是去空格后的值，逐字比对把已送达判成 already_sent。"""
    body = c.build_submit_body({**output(), "a_fields": {**A_FIELDS, "seo_title": " 瀑布版画 ", "tags": [" 森林", "水流 "]}},
                               level_mapping=MAPPING)
    assert body["seo_title"] == " 瀑布版画 "
    stored = readback(body)
    stored["seo_title"] = "瀑布版画"
    stored["tags"] = ["森林", "水流"]
    assert c.matches_readback(c.parse_task(envelope(stored), IDENTITY), body, expected_review_result=1)
    stored["seo_title"] = "瀑布 版画"
    assert not c.matches_readback(c.parse_task(envelope(stored), IDENTITY), body, expected_review_result=1)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", ["TPWL-8905-anli", "p1", 30001])
def test_project_id_accepts_string_and_int(value):
    row = readback()
    row["project_id"] = value
    identity = dict(IDENTITY, project_id=value)
    assert c.task_identity(identity)["project_id"] == value
    assert c.parse_task(envelope(row), identity)["project_id"] == value


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", ["", " x", "a:b", "x" * 121, 0, -1, True, None, 1.5, ["a"]])
def test_project_id_rejects_bad_values(value):
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.task_identity(dict(IDENTITY, project_id=value))


def test_gettask_unsubmitted_task_reads_grade_zero():
    row = readback()
    row.update({"status": 0, "result_status": 0, "review_result": 0, "error_message": "", "score": 0, "grade": 0,
                "tags": [], **{k: "" for k in c.TEXT_FIELDS}})
    parsed = c.parse_task(envelope(row), IDENTITY)
    assert parsed["status"] == 0 and parsed["grade"] == 0 and parsed["score"] == 0
    row["grade"] = -1
    with pytest.raises(c.InspirationFactoryContractError):
        c.parse_task(envelope(row), IDENTITY)


def test_empty_tags_fall_back_to_class_and_style_words():
    """09-29 正式域名实测：submit 收到空 tags 会挂到超时，只有空 tags 的 3 条全部死信。冻结 a_fields 不动，提交体兜底。"""
    fields = {**A_FIELDS, "tags": [], "class_text": "景观设计-公园步道", "style": "写实摄影"}
    body = c.build_submit_body({**output(), "a_fields": fields}, level_mapping=MAPPING)
    assert body["tags"] == ["景观设计", "公园步道", "写实摄影"]
    assert c.matches_readback(c.parse_task(envelope(readback(body)), IDENTITY), body, expected_review_result=1)
    placeholder = {**fields, "class_text": "其它-其它", "style": ""}
    assert c.build_submit_body({**output(), "a_fields": placeholder}, level_mapping=MAPPING)["tags"] == [A_FIELDS["title"]]
    assert c.build_submit_body({**output(), "a_fields": {**placeholder, "title": "  "}}, level_mapping=MAPPING)["tags"] == ["灵感图"]
    assert c.build_submit_body({**output(), "a_fields": {**fields, "tags": ["原有"]}}, level_mapping=MAPPING)["tags"] == ["原有"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_factory_delivery.py =====
"""No-network tests for one-send inspiration delivery and real getTask readback."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.connector_delivery import DeliveryBatch, DeliveryItem, DeliveryKey
from app.factory_test_claim import INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL as PRODUCTION_URL, INSPIRATION_TEST_URL as TEST_URL
from app.inspiration_factory_contract import SUBMIT_FIELDS
from app.inspiration_factory_delivery import (
    INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY, INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
    InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter, resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter,
)


IDENTITY = {"task_id": 101, "cloud_id": 201, "project_id": 301,
            "image_url": "https://img.example.com/a.jpg", "model_source": 2}
FIELDS = {"title": "客厅", "seo_title": "现代客厅", "class_text": "室内-客厅",
          "style": "现代", "tags": ["客厅", "现代", "空间"], "cons": "", "design": "层次清晰",
          "reason": "", "trait": "实景照片", "image_defects": ""}
CONFIG = {"schema_version": "inspiration-factory-result-v1",
          "level_mapping": {level: {"grade": index, "review_result": "pass"}
                            for index, level in enumerate(("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"), 1)},
          "review_result_values": {"pass": 1}}


def payload(*, score=86, human=False, trigger=None):
    output = {"inspiration_task": deepcopy(IDENTITY), "a_fields": deepcopy(FIELDS),
              "final_score": score, "final_level": "L1"}
    if human:
        output["human_override"] = {"schema_version": "incremental-level-override-v1"}
    data = {"result": {"output": output}}
    if trigger:
        data["change"] = {"trigger_kind": trigger}
    return data


def key(revision=1, review_state="auto", payload_hash="a" * 64):
    return DeliveryKey("inspiration:test:task:101", 44, payload_hash, revision, review_state)


def batch(k=None, data=None):
    return DeliveryBatch("inspiration-result", 1, (DeliveryItem(1, k or key(), data or payload()),))


class FakeFactory:
    def __init__(self):
        self.source = 2
        self.task = {**IDENTITY, "status": 1, "result_status": None, "review_result": None,
                     "error_message": None,
                     **{field: None for field in SUBMIT_FIELDS}}
        self.actions = []
        self.bodies = []
        self.submit_mode = "success"
        self.drift = False
        self.refusal = "只有已提交、已完成或已失败的任务允许人工纠偏"

    def send(self, url, envelope):
        assert url == TEST_URL
        action = envelope["action"]
        self.actions.append(action)
        if action == "getTask":
            return json.dumps({"code": 200, "data": {**self.task, "model_source": self.source}}).encode()
        assert action == "submit"
        body = envelope["body"]
        self.bodies.append(deepcopy(body))
        if self.submit_mode == "refuse" or ("override_source" in body and self.task["status"] in (0, 1)):
            # 正式工厂 10-01 实测（任务 171061）：只有已提交、已完成或已失败的任务允许人工纠偏；
            # 业务拒绝返回 code 0 加一句说明，不写任何内容。
            return json.dumps({"code": 0, "msg": self.refusal, "data": []}).encode()
        if self.submit_mode in {"success", "timeout_after_write", "status5"}:
            self.task.update({field: deepcopy(body[field]) for field in SUBMIT_FIELDS})
            self.task.update(status=4, result_status=1, review_result=1)
            if self.drift:
                self.task["title"] = "drift"
        if self.submit_mode.startswith("timeout"):
            raise TimeoutError("simulated")
        return json.dumps({"code": 200, "data": {"task_id": 101,
                                                   "status": 5 if self.submit_mode == "status5" else 4,
                                                   "accepted": True}}).encode()


@pytest.fixture
def private_path(tmp_path):
    parent = tmp_path / "private"
    parent.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    return parent / "send.sqlite"


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def explicitly_enable_for_offline_tests(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED", "1")


def adapter(path: Path, fake: FakeFactory):
    return InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
        TEST_URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
        review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"],
        journal_path=str(path), send=fake.send, clock=lambda: 1727000000,
    )


def test_success_needs_real_readback_and_fresh_readback(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    result = worker.deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert result.succeeded and fake.actions == ["getTask", "submit", "getTask"]
    assert worker.read_back((key(),))[0].key == key()
    assert fake.actions[-1] == "getTask"
    fake.task["design"] = "drift"
    assert worker.read_back((key(),)) == ()


def manual_failure_payload():
    data = payload(trigger="human_correction")
    provenance = {"actor": "ops", "reason": "人工失败结论", "correction_event_id": "manual-failure:test"}
    data["change"].update(provenance)
    data["result"].update(status="manual_resolution", manual_resolution=provenance)
    data["result"]["output"].update(
        schema_version="inspiration-factory-result-v1", prompt_a_status="manual", prompt_b_status="manual",
        normalized_result={"source": "manual_failure_resolution", "level": "L1", "score": 86},
        human_override={"schema_version": "manual-failure-resolution-v1", "field": "final_level",
                        "previous_level": None, "level": "L1", **provenance})
    return data


def test_first_reviewed_manual_failure_can_submit_once(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    data = manual_failure_payload()
    sent = batch(key(review_state="reviewed"), data)
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(sent).results[0].succeeded
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(sent).results[0].succeeded
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [0, 2, 3, 4, 5])
def test_first_manual_failure_still_requires_claimed_status(private_path, status):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    fake.task["status"] = status
    result = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch(key(review_state="reviewed"), manual_failure_payload())).results[0]
    assert not result.succeeded and result.error_code == "inspiration_factory_status_invalid"
    assert "submit" not in fake.actions


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mutation", ["ordinary", "no_marker", "bad_trigger", "wrong_event", "auto", "revision2"])
def test_manual_first_exception_does_not_relax_other_review_guards(private_path, mutation):
    data = manual_failure_payload()
    k = key(review_state="reviewed")
    if mutation == "ordinary":
        data = payload(human=True, trigger="human_correction")
    elif mutation == "no_marker":
        data["result"].pop("manual_resolution")
    elif mutation == "bad_trigger":
        data["change"]["trigger_kind"] = "auto_run"
    elif mutation == "wrong_event":
        data["change"]["correction_event_id"] = "another"
    elif mutation == "auto":
        k = key()
    elif mutation == "revision2":
        k = key(2, "reviewed")
    fake = FakeFactory()
    assert not adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch(k, data)).results[0].succeeded
    assert "submit" not in fake.actions

    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 2
    assert fake.task["title"] == "人工标题"

def test_unconfirmed_first_manual_keeps_its_plain_form_until_confirmed(private_path):
    # 首次落地的形态记在投递日志里：提交后暂未落定，下一次只读确认用同一份提交体，不改带标记重发。
    fake = FakeFactory()
    original = fake.send
    pending = {}

    def lagging(url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] == "submit":
            fake.actions.append("submit")
            fake.bodies.append(deepcopy(envelope["body"]))
            pending.update({field: deepcopy(envelope["body"][field]) for field in SUBMIT_FIELDS})
            raise TimeoutError("response lost while the factory was still writing")
        if pending and fake.actions.count("getTask") >= 2:
            fake.task.update(pending, status=4, result_status=1, review_result=1)
        return original(url, envelope)

    fake.send = lagging
    sent = batch(key(review_state="reviewed"), manual_failure_payload())
    first = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(sent).results[0]
    assert first.error_code == "inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed" and first.retryable
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(sent).results[0].succeeded
    assert len(fake.bodies) == 1 and "override_source" not in fake.bodies[0]


def test_factory_refusal_is_definitive_and_a_corrected_conclusion_can_land_first(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    fake.submit_mode = "refuse"
    fake.refusal = "风格不能为空\n" + "x" * 300
    first = batch(key(review_state="reviewed"), manual_failure_payload())
    result = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(first).results[0]
    assert (result.error_code, result.retryable) == ("inspiration_factory_submit_rejected", False)
    assert result.diagnostic.startswith("风格不能为空x") and len(result.diagnostic) == 201 and result.diagnostic.endswith("…")
    assert fake.task["status"] == 1 and fake.task["title"] is None
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(first).results[0].error_code == "inspiration_factory_rejected"
    assert len(fake.bodies) == 1
    fake.submit_mode = "success"
    corrected = manual_failure_payload()
    corrected["result"]["output"]["a_fields"]["title"] = "订正后的标题"
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch(key(2, "reviewed", "b" * 64), corrected)).results[0].succeeded
    assert "override_source" not in fake.bodies[-1] and fake.task["title"] == "订正后的标题"
    assert len(fake.bodies) == 2

def test_landing_form_chosen_before_the_lock_is_rechecked_under_it(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    k = key(review_state="reviewed")
    task = dict(fake.task)
    # 加锁前选了带标记（覆盖）的形态，但日志里其实没有任何意向：不发。
    assert worker._reserve(k, "f" * 64, {"task_id": 101}, task, False, False,
                           manual_failure=True, plain=False) == "journal_intent_changed"
    assert worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    # 加锁前选了首次落地（不带标记）的形态，但已有确认过的自动结果：也不发。
    assert worker._reserve(key(2, "reviewed", "b" * 64), "e" * 64, {"task_id": 101}, dict(fake.task), False, False,
                           manual_failure=True, plain=True) == "journal_intent_changed"
    assert len(fake.bodies) == 1


def test_default_disabled_and_runtime_pause_use_zero_network(private_path, monkeypatch):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    monkeypatch.delenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED", raising=False)
    result = worker.deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not result.succeeded and result.error_code == "inspiration_factory_disabled"
    assert worker.read_back((key(),)) == () and fake.actions == []
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED", "1")
    assert worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    before = list(fake.actions)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED", "0")
    assert not worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert worker.read_back((key(),)) == ()
    assert fake.actions == before


def test_test_api_switch_delivers_and_reads_only_test_endpoint(private_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED", "0")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED", "1")
    fake = FakeFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    assert worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert worker.read_back((key(),))[0].key == key()
    before = list(fake.actions)
    production = InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
        PRODUCTION_URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
        review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"],
        journal_path=str(private_path.with_name('production.sqlite')), send=fake.send,
    )
    result = production.deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not result.succeeded and result.error_code == 'inspiration_factory_disabled'
    assert production.read_back((key(),)) == () and fake.actions == before
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED", "0")
    assert not worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert worker.read_back((key(),)) == () and fake.actions == before
