def test_gettask_failure_after_intent_never_reposts(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    original = fake.send

    def failing_readback(url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] == "getTask" and "submit" in fake.actions:
            fake.actions.append("getTask")
            raise TimeoutError("readback timeout")
        return original(url, envelope)

    worker = InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
        TEST_URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
        review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"],
        journal_path=str(private_path), send=failing_readback, clock=lambda: 1727000000)
    assert not worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert not worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


def test_unknown_timeout_survives_new_adapter_and_never_reposts(private_path):
    # 工厂没收到（任务仍为 1）：每次都只读确认、按 30 秒退避重试，次数用完由投递进死信，绝不重发。
    fake = FakeFactory()
    fake.submit_mode = "timeout_no_write"
    first = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not first.succeeded and first.retryable
    assert first.error_code == "inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed"
    assert first.retry_after_seconds == 30
    second = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not second.succeeded and second.retryable
    assert second.error_code == "inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed"
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1
    assert fake.actions.count("getTask") >= 3


def test_factory_settling_late_is_confirmed_on_retry_without_resubmit(private_path):
    # 10-01 事件 78663：工厂已收下，但提交后马上读回时还没落定；下一次只读确认即送达。
    fake = FakeFactory()
    original = fake.send
    pending = {}

    def lagging(url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] == "submit":
            fake.actions.append("submit")
            pending.update({field: deepcopy(envelope["body"][field]) for field in SUBMIT_FIELDS})
            raise TimeoutError("response lost while the factory was still writing")
        if pending and fake.actions.count("getTask") >= 2:
            fake.task.update(pending, status=4, result_status=1, review_result=1)
        return original(url, envelope)

    fake.send = lagging
    first = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not first.succeeded and first.retryable
    assert first.error_code == "inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed"
    second_worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    second = second_worker.deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert second.succeeded
    assert second_worker.read_back((key(),))[0].key == key()
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


def test_intent_committed_but_never_sent_stays_read_only(private_path):
    # 意图落账后、POST 前进程中断：之后只读等待工厂落定，不补发。
    fake = FakeFactory()

    class Crash(BaseException):
        pass

    def crashing(url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] == "submit":
            raise Crash
        return fake.send(url, envelope)

    worker = InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
        TEST_URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
        review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"],
        journal_path=str(private_path), send=crashing, clock=lambda: 1727000000)
    with pytest.raises(Crash):
        worker.deliver(batch())
    later = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not later.succeeded and later.retryable
    assert later.error_code == "inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed"
    assert "submit" not in fake.actions


def test_pending_intent_read_failure_keeps_confirmation_delay(private_path):
    # r30 评审 R1 原样反例：已有未确认意图时读回超时，也要隔 30 秒再确认，不按秒级退避耗尽次数。
    fake = FakeFactory()
    fake.submit_mode = "timeout_no_write"
    first = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert first.retryable and first.retry_after_seconds == 30
    original = fake.send

    def unavailable(url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] == "getTask":
            raise TimeoutError("offline simulated read failure")
        return original(url, envelope)

    fake.send = unavailable
    second = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1
    assert second.retryable and second.retry_after_seconds == 30


def test_first_send_read_jitter_keeps_default_backoff(private_path):
    # 没有任何提交意图时的读失败仍是发送前抖动，沿用默认退避，不额外等待 30 秒。
    fake = FakeFactory()

    def unavailable(url, envelope):
        fake.actions.append(envelope["action"])
        raise TimeoutError("factory jitter before any intent")

    fake.send = unavailable
    result = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert result.retryable and result.retry_after_seconds is None
    assert result.error_code == "inspiration_factory_gettask_unavailable"
    assert "submit" not in fake.actions


def test_human_revision_lag_waits_read_only_instead_of_terminal_mismatch(private_path):
    # r30 评审 S1：人工新修订提交前任务已是 4；响应丢失后读回仍见旧修订，不能判确定不一致。
    fake = FakeFactory()
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    original = fake.send
    held = {}

    def lagging(url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] == "submit":
            fake.actions.append("submit")
            held.update({field: deepcopy(envelope["body"][field]) for field in SUBMIT_FIELDS})
            held.update(score=envelope["body"]["score"])
            raise TimeoutError("response lost; factory still shows the old revision")
        return original(url, envelope)

    fake.send = lagging
    human_key = key(2, "reviewed", "b" * 64)
    lagged = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch(human_key, payload(score=90, human=True))).results[0]
    assert not lagged.succeeded and lagged.retryable
    assert lagged.error_code == "inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed" and lagged.retry_after_seconds == 30
    fake.task.update(held)
    confirmed = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch(human_key, payload(score=90, human=True))).results[0]
    assert confirmed.succeeded
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 2  # 两个修订各一次，绝不重发


def test_settled_factory_mismatch_is_terminal_not_unconfirmed(private_path):
    # 工厂已落定（status 4）却与本次提交不一致：确定的不一致，不再等待。
    fake = FakeFactory()
    fake.drift = True
    first = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not first.succeeded and not first.retryable
    assert first.error_code == "inspiration_factory_submit_unknown"
    second = adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not second.retryable and second.error_code == "inspiration_factory_already_sent"
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


def test_human_revision_guard_and_old_revision_rejection(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    assert worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    human_key = key(2, "reviewed", "b" * 64)
    assert worker.deliver(batch(human_key, payload(score=90, human=True))).results[0].succeeded
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 2 and fake.task["score"] == 90
    old = worker.deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not old.succeeded and "stale_revision" in old.error_code
    auto_key = key(3, "auto", "c" * 64)
    denied = worker.deliver(batch(auto_key, payload(score=80))).results[0]
    assert not denied.succeeded and "review_guard" in denied.error_code
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 2


def test_same_revision_payload_conflict_and_new_business_key_rejected(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    assert worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    conflict = worker.deliver(batch(key(payload_hash="b" * 64))).results[0]
    assert not conflict.succeeded and "identity_conflict" in conflict.error_code
    different = DeliveryKey("another-key", 44, "a" * 64)
    denied = worker.deliver(batch(different)).results[0]
    assert not denied.succeeded and "business_key_conflict" in denied.error_code
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


def test_unknown_previous_version_blocks_new_human_revision(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    fake.submit_mode = "timeout_no_write"
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    assert not worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    later = worker.deliver(batch(key(2, "reviewed", "b" * 64),
                                 payload(score=90, human=True))).results[0]
    assert not later.succeeded and "prior_unknown" in later.error_code
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


def target(mapping=CONFIG):
    return SimpleNamespace(direction="downstream", category_key="inspiration_image",
                           adapter_key=INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY,
                           schema_fingerprint=INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
                           field_mapping_json=json.dumps(mapping), logical_locator="inspiration-test")


def test_resolver_requires_exact_config_and_explicit_journal(private_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_INSPIRATION_TEST", TEST_URL)
    monkeypatch.delenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_SUBMIT_JOURNAL", raising=False)
    fake = FakeFactory()
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter(target(), send=fake.send)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_SUBMIT_JOURNAL", str(private_path))
    assert resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter(target(), send=fake.send).verify_target().least_privileged
    for bad in ({**CONFIG, "review_result_values": {}},
                {**CONFIG, "level_mapping": {"L1": CONFIG["level_mapping"]["L1"]}},
                {**CONFIG, "schema_version": "other"}):
        with pytest.raises(Exception):
            resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter(target(bad), send=fake.send)
    ambiguous = deepcopy(CONFIG)
    ambiguous["level_mapping"]["L5"]["review_result"] = "reject"
    ambiguous["review_result_values"] = {"pass": 1, "reject": 1}
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter(target(ambiguous), send=fake.send)
    assert fake.actions == []


def test_journal_endpoint_permissions_and_symlink_rejected(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    adapter(private_path, fake)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
            PRODUCTION_URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
            review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"],
            journal_path=str(private_path), send=fake.send)
    private_path.parent.chmod(0o755)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        adapter(private_path, fake)
    private_path.parent.chmod(0o700)
    link = private_path.parent / "link.sqlite"
    link.symlink_to(private_path)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        adapter(link, fake)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        adapter(private_path.parent / ".." / "private" / "other.sqlite", fake)
    assert fake.actions == []


def test_missing_or_replaced_journal_cannot_reset_send_intent(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    private_path.unlink()
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        worker.verify_target()
    private_path.write_bytes(b"")
    private_path.chmod(0o600)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        adapter(private_path, fake)
    assert fake.actions == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", [
    TimeoutError("pre-send timeout"),
    __import__("urllib.error").error.HTTPError(TEST_URL, 502, "bad gateway", {}, None),
])
def test_pre_send_gettask_outage_is_retryable_and_never_submits(private_path, failure):
    # 复审 09-26：发送前 getTask 读取的网络超时/5xx 被标 retryable=False 直接死信；
    # 此时尚未写发送意图，应可安全重试。
    fake = FakeFactory()
    original = fake.send
    state = {"fail": True}

    def flaky(url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] == "getTask" and state["fail"]:
            fake.actions.append("getTask")
            raise failure
        return original(url, envelope)

    worker = InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
        TEST_URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
        review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"],
        journal_path=str(private_path), send=flaky, clock=lambda: 1727000000)
    result = worker.deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not result.succeeded and result.retryable
    assert result.error_code == "inspiration_factory_gettask_unavailable"
    assert "submit" not in fake.actions
    state["fail"] = False
    assert worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


def test_pre_send_gettask_client_error_stays_terminal(private_path):
    from urllib.error import HTTPError

    fake = FakeFactory()

    def forbidden(url, envelope):
        raise HTTPError(TEST_URL, 403, "forbidden", {}, None)

    worker = InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
        TEST_URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
        review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"],
        journal_path=str(private_path), send=forbidden, clock=lambda: 1727000000)
    result = worker.deliver(batch()).results[0]
    assert not result.succeeded and not result.retryable

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_factory_failure.py =====
"""A technical failure is a terminal notification, never a fabricated score."""
from copy import deepcopy
import hashlib
import json

import pytest

from app import inspiration_factory_contract as contract
from test_inspiration_factory_delivery import (
    FakeFactory, IDENTITY, adapter, batch, key, private_path,
    explicitly_enable_for_offline_tests,
)


def failure_output():
    return {"schema_version": "inspiration-factory-failure-v1",
            "inspiration_task": deepcopy(IDENTITY),
            "failure": {"terminal_status": "failed", "run_key": "run_terminal_fixture",
                        "snapshot_hash": "a" * 64}}


class FailedFactory(FakeFactory):
    def send(self, url, envelope):
        if envelope["action"] != "submit":
            return super().send(url, envelope)
        self.actions.append("submit")
        body = envelope["body"]
        assert set(body) == {"task_id", "result_status", "error_message"}
        assert body["result_status"] == "failed"
        if self.submit_mode != "timeout_no_write":
            self.task.update(status=5, result_status=None,
                             error_message=body["error_message"])
            if self.drift:
                self.task["error_message"] = "different failure"
        if self.submit_mode.startswith("timeout"):
            raise TimeoutError("simulated")
        return json.dumps({"code": 200, "data": {
            "task_id": self.task["task_id"], "status": 5, "accepted": True}}).encode()


def failure_batch(k=None):
    return batch(k, {"result": {"status": "failed", "output": failure_output()}})


def test_failure_body_contains_only_documented_fields_without_raw_error():
    output = failure_output()
    body = contract.build_failure_submit_body(output)
    assert set(body) == {"task_id", "result_status", "error_message"}
    assert body["task_id"] == IDENTITY["task_id"] and body["result_status"] == "failed"
    assert len(body["error_message"]) < 120
    assert contract.request_envelope("submit", body, 1727000000)["body"] == body
    for mutated in ({**output, "a_fields": {"final_score": 0}},
                    {**output, "failure": {**output["failure"], "terminal_status": "canceled"}},
                    {**output, "failure": {**output["failure"], "snapshot_hash": "invalid"}},
                    {**output, "failure": {**output["failure"], "error_message": "private"}}):
        with pytest.raises(contract.InspirationFactoryContractError):
            contract.build_failure_submit_body(mutated)


def test_failure_notifies_once_and_requires_fresh_status5_and_exact_error(private_path):
    fake = FailedFactory()
    worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    assert worker.deliver(failure_batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert fake.actions == ["getTask", "submit", "getTask"]
    assert worker.read_back((key(),))[0].key == key()
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(failure_batch()).results[0].succeeded
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1
    fake.task["error_message"] = "other failure"
    assert worker.read_back((key(),)) == ()
    fake.task.update(error_message=contract.build_failure_submit_body(failure_output())["error_message"], status=4)
    assert worker.read_back((key(),)) == ()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode,passed", [("timeout_after_write", True), ("timeout_no_write", False)])
def test_failure_unknown_outcome_reconciles_without_repost(private_path, mode, passed):
    fake = FailedFactory(); fake.submit_mode = mode
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(failure_batch()).results[0].succeeded is passed
    assert adapter(private_path, fake).deliver(failure_batch()).results[0].succeeded is passed
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


def test_failure_cannot_overwrite_success_or_human_result(private_path):
    fake = FakeFactory(); worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    assert worker.deliver(batch()).results[0].succeeded
    result = worker.deliver(failure_batch(key(2, "reviewed", "b" * 64))).results[0]
    assert not result.succeeded and not result.retryable
    assert fake.actions.count("submit") == 1 and fake.task["status"] == 4


def test_failure_disabled_does_not_contact_factory(private_path, monkeypatch):
    fake = FailedFactory(); worker = adapter(private_path, fake)
    monkeypatch.delenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED")
    result = worker.deliver(failure_batch()).results[0]
    assert not result.succeeded and result.error_code == "inspiration_factory_disabled"
    assert fake.actions == []

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_factory_integration.py =====
"""Real SQLite material/frozen-input path; only external bytes are replaced."""
import io
import json
import sqlite3
from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.database import Base
from app.models import Asset, ContentRecord, ContentIngressEvent, EvaluationCategoryProfile
from app.incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
from app import inspiration_factory_intake as intake, incremental_materials
from app.inspiration_factory_contract import TEXT_FIELDS
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions

URL='https://factory-api-test.3d66.com/api/factory'
IDENTITY=dict(task_id=901,cloud_id=201,project_id=301,image_url='https://images.example.test/a.png',model_source=2)


@pytest.fixture
def env(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    tmp_path.chmod(0o700)
    engine=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'platform.sqlite'))
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        seed_incremental_versions(db,category_key='inspiration_image',count=1)
        profile=db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key=='inspiration_image'))
        profile.allowed_mime_types_json='["image/png"]'
        db.commit()
    image=io.BytesIO(); Image.new('RGB',(8,8),'red').save(image,format='PNG')
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_download',lambda *_:image.getvalue())
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_storage_dir',lambda:tmp_path/'images')
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON',json.dumps({'inspiration-factory-material':{'allowed_hosts':['images.example.test']}}))
    monkeypatch.setenv(intake.ENABLED_ENV,'1')
    calls=[]
    def send(url, body):
        assert url==URL and body['service']=='LgtInternalLLM'
        action=body['action']; calls.append(action)
        if action=='claim':
            data=[{**{k:v for k,v in IDENTITY.items() if k!='model_source'},'width':8,'height':8,'file_size':100}]
        elif action=='getTask':
            data={**dict.fromkeys(TEXT_FIELDS),**IDENTITY,'status':0,'result_status':None,
                  'review_result':None,'error_message':None,'tags':None,'score':None,'grade':None}
        else:
            assert action=='confirmClaim' and body['body']=={'task_ids':[901]}
            data=dict(confirmed_task_ids=[901],already_claimed_task_ids=[],rejected_task_ids=[])
        return json.dumps(dict(code=200,data=data)).encode()
    kwargs=dict(sessions=sessions,controls=lambda:dict(poll=True,confirm=True,material=True),
                upstream=URL,daily_limit_micros=1000,per_task_micros=10,send=send)
    yield tmp_path/'intake.sqlite',kwargs,calls
    engine.dispose()


def test_default_closed_has_zero_network_database_and_files(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv(intake.ENABLED_ENV,raising=False)
    def forbidden(*a,**kw):pytest.fail('disabled intake must have no side effects')
    path=tmp_path/'missing'/'intake.sqlite'
    result=intake.stream_cycle(path,sessions=forbidden,controls=forbidden,upstream=URL,
        daily_limit_micros=1,per_task_micros=1,send=forbidden)
    assert result['network_calls']==0 and not path.exists()


def test_owned_task_survives_restart_and_freezes_exact_source(env):
    path,kwargs,calls=env
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==1
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==0
    assert calls.count('confirmClaim')==1
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==1
        incremental_materials.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='test',now=datetime.now(timezone.utc));db.commit()
        job=db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob))
        assert job.status=='completed',job.error_code
        event=db.get(ContentIngressEvent,job.event_id)
        from app.incremental_input import build_frozen_content_ingress_input,validate_frozen_content_ingress_input,frozen_input_hash,IncrementalInputError
        frozen=build_frozen_content_ingress_input(db,event=event,record=db.get(ContentRecord,event.content_record_id))
        assert frozen['inspiration_task']==IDENTITY and frozen['source_system']=='inspiration-factory-material'
        changed=deepcopy(frozen);changed['inspiration_task']['project_id']=302
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):
            validate_frozen_content_ingress_input(db,event=event,input_manifest=changed,
                input_manifest_hash=frozen_input_hash(changed),package_category_key='inspiration_image',package_item_asset_id=db.scalar(select(Asset.id)))


def test_test_api_switch_can_confirm_and_enqueue_with_production_disabled(env, monkeypatch):
    path, kwargs, calls = env
    monkeypatch.setenv(intake.ENABLED_ENV, '0')
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED', '1')
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)['material_enqueued'] == 1
    assert calls.count('confirmClaim') == 1
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED', '0')
    before = list(calls)
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)['network_calls'] == 0
    assert calls == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize('crash',[False,True])
def test_unknown_confirmation_never_repeats_or_enqueues(env,crash):
    path,kwargs,calls=env; original=kwargs['send']
    def send(url,body):
        if body['action']=='confirmClaim':
            calls.append('confirmClaim')
            if crash:raise KeyboardInterrupt()
            raise TimeoutError()
        return original(url,body)
    kwargs['send']=send
    if crash:
        with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    else:
        assert intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)['error']=='inspiration_confirmation_unknown'
    intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    assert calls.count('confirmClaim')==1
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==0


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind',['foreign','identity_changed','already_claimed'])
def test_foreign_or_unowned_task_cannot_enter_pipeline(env,kind):
    path,kwargs,calls=env; original=kwargs['send']
    def send(url,body):
        response=json.loads(original(url,body))
        if body['action']=='getTask':
            if kind=='foreign':response['data']['model_source']=1
            if kind=='identity_changed':response['data']['project_id']=999
        if body['action']=='confirmClaim' and kind=='already_claimed':
            response['data']={'confirmed_task_ids':[],'already_claimed_task_ids':[901],'rejected_task_ids':[]}
        return json.dumps(response).encode()
    kwargs['send']=send
    if kind=='identity_changed':
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    else:intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    if kind!='already_claimed':assert 'confirmClaim' not in calls
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==0


def test_pause_after_image_blocks_confirmation(env,monkeypatch):
    path,kwargs,calls=env
    download=incremental_materials._download
    def pausing(*args):
        monkeypatch.setenv(intake.ENABLED_ENV,'0')
        return download(*args)
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_download',pausing)
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)['stop']=='platform_paused'
    assert 'confirmClaim' not in calls


def test_journal_cannot_change_endpoint_or_lose_reservation(env):
    path,kwargs,_=env
    intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='endpoint_changed'):
        intake.stream_cycle(path,**{**kwargs,'upstream':'https://factory-api.3d66.com/api/factory'})
    from pathlib import Path
    Path(str(path)+'.allowance.sqlite').unlink()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='allowance_missing'):intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)


def test_existing_job_replay_needs_genuine_confirm_receipt(env):
    path,kwargs,_=env
    intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='confirmed'")
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==1
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='confirmed',confirmation=NULL")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='ownership_receipt'):intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', ['source', 'status', 'oversized_claim'])
def test_post_download_drift_and_oversized_claim_do_not_confirm(env, change):
    path,kwargs,calls=env; original=kwargs['send']; gets=0
    def send(url,body):
        nonlocal gets
        response=json.loads(original(url,body))
        if body['action']=='getTask':
            gets+=1
            if gets==2:
                response['data']['model_source' if change=='source' else 'status']=1
        if body['action']=='claim' and change=='oversized_claim':
            response['data']=[dict(response['data'][0],task_id=901+i) for i in range(11)]
        return json.dumps(response).encode()
    kwargs['send']=send
    if change=='status':intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    else:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):intake.stream_cycle(path,**kwargs)
    assert 'confirmClaim' not in calls
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==0


def test_longest_contract_identity_still_becomes_a_material_job(env, monkeypatch):
    """评审：合同允许 project_id 120 字符，拼上前缀和 19 位 cloud_id 超过物料的 160 上限。
    身份要压成定长摘要后照常建物料任务，而不是领了任务再在提交物料时抛错。"""
    path, kwargs, calls = env
    long_identity = dict(IDENTITY, cloud_id=2**63 - 1, project_id='P' * 120)
    monkeypatch.setitem(globals(), 'IDENTITY', long_identity)
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)['material_enqueued'] == 1
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:
        job = db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob))
        content_id = json.loads(job.manifest_json)['content_id']
        assert len(content_id) <= 160 and ':sha256-' in content_id and 'P' * 120 not in content_id
        assert json.loads(job.manifest_json)['inspiration_task']['project_id'] == 'P' * 120


def test_a_rejected_material_does_not_stall_the_intake(env, monkeypatch):
    """已确认领取的任务提交物料被确定性拒绝时：单独标记、释放预算占用，本轮与下一轮照常跑。"""
    path, kwargs, calls = env
    def reject(*_a, **_k):
        raise incremental_materials.MaterialError('material_manifest_too_large')
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials, 'submit_manifest', reject)
    result = intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)
    assert result['material_enqueued'] == 0 and result['material_rejected'] == 1
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute("SELECT state FROM tasks WHERE task_id=901").fetchone()[0] == 'material_rejected'
    allowance = intake.DailyAllowance(path.parent / (path.name + '.allowance.sqlite'), 1000)
    assert allowance.outstanding() == []
    claims = calls.count('claim')
    assert intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)['material_rejected'] == 0
    assert calls.count('claim') > claims

def test_metadata_projection_preserves_authoritative_scoring_and_source():
    context=fixture(); manifest=context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    before=deepcopy(context.input_manifest)
    expected=reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest,context.input_manifest['upstream'],model_execution_mode='real')
    output=execute_native_node(context)['output']
    assert output['normalized_result']==expected
    assert (output['final_score'],output['final_level'])==(expected['score'],expected['level'])
    assert output['final_score']!=99
    assert output['a_fields']['tags']==['客厅','木饰面']
    assert output['a_fields']['class_text']=='视觉设计-商业广告海报'
    assert output['a_fields']['design']=='浅色墙面与木饰面形成对比。'

    assert output['external_io_performed'] is False and context.input_manifest==before


@pytest.mark.parametrize('case',['pending_branch','missing_metadata','bad_identity','missing_b','nonterminal'])
def test_incomplete_or_mismatched_projection_never_produces_publish_payload(case):
    context=fixture(); outputs=context.input_manifest['upstream']
    if case=='pending_branch':outputs['track_routing']['status']='running'
    if case=='missing_metadata':outputs['prompt_a']['output'].pop('design')
    if case=='bad_identity':context.run_snapshot['runtime_context']['input_manifest']['inspiration_task']['task_id']=902
    if case=='missing_b':outputs['prompt_b']['output']={}
    if case=='nonterminal':context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['edges'].append({'from':'publish','to':'prompt_a'})
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):execute_native_node(context)


def _matcher_manifest(content):
    return {'nodes':[{'kind':'rule_module','config':{'module_type_key':'score_level_matcher',
                                                     'component_snapshot':{'content_json':canonical_json(content)}}}]}


def test_short_circuit_level_floor_reads_frozen_matcher():
    from app.inspiration_factory_projection import short_circuit_level_floor
    table=[{'level':'L1','min_score':83},{'level':'L3','min_score':67},{'level':'L5','min_score':0}]
    manifest=_matcher_manifest({'legacy_score_thresholds':table,'legacy_level_thresholds':table,
                                'levels':[{**row,'enabled':True} for row in table]})
    assert short_circuit_level_floor(manifest,'L3')==67
    assert short_circuit_level_floor(manifest,'L5')==0
    assert short_circuit_level_floor(manifest,'L2') is None
    assert short_circuit_level_floor({'nodes':[]},'L3') is None


def test_short_circuit_level_floor_refuses_disagreeing_tables():
    """评审 T1：两套表同一等级下限分不同，不静默取第一套（旧表 67 / 新表 60 要拒绝）。"""
    from app.inspiration_factory_projection import short_circuit_level_floor
    manifest=_matcher_manifest({'legacy_level_thresholds':[{'level':'L3','min_score':67},{'level':'L5','min_score':0}],
                                'levels':[{'level':'L3','min_score':60,'enabled':True},{'level':'L5','min_score':0,'enabled':True}]})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        short_circuit_level_floor(manifest,'L3')
    assert short_circuit_level_floor(manifest,'L5')==0


def test_class_text_from_categories_rules():
    from app.inspiration_production_fields import class_text_from_categories
    assert class_text_from_categories({'primary_category':'居住空间','secondary_category':'客厅'})=='居住空间-客厅'
    assert class_text_from_categories({'primary_category':'','secondary_category':'客厅'})=='其它-其它'
    assert class_text_from_categories({'primary_category':'居住空间','secondary_category':''})=='居住空间-其它'
    assert class_text_from_categories({'primary_category':'','secondary_category':''})=='其它-其它'
    assert class_text_from_categories({'primary_category':'其它','secondary_category':'其它'})=='其它-其它'
    assert class_text_from_categories({})=='其它-其它'
    # 评审 T2：顶层没有分类时读 production_fields；顶层有就以顶层为准，不和 production_fields 混拼
    nested={'production_fields':{'primary_category':'居住空间','secondary_category':'卧室'}}
    assert class_text_from_categories(nested)=='居住空间-卧室'
    assert class_text_from_categories({**nested,'primary_category':'商业空间'})=='商业空间-其它'
    assert class_text_from_categories({'production_fields':{'primary_category':'','secondary_category':''}})=='其它-其它'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_frozen_semantic.py =====
"""Actual run418/asset681 must be caught before B, using frozen exemption rules."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from app.inspiration_semantic_consistency import inspiration_semantic_conflicts
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE

FIXTURE=json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name('fixtures_inspiration_681.json').read_text())

def fixture():
    node={'kind':'prompt','config':{'asset_key':'prompt_a'},'output_schema':deepcopy(FIXTURE['output_schema'])}
    manifest={'category_key':'inspiration_image','resource_policy':{'source_provenance':{
        'semantic_validation_profile':'inspiration-frozen-redline-v1'}},'nodes':[node,{
        'kind':'rule_module','config':{'module_type_key':'eligibility_redline',
        'component_snapshot':{'content_json':json.dumps(FIXTURE['redline_policy'])}}}]}

def check(payload,node,snapshot):
    return inspiration_semantic_conflicts('inspiration_call_a',payload,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        output_schema=node['output_schema'],run_snapshot=snapshot,node=node)


def test_real_negative_evidence_is_retryable_without_mutating_output():

    assert check(payload,node,snapshot)==('evidence_conflict:redline:multi_image_collage',)
    assert payload==original


def test_corrected_negative_or_frozen_positive_exemption_is_accepted():
    payload,node,snapshot=fixture()
    payload['decisive_evidence']['redline_triggered']=[]
    assert check(payload,node,snapshot)==()
    payload['redline_triggered']['multi_image_collage']=True
    payload['redline_exempted']['multi_image_collage']=True
    payload['decisive_evidence']['redline_triggered']=[{'key':'multi_image_collage','evidence':'同一作品三个角度拆解展示'}]
    assert check(payload,node,snapshot)==()

    snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['resource_policy']['source_provenance'].clear()
    assert check(payload,node,snapshot)==()

    payload,node,snapshot=fixture();snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'].pop()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):check(payload,node,snapshot)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_golden_workflow.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from scripts.inspiration_golden_workflow import _emit


def test_emit_serializes_datetimes_to_iso8601(tmp_path, capsys) -> None:
    output = tmp_path / "run.json"
    created_at = datetime(2026, 8, 7, 12, 34, 56, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    _emit({"run_id": 1, "created_at": created_at}, str(output))

    expected = "2026-08-07T12:34:56+00:00"
    assert json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)["created_at"] == expected
    assert json.loads(output.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["created_at"] == expected

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_metadata_bounds.py =====
"""Regression for 141888: constrain generation, never truncate accepted data."""
from copy import deepcopy
import unittest

from app.inspiration_metadata_bounds import bounded_contract, TEXT_LIMITS
from app.prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract, validate_output_payload


class MetadataBoundsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.contract = {
            "name": "inspiration_production_metadata", "mode": "json_schema", "strict": True,
            "schema": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False,
                "required": [*TEXT_LIMITS, "tags"],
                "properties": {**{k: {"type": "string", "minLength": 1} for k in TEXT_LIMITS},
                    "tags": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}}}}}
        self.valid = {"class_text": "游戏海报", "style": "哥特风格", "cons": "文字较多",
                      "design": "人物与服饰构成主体。", "tags": ["人物", "服饰"]}

    def test_long_unclosed_field_risk_is_bounded_without_rewriting_payload(self):
        old = deepcopy(self.contract)
        contract = parse_output_contract(bounded_contract(self.contract))
        self.assertEqual(old, self.contract)
        validate_output_payload(contract, self.valid)
        for key, limit in TEXT_LIMITS.items():
            with self.subTest(key=key):
                validate_output_payload(contract, {**self.valid, key: "字" * limit})
                bad = {**self.valid, key: "字" * (limit + 1)}
                saved = deepcopy(bad)
                with self.assertRaises(Exception) as caught:
                    validate_output_payload(contract, bad)
                self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "structured_output_schema_mismatch")
                self.assertEqual(bad, saved)

    def test_tags_empty_and_boundaries_and_missing_fields(self):
        contract = parse_output_contract(bounded_contract(self.contract))
        for tags in ([], ["字" * 32] * 12):
            validate_output_payload(contract, {**self.valid, "tags": tags})
        for bad in ({**self.valid, "tags": ["字"] * 13},
                    {**self.valid, "tags": ["字" * 33]},
                    {k: v for k, v in self.valid.items() if k != "cons"},
                    {**self.valid, "score": 100}):
            with self.assertRaises(Exception) as caught:
                validate_output_payload(contract, bad)
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "structured_output_schema_mismatch")

    def test_scope_idempotence_and_stricter_existing_limits(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            bounded_contract({**self.contract, "name": "inspiration_call_a"})
        self.contract["schema"]["properties"]["design"]["maxLength"] = 100
        bounded = bounded_contract(self.contract)
        self.assertEqual(bounded["schema"]["properties"]["design"]["maxLength"], 100)
        self.assertEqual(bounded_contract(bounded), bounded)

    def test_projection_accepts_legacy_and_exact_bounded_contract_only(self):
        from app.inspiration_metadata_generation import metadata_contract, resolve_metadata_node
        mapping = {"schema_version": "inspiration-factory-result-v1", "prompt_a_node_id": "a",
                   "prompt_b_node_id": "b", "production_fields_node_id": "c"}
        manifest = {"category_key": "inspiration_image", "nodes": [
            {"node_instance_id": "a", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"asset_key": "prompt_a"}},
            {"node_instance_id": "b", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"asset_key": "prompt_b"}},
            {"node_instance_id": "rule", "kind": "rule_module", "config": {}},
            {"node_instance_id": "c", "kind": "prompt", "config": {
                "asset_key": "production_metadata", "execution_role": "production_metadata",
                "component_snapshot": {"content": {"stage": "C"}},
                "output_contract": metadata_contract()}},
            {"node_instance_id": "out", "kind": "downstream_projection", "config": {"mapping": mapping}}],
            "edges": [{"from": a, "to": b} for a, b in zip(["a", "b", "rule", "c"], ["b", "rule", "c", "out"])]}
        config = manifest["nodes"][3]["config"]
        config.update(max_tokens=4096)
        config["component_snapshot"]["content"]["system_prompt"] = "Concise complete JSON."
        self.assertEqual(resolve_metadata_node(manifest, mapping, "out"), "c")
        config["output_contract"] = bounded_contract(metadata_contract())
        self.assertEqual(resolve_metadata_node(manifest, mapping, "out"), "c")
        exact = deepcopy(config["output_contract"])
        for mutation in ("oversize", "remove_limit", "extra_field", "remove_required"):
            with self.subTest(mutation=mutation):
                changed = deepcopy(exact)
                schema = changed["schema"]
                if mutation == "oversize":
                    schema["properties"]["design"]["maxLength"] = 321
                elif mutation == "remove_limit":
                    del schema["properties"]["tags"]["maxItems"]
                elif mutation == "extra_field":
                    schema["properties"]["score"] = {"type": "integer"}
                else:
                    schema["required"].remove("cons")
                config["output_contract"] = changed
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "inspiration_metadata_wiring_invalid"):
                    resolve_metadata_node(manifest, mapping, "out")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_metadata_feedback.py =====
"""141888: preserve defects; repair only settled invalid C text within existing budget."""
import hashlib,json
from copy import deepcopy
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine,select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app.database import Base
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
from app.evaluation_component_catalog import canonical_component_hash
from app.inspiration_metadata_generation import metadata_contract
from app.inspiration_metadata_bounds import bounded_contract,metadata_text_conflicts
from app.models import ProductionRun,ProductionStepAttempt,RuntimeDispatchItem,WorkflowModelInvocation
from test_workflow_fixture_executor import _fixture_module,_real_model_case,_convert_to_incremental_run


def metadata_payload(*,inconsistent):
    return {"class_text":"海报","style":"哥特","tags":["海报"],"design":"人物与装饰构成画面。",
            "cons":"将“lavished”误写为“lavished”" if inconsistent else "底部有水印遮挡文字。"}


def test_metadata_conflicts_are_exact_opt_in_and_do_not_supply_answers():
    from app.inspiration_semantic_consistency import inspiration_semantic_conflicts
    from app.workflow_retry_feedback import append_validation_feedback
    bad=metadata_payload(inconsistent=True);saved=deepcopy(bad)
    node={"config":{"execution_role":"production_metadata"}}
    snapshot={"workflow":{"manifest":{"category_key":"inspiration_image"}}}
    args=dict(contract_profile=None,run_snapshot=snapshot,node=node)
    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts("inspiration_production_metadata",bad,output_schema=metadata_contract()["schema"],**args)==()
    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts("inspiration_production_metadata",bad,output_schema=bounded_contract(metadata_contract())["schema"],**args)==("metadata_self_correction:cons",)
    assert bad==saved
    for text in ["左上与右下均有 'ABC' 标识。", "将“abc”误写为“ABC”", "将“lavished”误写为“lavish”", "未见明显不足"]:
        assert metadata_text_conflicts({**bad,"cons":text})==()
    for text in ["'lavished' 应为 'lavished'", "将“lavished”误写为“lavished”"]:
        assert metadata_text_conflicts({**bad,"cons":text})==("metadata_self_correction:cons",)
    assert metadata_text_conflicts({**bad,"cons":"请看相同的描述反复出现且没有新增信息。"*3})==("metadata_repeated_text:cons",)
    message=append_validation_feedback("input",{"issues":[{"consistency":"metadata_self_correction:cons"}]})
    assert "lavished" not in message and "未见明显不足" not in message
    assert "不得统一清空cons" in message
    assert "不引用或转写具体字词" in message
    assert "模糊" in message and "水印" in message

@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "second_inconsistent",
    (False, True),
)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("postcheck", (False, True))
def test_metadata_feedback_settles_once_and_stops_after_two_attempts(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    second_inconsistent: bool,
    postcheck: bool,
) -> None:
    """Worker retries one settled conflict and never creates attempt 3."""

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'semantic-worker-{int(second_inconsistent)}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    responses = [True, second_inconsistent]
    prompts=[]
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            contract = metadata_contract() if postcheck else bounded_contract(metadata_contract())
            node["config"]["output_contract"] = contract
            node["config"].update(asset_key="production_metadata",execution_role="production_metadata")
            content=node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content"]
            content.update(stage="C",system_prompt="Describe visible defects without scoring.",user_prompt="Describe the image.")
            node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"]=canonical_component_hash(content)
            node["output_schema"] = deepcopy(contract["schema"])
            node["retry_policy"] = {
                "kind": "fixed",
                "max_attempts": 2,
                "delay_seconds": 0,
            }
            attempt.script_version.max_attempts = 2
            attempt.script_version.retry_policy_json = canonical_json(
                node["retry_policy"]
            )
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            run_snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_budget"].update(
                {
                    "max_requests": 2,
                    "max_input_tokens": 30_000,
                    "max_output_tokens": 2_048,
                    "max_cost_micros": 100_000,
                }
            )
            manifest = run_snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]
            manifest["nodes"] = [node]
            manifest["category_key"]="inspiration_image"
            run_snapshot["runtime_context"]["category_key"]="inspiration_image"
            run.category_key="inspiration_image"
            manifest["resource_policy"] = {
                "source_provenance": {
                    "retry_feedback_profile": "validation-feedback-v1",
                }
            }
            if postcheck:
                manifest["resource_policy"]["source_provenance"]["metadata_validation_profile"] = "bounded-metadata-postcheck-v1"
            encoded_snapshot = canonical_json(run_snapshot)
            run.snapshot_json = encoded_snapshot
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                encoded_snapshot.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            run.status = "retryable"
            run.lease_owner = None
            run.lease_token = None
            run.lease_expires_at = None
            attempt.status = "retryable"
            attempt.lease_owner = None
            attempt.lease_token = None
            attempt.lease_expires_at = None
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            assert dispatch is not None
            dispatch.status = "queued"
            dispatch.lease_owner = None
            dispatch.lease_token = None
            dispatch.leased_at = None
            dispatch.lease_expires_at = None
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class SequentialClient:
            calls = 0

            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                prompts.append(options.get("user_prompt",_args[1] if len(_args)>1 else ""))
                inconsistent = responses[self.__class__.calls]
                self.__class__.calls += 1
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=metadata_payload(
                        inconsistent=inconsistent
                    ),
                    raw_text="{}",
                    raw_payload={
                        "id": f"req-semantic-{self.__class__.calls}"
                    },
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id=(
                        f"req-semantic-{self.__class__.calls}"
                    ),
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once(
            "worker-semantic",
            global_limit=1,
            model_client_type=SequentialClient,
        ) is True
        with sessions() as db:
            after_first = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert after_first is not None
            assert after_first.status == "retryable", (
                after_first.error_code,
                after_first.error_message,
            )
        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once(
            "worker-semantic",
            global_limit=1,
            model_client_type=SequentialClient,
        ) is True

        with sessions() as db:
            persisted_run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            invocations = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation)
                .where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)
            ).all()
            assert persisted_run is not None
            # This deliberately C-only fixture has no grading rule modules. A valid
            # second C response reaches that independent parent guard; it must
            # not be reported as a complete grading run.
            assert persisted_run.status == "failed", (
                persisted_run.error_code,
                persisted_run.error_message,
            )
            assert SequentialClient.calls == 2
            assert "上次发布说明的校验反馈" not in prompts[0]
            assert "上次发布说明的校验反馈" in prompts[1]
            assert "不得统一清空cons" in prompts[1]
            assert "lavished" not in prompts[1]
            assert [row.attempt_no for row in attempts] == [1, 2]
            assert attempts[0].status == "failed"
            assert json.loads(attempts[0].checkpoint_json)["status"] == (
                "failed_post_settlement"
            )
            assert len(invocations) == 2
            assert all(row.status == "succeeded" for row in invocations)
            assert all(row.input_tokens == 80 for row in invocations)
            assert all(row.output_tokens == 20 for row in invocations)
            assert all(row.actual_cost_micros > 0 for row in invocations)
            first_evidence = json.loads(invocations[0].evidence_json)["result"]
            assert first_evidence["semantic_validation"] == {
                "status": "rejected",
                "reasons": [
                    "metadata_self_correction:cons",
                ],
            }
            if second_inconsistent:
                assert attempts[1].status == "failed"
                assert attempts[1].last_error_code == (
                    "model_output_semantic_inconsistency"
                )
                assert json.loads(invocations[1].evidence_json)["result"][
                    "semantic_validation"
                ]["status"] == "rejected"
            else:
                assert persisted_run.error_code == "native_authoritative_component_missing"
                assert attempts[1].status == "succeeded"
                assert json.loads(attempts[1].output_manifest_json)["output"]["cons"] == "底部有水印遮挡文字。"
                assert "semantic_validation" not in json.loads(invocations[1].evidence_json)["result"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_soft_contract_postcheck_preserves_legacy_and_refuses_length_before_projection():
    from app.inspiration_semantic_consistency import inspiration_semantic_conflicts
    from app.inspiration_metadata_bounds import POSTCHECK_PROFILE
    from app.inspiration_metadata_generation import merge_metadata
    manifest={"category_key":"inspiration_image","resource_policy":{"source_provenance":{"metadata_validation_profile":POSTCHECK_PROFILE}}}
    good=metadata_payload(inconsistent=False)
    bad={**good,"cons":"".join(chr(0x4e00+i) for i in range(241)),"tags":["tag"]*13}
    args=dict(contract_profile=None,run_snapshot={"workflow":{"manifest":manifest}},node={"config":{"execution_role":"production_metadata"}},output_schema=metadata_contract()["schema"])
    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts("inspiration_production_metadata",good,**args)==()
    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts("inspiration_production_metadata",bad,**args)==("metadata_length:cons","metadata_length:tags")
    assert merge_metadata({},bad,output_contract=metadata_contract())==bad
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="inspiration_metadata_length"):
        merge_metadata({},bad,output_contract=metadata_contract(),postcheck=True)
    assert merge_metadata({},good,output_contract=metadata_contract(),postcheck=True)==good


def test_soft_metadata_truncation_feedback_does_not_enable_other_nodes(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from datetime import datetime,timezone
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.workflow_retry_feedback import validation_retry_feedback,append_validation_feedback
    engine=create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'metadata-truncated.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine);sessions=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run,first,node,_,_=_case(db,tmp_path,monkeypatch,strict_output=True)
            first.step_key='production_metadata'
            data={c.name:getattr(first,c.name) for c in ProductionStepAttempt.__table__.columns if c.name!='id'}
            data.update(attempt_no=2,idempotency_key=first.idempotency_key+':second')
            second=ProductionStepAttempt(**data);db.add(second);db.flush()
            source=WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id,step_attempt_id=first.id,node_instance_id='production_metadata',invocation_key='root',status='failed',error_code='model_provider_json_truncated',provider='local_qwen',model_id='fixture',upstream_status_code=200,reserved_output_tokens=4096,output_tokens=4096,finished_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),evidence_json=canonical_json({'failure':{'usage_status':'provider_reported','finish_reason':'length','provider_usage':{'output_tokens':4096,'raw_text':'PRIVATE_PROVIDER_BODY'}}}))
            db.add(source);db.flush();saved=source.evidence_json
            manifest={'category_key':'inspiration_image','resource_policy':{'source_provenance':{'retry_feedback_profile':'validation-feedback-v1','metadata_validation_profile':'bounded-metadata-postcheck-v1'}}}
            args=dict(run=run,attempt=second,manifest=manifest,node_id='production_metadata',invocation_key='root',schema=metadata_contract()['schema'])
            result=validation_retry_feedback(db,**args)
            assert result['profile']=='validation-feedback-v3'
            assert result['issues'][0]['truncation_feedback']['output_tokens']==4096
            assert result['issues'][1]['metadata_text_bounds']['cons']==240
            message=append_validation_feedback('original',result)
            assert 'PRIVATE_PROVIDER_BODY' not in message and '未见明显不足' not in message
            assert '不得统一清空cons' in message and '不引用或转写具体字词' in message
            assert validation_retry_feedback(db,**{**args,'node_id':'prompt_a'}) is None
            manifest['resource_policy']['source_provenance'].pop('metadata_validation_profile')
            assert validation_retry_feedback(db,**args) is None
            assert source.evidence_json==saved
    finally:engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_metadata_freeze.py =====
"""Register and freeze the opt-in third prompt through normal platform APIs."""
import json
from copy import deepcopy

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.evaluation_components import (
    create_component_asset, generate_component_version, save_component_working_copy,
)
from app.inspiration_metadata_generation import (
    EXECUTION_ROLE, GUIDANCE, PROMPT_STAGE, metadata_contract, production_fields_ready, resolve_metadata_node,
)
from app.models import CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, ModelConfig, ModelRegistryEntry, PromptVersion, WorkflowDefinition
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract, LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from app.workflow_working_copies import create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version
from test_evaluation_components import _profile
from test_inspiration_factory_projection import fixture
from test_workflow_v3 import _node
from test_workflow_working_copies import _seed_managed_dependencies


def test_full_component_registration_and_frozen_candidate_preserve_scoring(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'metadata-freeze.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            # Reuse a local model registry fixture, never a server model or DB.
            _, example = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            model = next(n['config']['model_registry_id'] for n in example['nodes'] if n['kind'] == 'prompt')
            registered = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, model)
            registered.protocol = 'openai_chat'
            db.get(ModelConfig, registered.source_model_config_id).protocol = 'openai_chat'
            _profile(db, 'inspiration_image')
            context = fixture()
            source = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
            nodes = [_node('upstream', 'upstream_connector', {'connector_key': 'logical.inspiration_image.input'})]

            def register_prompt(key, stage, contract, system):
                asset = create_component_asset(db, category_key='inspiration_image', asset_key=key,
                    kind='prompt', display_name=key, prompt_stage=stage, created_by='test')
                save_component_working_copy(db, asset_id=asset.id, expected_lock_version=1,
                    content=dict(stage=stage, system_prompt=system, user_prompt='仅描述目标图片。',
                                 rubric_version='metadata-freeze-test'), updated_by='test')
                version = generate_component_version(db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by='test')
                return _node(key, 'prompt', dict(asset_key=key, prompt_version_id=version.id,
                    model_registry_id=model, max_tokens=4096, thinking_level='none', thinking_enabled=False,
                    structured_output=True, output_contract=contract), output_schema=contract['schema'])

            for stage in ('A', 'B'):
                contract = strict_output_contract('inspiration_image', stage, evaluation_contract={},
                    contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE)
                nodes.append(register_prompt('prompt_' + stage.lower(), stage, contract, '原有评分提示词。'))
            for original in source['nodes']:
                if original['kind'] != 'rule_module':
                    continue
                config = original['config']
                key = config['module_type_key']
                asset = create_component_asset(db, category_key='inspiration_image', asset_key=key,
                    kind='rule_module', display_name=key, module_type_key=key, created_by='test')
                save_component_working_copy(db, asset_id=asset.id, expected_lock_version=1,
                    content=json.loads(config['component_snapshot']['content_json']), updated_by='test')
                version = generate_component_version(db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by='test')
                nodes.append(_node(key, 'rule_module', dict(asset_key=key, module_type_key=key,
                    rule_module_version_id=version.id, input_bindings={'model_result': 'prompt_b.result'}, scope='default')))
            projection = _node('publish', 'downstream_projection', deepcopy(context.node['config']))
            nodes.append(projection)
            manifest = dict(schema_version='workflow-v3', category_key='inspiration_image', lane_key='evaluation',
                run_modes=['stock_regression'], nodes=nodes,
                edges=[{'from': a['node_instance_id'], 'to': b['node_instance_id']} for a, b in zip(nodes, nodes[1:])],
                queue_class='validation', input_schema={'type': 'object'}, output_schema={'type': 'object'},
                resource_policy={'max_parallel': 1})
            definition = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key='metadata-freeze', name='local metadata fixture',
                description='', owner='platform', allowed_categories_json='["inspiration_image"]',
                status='active', created_by='test')
            db.add(definition)
            db.flush()
            old = create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(db, definition=definition, version='original',
                manifest=manifest, created_by='test')
            frozen_before = persisted_workflow_manifest(old)
            old_hash = old.canonical_hash

            metadata = register_prompt(EXECUTION_ROLE, PROMPT_STAGE, metadata_contract(), GUIDANCE)
            metadata['config']['execution_role'] = EXECUTION_ROLE
            candidate = deepcopy(frozen_before)
            candidate['nodes'].insert(-1, metadata)
            mapping = candidate['nodes'][-1]['config']['mapping']
            mapping['production_fields_node_id'] = EXECUTION_ROLE
            for edge in candidate['edges']:
                if edge['to'] == 'publish':
                    edge['to'] = EXECUTION_ROLE
            candidate['edges'].append({'from': EXECUTION_ROLE, 'to': 'publish'})
            new = create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(db, definition=definition, version='with-metadata',
                manifest=candidate, created_by='test')
            db.commit()
            db.expire_all()
            frozen = persisted_workflow_manifest(new)
            assert new.canonical_hash != old_hash
            assert production_fields_ready(frozen)
            assert resolve_metadata_node(frozen, mapping, 'publish') == EXECUTION_ROLE
            assert persisted_workflow_manifest(old) == frozen_before and old.canonical_hash == old_hash
            before_nodes = {n['node_instance_id']: n for n in frozen_before['nodes']}
            after_nodes = {n['node_instance_id']: n for n in frozen['nodes']}
            for key in before_nodes.keys() - {'publish'}:
                assert after_nodes[key] == before_nodes[key]
            c = after_nodes[EXECUTION_ROLE]['config']
            # SQLite ignores VARCHAR length; production MySQL does not. Keep the
            # new stage inside the existing column contract without another DDL.
            assert len(c['component_snapshot']['content']['stage']) <= PromptVersion.__table__.c.stage.type.length
            assert c['component_snapshot']['content']['stage'] == PROMPT_STAGE
            assert c['component_snapshot']['content']['system_prompt'] == GUIDANCE
            assert c['model_snapshot'] == after_nodes['prompt_a']['config']['model_snapshot']
            assert c['vision_policy_hash']
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)) == 0
            assert 'production_metadata' not in canonical_json(frozen_before)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_metadata_generation.py =====
"""Publishing descriptions cannot replace or modify frozen A/B scoring evidence."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json

import pytest

from app.inspiration_metadata_generation import (
    EXECUTION_ROLE, GUIDANCE, PROMPT_STAGE, metadata_contract, metadata_node_ids, merge_metadata, production_fields_ready,
)
from app.inspiration_production_fields import METADATA_FIELDS, extend_contract
from app.prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract, LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
from app.workflow_node_executors import execute_native_node, NodeExecutorError
from app.workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result, _prompt_stage_payloads, _legacy_summary
from app.workflow_short_circuit import downstream_skipped_nodes
from test_inspiration_factory_projection import fixture
from test_inspiration_production_fields import fields


def test_bounded_projection_refuses_repetition_without_rewriting_legacy_evidence():
    from app.inspiration_metadata_bounds import bounded_contract
    context = generation_fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    node = manifest['nodes'][0]
    entry = context.input_manifest['upstream']['publish_metadata']
    entry['output']['cons'] = "正确应为 'lavished' 或 'lavished' 的误用，" * 4
    before = deepcopy(context.input_manifest)
    node['config']['output_contract'] = bounded_contract(metadata_contract())
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as caught:
        execute_native_node(context)
    assert caught.value.code == 'inspiration_metadata_repeated_text'
    assert context.input_manifest == before
    # The opt-in guard must not reinterpret an old frozen contract.
    node['config']['output_contract'] = metadata_contract()
    execute_native_node(context)
    entry['output']['cons'] = '画面元素较多，部分装饰与标题重叠。'
    node['config']['output_contract'] = bounded_contract(metadata_contract())
    execute_native_node(context)


def generation_fixture():
    context = fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    outputs = context.input_manifest['upstream']
    for key in METADATA_FIELDS:
        outputs['prompt_a']['output'].pop(key)
    a = manifest['nodes'][0]
    a['config']['output_contract'] = strict_output_contract('inspiration_image', 'A',
        evaluation_contract={}, contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE)
    metadata = dict(node_instance_id='publish_metadata', kind='prompt', config={
        'asset_key': EXECUTION_ROLE, 'execution_role': EXECUTION_ROLE,
        'component_snapshot': {'content': {'stage': PROMPT_STAGE}, 'version': 999},
        'output_contract': metadata_contract()})
    manifest['nodes'].insert(0, metadata)  # Node list order is not execution order.
    context.node['config']['mapping']['production_fields_node_id'] = 'publish_metadata'
    chain = [n['node_instance_id'] for n in manifest['nodes']
             if n['node_instance_id'] not in {'publish_metadata', 'publish'}]
    chain += ['publish_metadata', 'publish']
    manifest['edges'] = [{'from': a, 'to': b} for a, b in zip(chain, chain[1:])]
    outputs['publish_metadata'] = dict(status='succeeded', output={key: fields()[key] for key in METADATA_FIELDS})
    for key, entry in outputs.items():
        entry['node_kind'] = next(n['kind'] for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == key)
    return context


def test_new_generation_joins_only_five_fields_without_changing_score_or_input():
    context = generation_fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    before = deepcopy((context.run_snapshot, context.input_manifest))
    original = fixture()
    expected = reduce_native_workflow_result(original.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest'],
        original.input_manifest['upstream'], model_execution_mode='real')
    output = execute_native_node(context)['output']
    assert output['normalized_result'] == expected
    assert output['a_fields']['design'] == fields()['design']
    assert output['a_fields']['tags'] == fields()['tags']
    assert output['final_score'] != 99
    assert output['external_io_performed'] is False
    assert (context.run_snapshot, context.input_manifest) == before
    assert production_fields_ready(manifest)
    assert _legacy_summary(manifest)['prompt_a_version'] != '999'
    assert _legacy_summary(manifest)['prompt_b_version'] != '999'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', [
    'missing', 'running', 'failed', 'skipped', 'missing_field', 'blank', 'wrong_type',
    'extra_score', 'extra_grade', 'wrong_role', 'wrong_stage', 'wrong_a', 'alias',
    'no_contract', 'loose_contract', 'early', 'feedback', 'not_terminal', 'duplicate_node', 'scoring_rule',
])
def test_invalid_generation_cannot_produce_a_publish_payload(change):
    context = generation_fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    metadata = manifest['nodes'][0]
    entry = context.input_manifest['upstream']['publish_metadata']
    if change == 'missing':
        del context.input_manifest['upstream']['publish_metadata']
    elif change in {'running', 'failed', 'skipped'}:
        entry['status'] = change
    elif change == 'missing_field':
        entry['output'].pop('design')
    elif change == 'blank':
        entry['output']['design'] = ' '
    elif change == 'wrong_type':
        entry['output']['tags'] = '客厅'
    elif change in {'extra_score', 'extra_grade'}:
        entry['output'][change.removeprefix('extra_')] = 90
    elif change == 'wrong_role':
        metadata['config']['execution_role'] = 'grading'
    elif change == 'wrong_stage':
        metadata['config']['component_snapshot']['content']['stage'] = 'SINGLE'
    elif change == 'wrong_a':
        next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_a')['config']['asset_key'] = 'unrelated'
    elif change == 'alias':
        context.node['config']['mapping']['production_fields_node_id'] = 'prompt_b'
    elif change == 'no_contract':
        del metadata['config']['output_contract']
    elif change == 'loose_contract':
        metadata['config']['output_contract']['schema']['additionalProperties'] = True
    elif change == 'early':
        manifest['edges'] = [e for e in manifest['edges'] if e['to'] != 'publish_metadata']
        manifest['edges'].append({'from': 'prompt_a', 'to': 'publish_metadata'})
    elif change == 'feedback':
        manifest['edges'].append({'from': 'publish_metadata', 'to': 'prompt_b'})
    elif change == 'not_terminal':
        manifest['edges'].append({'from': 'publish', 'to': 'prompt_a'})
    elif change == 'duplicate_node':
        manifest['nodes'].append(deepcopy(metadata))
    elif change == 'scoring_rule':
        metadata['config']['short_circuit_rules'] = [{'target_level': 'L1'}]
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):
        execute_native_node(context)


def test_redline_short_circuit_still_generates_fields_and_does_not_treat_c_as_b():
    context = generation_fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    outputs = context.input_manifest['upstream']
    b = next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_b')
    b['config']['execution_role'] = 'grading'
    skipped = downstream_skipped_nodes(manifest, source_node_id='prompt_a', skip_roles=['grading'])
    assert skipped == ('prompt_b',)
    decision = dict(schema_version='short-circuit-decision-v1', rule_key='frozen-redline',
        rule_version='short-circuit-rule-v1', target_level='L4', skipped_nodes=list(skipped),
        evidence=dict(priority=90, skip_roles=['grading'], matched_rule_keys=['frozen-redline'], matched_conditions=[]))
    outputs['prompt_a']['short_circuit_decision'] = decision
    outputs['prompt_b'] = dict(status='skipped', node_kind='prompt',
        skipped_reason='short_circuited', short_circuit_decision=decision)
    output = execute_native_node(context)['output']
    assert output['final_level'] == 'L4' and output['normalized_result']['decision_source'] == 'frozen_short_circuit'
    assert output['a_fields']['design'] == fields()['design']
    precheck, aesthetic = _prompt_stage_payloads(outputs, manifest)
    assert precheck == outputs['prompt_a']['output'] and aesthetic is None


def test_short_circuit_floor_ambiguity_fails_the_projection_instead_of_guessing():
    """评审 T1：短路补分遇到两套阈值表不一致，投递节点明确失败，不把某一套的下限分交给工厂。"""
    context = generation_fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    outputs = context.input_manifest['upstream']
    b = next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_b')
    b['config']['execution_role'] = 'grading'
    skipped = downstream_skipped_nodes(manifest, source_node_id='prompt_a', skip_roles=['grading'])
    decision = dict(schema_version='short-circuit-decision-v1', rule_key='frozen-redline',
        rule_version='short-circuit-rule-v1', target_level='L4', skipped_nodes=list(skipped),
        evidence=dict(priority=90, skip_roles=['grading'], matched_rule_keys=['frozen-redline'], matched_conditions=[]))
    outputs['prompt_a']['short_circuit_decision'] = decision
    outputs['prompt_b'] = dict(status='skipped', node_kind='prompt',
        skipped_reason='short_circuited', short_circuit_decision=decision)
    matcher = next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if (n.get('config') or {}).get('module_type_key') == 'score_level_matcher')
    snapshot = matcher['config']['component_snapshot']
    content = json.loads(snapshot['content_json'])
    content['legacy_level_thresholds'] = [dict(row, min_score=row['min_score'] + 1) if row.get('level') == 'L4' else row
                                          for row in content.get('legacy_level_thresholds') or content['levels']
                                          if isinstance(row.get('min_score'), int)]
    snapshot['content_json'] = json.dumps(content)
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as error:
        execute_native_node(context)
    assert error.value.code == 'inspiration_short_circuit_floor_ambiguous'


def test_metadata_payload_cannot_become_the_dimension_fallback():
    context = generation_fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    outputs = context.input_manifest['upstream']
    baseline = reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, outputs, model_execution_mode='real')
    outputs['publish_metadata']['output'] = {'score': 100, 'level': 'L1', 'dimensions': {}}
    outputs['publish_metadata']['short_circuit_decision'] = dict(
        schema_version='short-circuit-decision-v1', rule_key='injected', rule_version='short-circuit-rule-v1',
        target_level='L1', skipped_nodes=[], evidence=dict(priority=999, skip_roles=[], matched_conditions=[]))
    assert reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, outputs, model_execution_mode='real') == baseline
    outputs['prompt_b'].pop('output')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, outputs, model_execution_mode='real')


def test_metadata_contract_admission_and_rendering_keep_legacy_and_opt_in_distinct():
    contract = metadata_contract()
    assert set(parse_output_contract(contract).schema['properties']) == set(METADATA_FIELDS)
    old = fixture()
    manifest = old.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    a = manifest['nodes'][0]
    a['config']['output_contract'] = extend_contract(strict_output_contract('inspiration_image', 'A',
        evaluation_contract={}, contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE))
    assert production_fields_ready(manifest)
    # An explicit broken C must not fall back to the old complete A contract.
    old.node['config']['mapping']['production_fields_node_id'] = 'missing'
    assert not production_fields_ready(manifest)
    context = generation_fixture()
    manifest = context.run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
    next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_a')['config']['output_contract']['schema']['required'].remove('title')
    assert not production_fields_ready(manifest)
    original = fields()
    generated = {key: fields()[key] for key in METADATA_FIELDS}
    generated['tags'] = []
    merged = merge_metadata(original, generated)
    merged['tags'].append('修改副本')
    assert generated['tags'] == [] and original == fields()

    monkeypatch.delenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED', raising=False)

        upstream_contract_id=1, downstream_contract_id=2, expected_workflow_hash='unused',
        category_key='inspiration_image') == dict(poll=False, confirm=False, material=False)
